# Harness integration inventory

Status: research inventory, September 2026. Hooks, MCP, headless modes,
session handling and automation gotchas for the coding-agent harnesses Bee
drivers must launch, observe and equip. Facts below are dated; harness
flags change monthly, so a driver must be tested against the pinned version
it declares.

## Why this document

A Bee driver has three jobs: launch a harness into a window, a headless
session or a batch run; observe what it does, from its own output stream and
from lifecycle hooks; and equip it with Bee's tools over MCP. Every harness
does these three things differently. This inventory records the differences
so drivers stay thin and the shared kit stays honest.

## The driver contract Bee already has

The legacy tree (`bee-legacy/_archive/poc/lineage/src/harness` and
`bee-legacy/driver-claude`) settled a shape that held for four harnesses:

| Method | Answers | Legacy signature |
|---|---|---|
| `prepare` | Which executor runs the child, with what environment, after what setup | `prepare(action, placement) -> {executor, env, setup?}` |
| `command` | The exact argv for a mode: `window` (PTY, brief as prompt), `session` (one headless resumed turn), `batch` (run to completion) | `command(mode, action, text, placement, settings, session, first) -> argv` |
| `parse` | One line of headless output as a normalized event | `parse(line) -> init, text(delta?), tool_call, tool_result, turn_end, error, raw` |

Binding metadata declares `kind`, `title`, `modes`, `default_mode` and
`hooks`; the catalog discovers drivers with `registry.find` on
`contract.binding` entries tagged `harness.driver`. The gateway exposes two
endpoints bound to the launched action: `/mcp/{action}` for tools and
`/hook/{action}` for lifecycle events, so hook observations and MCP calls land
on the same thread record chain.

Rules the carriers learned the hard way, all still true:

- The child's entire environment comes from `prepare`; nothing leaks from the runtime.
- Generated Bee configuration lives in the retained Agent session home. Default
  Claude, Codex and Agy windows can read an explicitly authorized host HOME;
  Agy receives the session home as an additional customization root. Grok keeps
  a private home and imports only the host-approved global `config.toml` as a
  private composition base. Placement inserts Bee MCP into the generated private
  `config.toml`; Bee hooks remain a separate private file.
- Sessions are one process per turn, resumed by id. A long-lived stdin stream parks the actor and can never receive thread messages.
- Window carriers hold typed input until the first hook arrives; keystrokes before the TUI is up are lost.
- Harnesses without admitted hooks are observed only through output and MCP.
- A hook handler must return a JSON object, never an empty array.

## Integration point taxonomy

Every harness is inventoried against the same twelve points:

1. Identity and install
2. Launch modes: TUI, headless, stdin, SDK or server
3. Input: prompt passing and follow-ups
4. Output: text, JSON, streaming schema, where the final answer is
5. Sessions: ids, resume, transcripts
6. Hooks: events, config, blocking semantics, payload casing
7. MCP: config, transports, auth, scope, tool filtering, server mode
8. Extensibility: commands, skills, subagents, instruction files
9. Permissions and sandbox, trust prompts
10. Environment, settings precedence, model flags
11. Observability and exit codes
12. Automation gotchas

## Harnesses

The popular set first, then Grok, Agy and Kiro because Bee already ran two of them and the third has the strictest hook and trust model. pi is included because it is the most scriptable of them and has no MCP at all. Aider was reviewed and dropped: no session ids, no JSON output, no hooks and no MCP, so it can only be wrapped as a batch tool, never driven as a peer.

### Claude Code (Anthropic, proprietary/closed-source signed native binary, v2.1.263, `curl -fsSL https://claude.ai/install.sh | bash`)

**Launch.** TUI: `claude [prompt]`; `/tui fullscreen` or `CLAUDE_CODE_NO_FLICKER=1` switches renderer. Headless: `-p, --print` (also implied when stdout is not a TTY), with `--max-turns N`, `--max-budget-usd`, `--no-session-persistence`, `--permission-prompts host|none`, `--fallback-model`, `--json-schema`; `--bare` is the recommended CI mode (skips hooks, LSP, plugin sync, auto-memory, keychain, CLAUDE.md discovery; sets `CLAUDE_CODE_SIMPLE=1`; auth strictly `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` or `apiKeyHelper` via `--settings`); `--safe-mode` (`CLAUDE_CODE_SAFE_MODE=1`), `--restricted` (drops Bash/PowerShell/REPL/WebFetch, ignores user/project/local settings), `--init-only`/`--init`/`--maintenance`. Background: `claude --bg [--exec '<cmd>']` + `claude attach|logs|stop|rm|respawn <id>`, `claude agents [--json]`. Stdin: `cat build.log | claude -p "explain"`, piped stdin capped at 10 MB; live channel with `--input-format stream-json`. SDK/server/ACP: Claude Agent SDK TS `@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk` (`query({prompt, options}) : Query extends AsyncGenerator<SDKMessage>` plus `interrupt()`, `initializationResult()`, `reinitialize()`, `getContextUsage()`, `setPermissionMode()`, `setModel()`, `reloadSkills()`) and Python `claude-agent-sdk` (`query(prompt=…, options=ClaudeAgentOptions(…))`, `ClaudeSDKClient` with `connect/query/receive_messages/receive_response/interrupt/set_permission_mode/set_model/disconnect`); MCP server via `claude mcp serve` (stdio); remote `--cloud`, `--environment ccpool_…`, `--teleport`, `--remote-control`. No ACP.

**Input and follow-up.** Prompt via argv, stdin pipe, or `--input-format stream-json` (one JSON object per stdin line: `{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"…"},"parent_tool_use_id":null}`; `content` may be a block array incl. `{"type":"image","source":{"type":"base64",…}}`; `session_id` optional on `SDKUserMessage`; `--replay-user-messages` echoes accepted input). Process-per-turn follow-up: `-c/--continue` (most recent conversation in cwd; skips `-p`/SDK/background sessions unless `-p` is also passed) or `-r/--resume <session-id|name>` (ids resolve from any directory, v2.1.223+). One-process multi-turn: `claude -p --input-format stream-json --output-format stream-json --verbose`. Files with `--file file_abc:doc.txt`; `/skill-name`, `/model sonnet`, `/effort`, `/config key=value`, `/mcp` work inside `-p` prompts.

**Output.** Flags: `--output-format text|json|stream-json`, `--input-format text|stream-json`, `--verbose` (required for `stream-json`), `--include-partial-messages`, `--include-hook-events`, `--forward-subagent-text`, `--replay-user-messages`, `--prompt-suggestions`, `--json-schema '<JSON Schema>'`. `json` = one `SDKResultMessage`: `type:"result"`, `subtype:"success"`, `uuid`, `session_id`, `duration_ms`, `duration_api_ms`, `is_error`, `api_error_status`, `num_turns`, `result`, `stop_reason`, `ttft_ms`, `total_cost_usd`, `usage`, `modelUsage`, `permission_denials[{tool_name,tool_use_id,tool_input}]`, `queued_turn_count`, `structured_output`, `deferred_tool_use`, `terminal_reason`, `origin`; error `subtype` ∈ `error_max_turns | error_during_execution | error_max_budget_usd | error_max_structured_output_retries` plus `errors: string[]`; `terminal_reason` ∈ `completed, max_turns, tool_deferred, aborted_streaming, aborted_tools, hook_stopped, stop_hook_prevented, background_requested, blocking_limit, rapid_refill_breaker, prompt_too_long, image_error, model_error, api_error, malformed_tool_use_exhausted, budget_exhausted, structured_output_retry_exhausted, tool_deferred_unavailable, turn_setup_failed`. `stream-json` NDJSON union: `system`/`init` (`session_id`, `model`, `tools[]`, `skills[]`, `slash_commands[]`, `agents[]`, `cwd`, `permissionMode`, `apiKeySource`, `claude_code_version`, `output_style`, `betas[]`, `effort`, `capabilities[]` e.g. `interrupt_receipt_v1`/`interrupt_cancel_queued_v1`, `mcp_servers[{name,status}]`, `mcp_server_errors[{name,type,message}]` with `type` ∈ `unknown_type|url_missing_type|invalid_config|reserved_name`, `plugins[{name,path}]`, `plugin_errors[{plugin,type,message}]`); `assistant` (`message`, `parent_tool_use_id`, `uuid`, `session_id`, `context_usage`, `aborted`); `user`/replay (`message`, `parent_tool_use_id`, `isSynthetic`, `tool_use_result`, `isReplay`); `stream_event` (`event`, `parent_tool_use_id` always null, `ttft_ms`); `result`; `system/compact_boundary` (`compact_metadata{trigger:"manual"|"auto", pre_tokens}`); `system/api_retry` (`attempt`, `max_retries`, `retry_delay_ms`, `error_status`, `no_response{waited_ms,retry_wait_ms}`, `error` ∈ `authentication_failed|oauth_org_not_allowed|billing_error|rate_limit|overloaded|invalid_request|model_not_found|server_error|max_output_tokens|unknown`); `system/plugin_install` (`status` ∈ `started|installed|failed|completed`, `name`, `error`); `system/permission_denied` (`tool_name`, `tool_use_id`, `agent_id`, `decision_reason_type`, `decision_reason`, `message`); `system/informational` (`content`, `level` ∈ `info|notice|suggestion|warning`, `prevent_continuation`); `hook_started`/`hook_progress`/`hook_response` (with `--include-hook-events`); plus `SDKTask*`, `SDKStatusMessage`, `SDKThinkingTokensMessage`, `SDKRateLimitEvent`, `SDKPromptSuggestionMessage`, `SDKMemoryRecallMessage`, `SDKWorkerShuttingDownMessage`, `SDKConversationResetMessage`. Control protocol (SDK/host): `{"type":"control_request","request_id":…,"request":{…}}` answered by `control_response` echoing `request_id`; used for `canUseTool`, interrupts (`cancel_queued: true` under `interrupt_cancel_queued_v1`), initialize (`SDKControlInitializeResponse{commands, agents, output_style, available_output_styles, models, account, hooks_applied}`). Final answer: `result.result`; `--json-schema` puts the object in `structured_output`.

**Sessions.** `--session-id <uuid>`, `-r/--resume [id|name|search]`, `-c/--continue`, `--fork-session`, `-n/--name`, `--from-pr`, `--no-session-persistence`, `--bg`. Transcript `~/.claude/projects/<project>/<session-id>.jsonl`, `<project>` = cwd path with every non-alphanumeric char replaced by `-` (`/home/wolfy-j/wippy/bee` → `-home-wolfy-j-wippy-bee`); names over 200 chars truncated with a hash appended; `CLAUDE_CODE_PROJECT_DIR_NAME` (with `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`) overrides. Format JSONL, one object per message/tool-use/metadata entry (`{"type":"mode",…}`, `{"type":"permission-mode",…}`, `{"type":"file-history-snapshot",…}`); docs declare it internal and version-unstable — use `/export`, `-p --output-format json`, hook `transcript_path`, or the SDK. Listing: `claude --resume` / `/resume` picker (`Ctrl+A` all projects, `Ctrl+W` all worktrees, `Ctrl+B` branch filter), `claude agents --json`, or enumerate `~/.claude/projects/*/*.jsonl`. Delete `claude rm <id>` / `claude project purge [path]`; retention `cleanupPeriodDays` (default 30). Not restored on resume: `--mcp-config`, `--settings`, `--plugin-dir`, `--fallback-model`, `--add-dir`.

**Hooks.** Events (33): `Setup`, `SessionStart`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `UserPromptExpansion`, `InstructionsLoaded`, `ConfigChange`, `PreToolUse`, `PermissionRequest`, `PermissionDenied`, `PreModelSwitch`, `PostModelSwitch`, `Elicitation`, `ElicitationResult`, `PostToolUse`, `PostToolUseFailure`, `PostToolBatch`, `MessageDisplay`, `Stop`, `StopFailure`, `SubagentStart`, `SubagentStop`, `TaskCreated`, `TaskCompleted`, `TeammateIdle`, `PreCompact`, `PostCompact`, `CwdChanged`, `DirectoryAdded`, `FileChanged`, `WorktreeCreate`, `WorktreeRemove`, `Notification`, `SessionEnd`. Config: `~/.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.local.json`, managed settings, plugin `hooks/hooks.json`, skill frontmatter (`hooks:`), subagent frontmatter; kill switches `"disableAllHooks": true`, `allowManagedHooksOnly`, `allowedHttpHookUrls`, `httpHookAllowedEnvVars`, `--bare`, `--safe-mode`. Shape `{"hooks":{"<Event>":[{"matcher":"Bash|Edit","hooks":[{…}]}]}}`; handler keys `type` (`command | http | mcp_tool | prompt | agent`), `if`, `timeout`, `statusMessage`, `once`, `command`/`args`/`shell`/`async`/`asyncRewake`, `url`/`headers`/`allowedEnvVars`, `server`/`tool`/`input` (templated `${tool_input.file_path}`), `prompt`/`model`. Payload casing: snake_case stdin (`session_id`, `prompt_id`, `transcript_path`, `cwd`, `permission_mode`, `effort.level`, `hook_event_name`, `agent_id`/`agent_type`; extras `tool_name`, `tool_input`, `tool_use_id`, `tool_response`, `reason`, `model`, `prompt`, `command`, `last_assistant_message`, `from_model`/`to_model`, `file_path`/`change_type`, `notification_type`, `tool_calls[]`), camelCase JSON stdout. Control: exit `0` — stdout starting `{` and ending `}` parsed as JSON, otherwise plain text injected as model-visible context only for `UserPromptSubmit`, `UserPromptExpansion`, `SessionStart`, `PostModelSwitch`; exit `2` blocks (tool call, prompt, expansion, stopping, going idle, task create/complete, config change, model switch, agentic loop) with stderr as reason and a JSON `allow` cannot override it, and on `WorktreeCreate` any non-zero exit aborts; other exit codes are non-blocking errors (action proceeds, `<hook> hook error` notice with first stderr line). JSON output `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":…,"permissionDecision":"allow|deny|block","permissionDecisionReason":…,"decision":"allow|deny|continue","stopReason":…,"suppressOutput":true,"additionalContext":"…","updatedInput":{…},"retry":true},"systemMessage":"…","terminalSequence":"…"}` — `permissionDecision` → `PreToolUse`/`PermissionDenied`; `decision` → `PermissionRequest`/`UserPromptSubmit`/`UserPromptExpansion`; `additionalContext` → `PreToolUse`/`PermissionRequest`/`UserPromptSubmit`; `updatedInput` → `PreToolUse`/`UserPromptSubmit`/`UserPromptExpansion`; `stopReason` → `Stop`; `suppressOutput` → `MessageDisplay`; `terminalSequence` → all. Timeouts in **seconds**: `command`/`http`/`mcp_tool` 600 s default (30 s on `UserPromptSubmit` and model-switch events, 10 s on `MessageDisplay`), `prompt` 30 s, `agent` 60 s, `SessionEnd` shares a 1.5 s budget raisable to 60 s with explicit `timeout` (`CLAUDE_CODE_SESSIONEND_HOOKS_TIMEOUT_MS`); `async: true` detaches with no timeout, `asyncRewake: true` wakes Claude on exit 2. Timeout/other-exit behavior is fail-open (action proceeds); only exit 2 fails closed. Matchers: tool-name regex on `PreToolUse`/`PostToolUse`/`PostToolUseFailure`/`PermissionRequest`/`PermissionDenied`; `startup|resume|clear|compact|fork` on `SessionStart`; `init|maintenance` on `Setup`; `clear|resume|logout|prompt_input_exit|other` on `SessionEnd`; notification type; agent type on `SubagentStart`/`SubagentStop`; `manual|auto` on `PreCompact`/`PostCompact`; canonical model name on model-switch; `user_settings|project_settings|local_settings|policy_settings|skills` on `ConfigChange`; `slash_command|register_repo_root` on `DirectoryAdded`; literal filenames on `FileChanged`; error type on `StopFailure`; load reason on `InstructionsLoaded`; MCP server name on `Elicitation*`. No matcher: `UserPromptSubmit`, `PostToolBatch`, `Stop`, `TeammateIdle`, `TaskCreated`, `TaskCompleted`, `WorktreeCreate`, `WorktreeRemove`, `MessageDisplay`, `CwdChanged`. Trust gating of hooks: none — settings-file hooks run before the trust dialog, including under `-p` in an untrusted folder.

**MCP.** CLI `claude mcp add [--transport http|sse|stdio|ws] [--scope local|project|user] [-e KEY=val] [-H "K: V"] [--client-id] [--client-secret] [--callback-port] <name> (<url> | -- <command> [args…])`, `claude mcp add-json <name> '<json>'`, `list`, `get`, `remove`, `login <name> [--no-browser]`, `logout`, `reset-project-choices`, `add-from-claude-desktop`, `serve`. Storage: `local` (default) in `~/.claude.json` under the project entry; `project` in `.mcp.json` at repo root; `user` at `~/.claude.json` top level (MCP "local scope" is unrelated to `.claude/settings.local.json`). Flags `--mcp-config <files-or-json…>`, `--strict-mcp-config`; under `-p` Claude waits for pending servers up to `MCP_TIMEOUT` (30 s) before the first turn. `.mcp.json`: `mcpServers.<name>` with `type` (`http` | `streamable-http` | `sse` | `stdio` | `ws`), `url`, `headers`, `headersHelper`, `oauth{clientId,callbackPort,authServerMetadataUrl,scopes}`, `command`, `args`, `env`, `timeout`, `alwaysLoad`, `_meta{"anthropic/maxResultSizeChars","anthropic/requiresUserInteraction"}`; `${VAR}` / `${VAR:-default}` expansion in `command`, `args`, `env`, `url`, `headers`, `headersHelper`; placeholders `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}`, `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}`, `${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}`. Transports: stdio, streamable HTTP, SSE, WebSocket. Auth: OAuth (`claude mcp login`, `/mcp` flow, `oauth` block, `MCP_OAUTH_CALLBACK_PORT`, `MCP_CLIENT_SECRET`). Gating: `enableAllProjectMcpServers`, `enabledMcpjsonServers` (names or `["*"]`), `disabledMcpjsonServers`, `allowedMcpServers`, `deniedMcpServers`, `allowManagedMcpServersOnly`, `allowAllClaudeAiMcps`, `disableClaudeAiConnectors`, per-project `projects["/path"].disabledMcpServers`; `-p`/SDK sessions never prompt for `.mcp.json` servers, they load. Tool filtering via permission rules `mcp__server`, `mcp__server__*`, `mcp__server__tool` (settings files silently skip any `mcp__` rule containing parentheses; use `--disallowedTools` for parameterized MCP rules). Naming `mcp__<server>__<tool>`; plugin servers `mcp__plugin_<plugin>_<server>__<tool>`, id `plugin:<plugin>:<server>`; resources `@<server>:protocol://path`; prompts `/server:prompt (MCP)` or `/mcp__server__prompt`. Acts as MCP server: yes — `claude mcp serve` (stdio), client config `{"mcpServers":{"claude-code":{"type":"stdio","command":"/path/to/claude","args":["mcp","serve"]}}}`; exposed tool list unknown. Unreachable server: reported in `system/init` as `mcp_servers[{name,status}]` and `mcp_server_errors[…]`; session continues.

**Extensibility.** Commands: built-ins at `/docs/en/commands`; custom commands are now skills, legacy flat `.claude/commands/<name>.md` → `/name` still works. Skills: `~/.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md`, `.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md`, nested `<subdir>/.claude/skills/…` (→ `/apps/web:deploy`), `<plugin>/skills/<name>/SKILL.md`, and `.claude/skills/` inside `--add-dir` paths; precedence Enterprise > Personal > Project; frontmatter `name`, `description`, `when_to_use`, `argument-hint`, `arguments`, `disable-model-invocation`, `user-invocable`, `allowed-tools`, `disallowed-tools`, `model`, `effort`, `context: fork`, `agent`, `background`, `hooks`, `paths`, `shell`, `metadata`, `license`, `compatibility`; substitutions `$ARGUMENTS`, `$ARGUMENTS[N]`, `$N`, `$name`, `${CLAUDE_SKILL_DIR}`, `${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}`, `${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID}`, `${CLAUDE_EFFORT}`, `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}`; inline shell `` !`cmd` `` or a ```` ```! ```` block (2-min timeout, gated by `disableSkillShellExecution`); visibility `skillOverrides` (`on|name-only|user-invocable-only|off`), `disableBundledSkills`. Subagents: `.claude/agents/*.md`, `~/.claude/agents/*.md`, plugin `agents/` (`my-plugin:agent`), managed settings, `--agents '<json>'` / `--agent <name>`; frontmatter `name`*, `description`*, `tools`, `disallowedTools`, `model` (`sonnet|opus|haiku|fable|<id>|inherit`), `permissionMode`, `maxTurns`, `skills`, `mcpServers`, `hooks`, `memory` (`user|project|local`), `background`, `effort`, `isolation: worktree`, `color`, `initialPrompt`, `experimental.cacheTtl`; built-ins `Explore`, `Plan`, `general-purpose`; depth cap 3, 20 concurrent. Plugins: `.claude-plugin/plugin.json` plus `skills/ commands/ agents/ workflows/ hooks/hooks.json .mcp.json .lsp.json output-styles/ themes/ monitors/ bin/ scripts/ settings.json`; `claude plugin init|install|uninstall|prune|enable|disable|update|list [--scope user|project|local] [--config k=v] [-y] [--json]`; session-only `--plugin-dir <path|zip>`, `--plugin-url <url>`; `enabledPlugins` maps `"plugin@marketplace" → bool`; marketplace manifest `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`. Instruction files: `CLAUDE.md` only, **`AGENTS.md` is not read**; load order broad → specific: managed policy `CLAUDE.md` (`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/`, `/etc/claude-code/`, `C:\Program Files\ClaudeCode\`) → `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` → `./CLAUDE.md` or `./.claude/CLAUDE.md` → `./CLAUDE.local.md`; every directory from filesystem root down to cwd is scanned and all hits concatenated root-first; subdirectory files load on demand; `@path` imports max depth 4; `claudeMdExcludes` globs (managed policy file cannot be excluded); files > 4 MiB skipped; `--add-dir` memory only with `CLAUDE_CODE_ADDITIONAL_DIRECTORIES_CLAUDE_MD=1`; AGENTS.md workaround is `@AGENTS.md` inside CLAUDE.md or a symlink. Also `.claude/rules/*.md` (frontmatter `paths:` globs) and auto-memory `~/.claude/projects/<project>/memory/MEMORY.md`. `claude import [codex|gemini]` migrates another agent's config.

**Permissions.** Modes `default` (labelled Manual; alias `manual`), `acceptEdits`, `plan`, `auto`, `dontAsk`, `bypassPermissions`, set with `--permission-mode <mode>`, `--dangerously-skip-permissions` (≡ `bypassPermissions`), `--allow-dangerously-skip-permissions`, or `permissions.defaultMode` (a `bypassPermissions` default honoured only from user, `--settings`, or managed settings); `-p` starts in Manual on every plan. Blockers `permissions.disableBypassPermissionsMode: "disable"`, `permissions.disableAutoMode: "disable"`. Allow/deny: `--allowedTools "Bash(git *) Edit"`, `--disallowedTools`, `--tools` (`""` disables all built-ins), `permissions.allow|ask|deny|additionalDirectories`; evaluation **deny → ask → allow**, first match wins, specificity irrelevant; a bare tool name in `deny` removes the tool from context; rule syntax `Bash(npm run *)` (the space before `*` matters), `Read(//abs)`, `Read(~/home)`, `Read(/settings-relative)`, `Edit(./cwd)`, `WebFetch(domain:*.example.com)`, `mcp__server__tool`, `Agent(Explore)`, `Cd(**)`, parameter rules `Tool(param:value)` (deny/ask only; `command`, `file_path`, `url` not matchable this way). Unattended: `--permission-prompts none` denies anything that would prompt (removes `AskUserQuestion`, cancels unanswered MCP elicitations); denials surface as `permission_denied` system messages and `result.permission_denials`; `--permission-prompt-tool <mcp tool>` delegates prompts to an MCP tool. Sandbox: Bash sandbox built in on macOS (Seatbelt), Linux and WSL2 (bubblewrap + socat, optional seccomp from `@anthropic-ai/sandbox-runtime`); native Windows and WSL1 unsupported; settings `sandbox.{enabled, failIfUnavailable, autoAllowBashIfSandboxed, excludedCommands, allowUnsandboxedCommands, ignoreViolations, bwrapPath, socatPath, ripgrep}`, `sandbox.filesystem.{allowWrite,denyWrite,denyRead,allowRead,allowManagedReadPathsOnly,disabled}`, `sandbox.credentials.{files,envVars,allowPlaintextInject,awsPairs,sigv4}`, `sandbox.network.{allowedDomains,deniedDomains,strictAllowlist,allowManagedDomainsOnly,allowUnixSockets,allowAllUnixSockets,allowLocalBinding,allowMachLookup,httpProxyPort,socksProxyPort,tlsTerminate}`; filesystem arrays merge across scopes, `filesystem.disabled` cannot be set from project settings; `/sandbox` writes the mode to `.claude/settings.local.json`. Trust: the dialog appears in interactive sessions only; `-p`/SDK never show it but project `permissions.allow` and `additionalDirectories` are not applied and stderr warns "this workspace has not been trusted". Pre-answer by setting `projects["<repo-root>"].hasTrustDialogAccepted = true` in `~/.claude.json`; trust keys on the git repo root inside a repo, the start directory outside one, session-only in `$HOME`. Running before trust: settings-file hooks, the `env` block, `apiKeyHelper`, project-skill `hooks`/`allowed-tools`, `.mcp.json` servers under `-p`/SDK; only `headersHelper` is held back.

**Environment.** Settings precedence highest first: managed settings (`managed-settings.json`, MDM, console) → CLI `--settings <file-or-json>` → `.claude/settings.local.json` → `.claude/settings.json` → `~/.claude/settings.json`; list-valued keys merge across layers except `fallbackModel`, `modelPicker`, `availableModels`, `modelSettings`; `--setting-sources user,project,local` selects which filesystem sources load; verify with `/status`, `claude doctor`, `/config`. Env vars are not a stack level — a settings `env` block overwrites the shell value and can only be set to `""`, not unset; `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` and `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_CONNECT_IDE` are read before the equivalent settings keys; `--model`/`/model` beat `ANTHROPIC_MODEL`; `CLAUDE_CODE_EFFORT_LEVEL` beats `--effort` and `/effort`. Key env vars — auth/provider: `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN`, `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`, `ANTHROPIC_MODEL`, `ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_MODEL`, `ANTHROPIC_BETAS`, `ANTHROPIC_CUSTOM_HEADERS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_BEDROCK`, `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_VERTEX`, `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_FOUNDRY`, `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN`, `CLAUDE_CODE_API_KEY_HELPER_TTL_MS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_PROVIDER_MANAGED_BY_HOST`; locations: `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`, `CLAUDE_CODE_PROJECT_DIR_NAME` (shell env only), `CLAUDE_CODE_TMPDIR`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SHELL`, `CLAUDE_ENV_FILE`; traffic: `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC` (presence-only), `DISABLE_TELEMETRY` (presence-only), `DISABLE_ERROR_REPORTING` (presence-only), `DO_NOT_TRACK`, `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER`, `DISABLE_UPDATES`, `DISABLE_COST_WARNINGS`, `DISABLE_BUG_COMMAND`; limits: `MAX_THINKING_TOKENS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_CONTEXT_TOKENS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_TURNS`, `API_TIMEOUT_MS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_RETRIES`, `BASH_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS`, `BASH_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`, `BASH_MAX_OUTPUT_LENGTH`, `MCP_TIMEOUT` (30000), `MCP_TOOL_TIMEOUT` (~28 h), `MAX_MCP_OUTPUT_TOKENS` (25000), `CLAUDE_CODE_PRINT_BG_WAIT_CEILING_MS`; behavior: `CLAUDECODE=1` and `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID` in every subprocess, `CLAUDE_CODE_SIMPLE`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SAFE_MODE`, `CLAUDE_CODE_RESTRICTED`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_PROMPT_HISTORY`, `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_CLAUDE_MDS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_FORWARD_SUBAGENT_TEXT`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBPROCESS_ENV_SCRUB`, `CLAUDE_BASH_MAINTAIN_PROJECT_WORKING_DIR`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL[_FORCE]`, `USE_BUILTIN_RIPGREP`, `HTTP_PROXY`/`HTTPS_PROXY`/`NO_PROXY`. Global config keys live in `~/.claude.json`, not a settings file: `autoConnectIde`, `autoInstallIdeExtension`, `diffTool`, `externalEditorContext`, `permissionExplainerEnabled`, `teammateDefaultModel`. Model flag `--model` (alias `opus|sonnet|haiku|fable` or full id), `--fallback-model a,b`, `--effort low|medium|high|xhigh|max`, `--advisor`; settings `model`, `availableModels`, `modelSettings`, `fallbackModel`, `effortLevel`; pinning `autoUpdatesChannel` (`latest|stable`), `minimumVersion`, `requiredMinimumVersion`, `requiredMaximumVersion`. Per-action home isolation: `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` (plus `CLAUDE_CODE_PROJECT_DIR_NAME`).

**Observability.** OTel: `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TELEMETRY=1` then `OTEL_METRICS_EXPORTER`, `OTEL_LOGS_EXPORTER`, `OTEL_TRACES_EXPORTER`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_PROTOCOL|ENDPOINT|HEADERS` (+ per-signal), mTLS via `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_CLIENT_KEY|_CERTIFICATE` or `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_CERT|_KEY|_KEY_PASSPHRASE`, `otelHeadersHelper`; intervals `OTEL_METRIC_EXPORT_INTERVAL`, `OTEL_LOGS_EXPORT_INTERVAL`, `OTEL_TRACES_EXPORT_INTERVAL`; content gates `OTEL_LOG_USER_PROMPTS`, `OTEL_LOG_ASSISTANT_RESPONSES`, `OTEL_LOG_TOOL_DETAILS`, `OTEL_LOG_TOOL_CONTENT`, `OTEL_LOG_RAW_API_BODIES`; cardinality `OTEL_METRICS_INCLUDE_{SESSION_ID,VERSION,ACCOUNT_UUID,ENTRYPOINT,RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES}`; trace context `CLAUDE_CODE_PROPAGATE_TRACEPARENT`, `TRACEPARENT`, `TRACESTATE`. Metrics (8): `claude_code.session.count`, `.lines_of_code.count`, `.pull_request.count`, `.commit.count`, `.cost.usage`, `.token.usage`, `.code_edit_tool.decision`, `.active_time.total`. Events (12): `claude_code.user_prompt`, `.assistant_response`, `.tool_result`, `.api_request`, `.api_error`, `.api_refusal`, `.api_request_body`, `.api_response_body`, `.tool_decision`, `.permission_mode_changed`, `.auth`, `.mcp_server_connection`; correlation attributes `prompt.id`, `message.uuid`, `client_request_id`. Spans (beta): `claude_code.interaction` → `.llm_request`, `.hook`, `.tool` → `.tool.blocked_on_user`, `.tool.execution`. Logs: `-d/--debug [filter]` (`api,hooks`, `!1p,!file`), `--debug-file <path>`, `CLAUDE_CODE_DEBUG_LOGS_DIR`, `~/.claude/debug/`, `claude doctor`. Cost/usage: `--output-format json` gives `total_cost_usd`, `usage`, `modelUsage` (client-side estimates); `--max-budget-usd` stops a run; `/cost`, `/usage`, `/stats`. Exit codes: `0` success, non-zero failure; SIGTERM → **143** (turn unfinished, no result recorded, Bash process trees killed, only `SessionEnd` hooks run); SIGINT / SDK `interrupt()` ends the turn cleanly; `--max-turns` exhaustion is an error exit. Partly trustworthy: an invalid flag reports to stderr before the run, but an in-run failure (e.g. missing auth) is printed as the *result* on stdout — check `is_error`/`subtype`.

**Gotchas.**
- `-p` skips the workspace trust dialog entirely: a repo's `.claude/settings.json` hooks, `env` block and `.mcp.json` servers run in a folder never trusted. Use `--bare` (or `--strict-mcp-config` + `--setting-sources`) for untrusted checkouts.
- Under `-p` the starting permission mode is Manual on every plan — without `--permission-mode` or `--allowedTools` the run denies or stalls.
- `--verbose` is mandatory for `stream-json`; `--include-partial-messages` and `--forward-subagent-text` need `-p` + `stream-json`.
- Background Bash started during `-p` is killed ~5 s after the result; background subagents/workflows hold the process open until they finish, capped at 10 min idle (`CLAUDE_CODE_PRINT_BG_WAIT_CEILING_MS`, `0` = no cap); a `Monitor` watch is waited on too (5-min default).
- If the consumer reads stdout slowly, Claude Code waits up to 30 s for the queue to drain before exiting.
- Terminal: the input box collapses pastes over 800 chars or 3 lines; below 12 rows the line threshold drops, at ≤10 rows any multi-line paste collapses. Under tmux set `allow-passthrough on`, `extended-keys on`, `terminal-features 'xterm*:extkeys'`. `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_SYNC_OUTPUT=1` or `CLAUDE_CODE_NO_FLICKER=1` fix flicker; `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ALTERNATE_SCREEN`, `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_MOUSE` for constrained PTYs.
- Settings files that fail validation are silently ignored in `-p` mode.
- Do not parse `~/.claude/projects/**/*.jsonl` — explicitly internal and version-unstable.
- `~/.claude` is also written by the VS Code extension, JetBrains plugin and Desktop app; it reappears if any of them runs.

**Driver notes.**
- Window mode: spawn `claude` in a PTY with `CLAUDE_CODE_NO_FLICKER=1` (or `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ALTERNATE_SCREEN`/`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_MOUSE` on constrained PTYs); keep ≥12 rows so pastes are not collapsed; under tmux enable passthrough and extended keys.
- Session mode: one `claude -p --output-format json --permission-mode <mode> -r <session-id>` per turn; capture `session_id` from the first run's `result`, never parse the JSONL transcript; re-pass `--mcp-config`, `--settings`, `--plugin-dir`, `--fallback-model`, `--add-dir` on every resume since they are not restored.
- Batch mode: `--bare` for untrusted checkouts (skips hooks/plugins/CLAUDE.md and forces API-key auth), plus `--permission-prompts none`, `--max-turns`, `--max-budget-usd`; read `permission_denials` and `terminal_reason` rather than trusting the process exit code alone.
- Pre-answer trust by writing `projects["<repo-root>"].hasTrustDialogAccepted = true` in `~/.claude.json`; isolate per-action config with `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`.
- Most stable surface: `-p --input-format stream-json --output-format stream-json --verbose` with the control protocol (`control_request`/`control_response`) for `canUseTool` and interrupts; the Agent SDK wraps the same protocol.
- Handle SIGTERM → 143 as an unfinished turn with no result; use SIGINT or SDK `interrupt()` for clean turn termination.

**Sources.** https://code.claude.com/docs/llms.txt · https://code.claude.com/docs/en/cli-reference · https://code.claude.com/docs/en/headless · https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks · https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings-reference · https://code.claude.com/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript

---

### Codex CLI (OpenAI, Apache-2.0, v0.153.4, `curl -fsSL https://chatgpt.com/codex/install.sh | sh`)

**Launch.** TUI: `codex [OPTIONS] [PROMPT]`; `--no-alt-screen` runs inline instead of the alternate screen. Headless: `codex exec [OPTIONS] [PROMPT]` (alias `codex e`) with subcommands `resume`, `fork`, `review`; also non-interactive `codex review`, `codex apply`. Stdin: `codex exec` reads the prompt from stdin when no prompt argument is given or the argument is `-`; if stdin is piped *and* a prompt is given, stdin is appended as a `<stdin>` context block. SDK/server/ACP: TS `npm install @openai/codex-sdk` (Node 18+) — `new Codex()`, `codex.startThread()`, `thread.run(prompt)` → `result.finalResponse`, `codex.resumeThread(threadId)`; Python `pip install openai-codex` (3.10+) — `Codex`/`AsyncCodex`, `codex.thread_start(model=…, sandbox=Sandbox.workspace_write)`, `thread.run(…)` → `result.final_response`, presets `Sandbox.read_only|workspace_write|full_access` settable per turn, `CodexConfig(codex_bin=…)`; both drive the local app-server over JSON-RPC. App server: `codex app-server --listen stdio://|unix://[PATH]|ws://IP:PORT|off` (`--stdio` shorthand), JSON-RPC 2.0 newline-delimited with the `"jsonrpc"` header omitted on the wire; WS listeners expose `GET /readyz` and `GET /healthz` and 403 any request carrying `Origin`; non-loopback auth `--ws-auth capability-token|signed-bearer-token`, `--ws-token-file`, `--ws-token-sha256`, `--ws-shared-secret-file`, `--ws-issuer`, `--ws-audience`, `--ws-max-clock-skew-seconds`; bindings via `codex app-server generate-ts` / `generate-json-schema`; `codex app-server daemon` runs a shared local daemon, `codex app-server proxy` bridges stdio to its control socket, `codex --remote ws://…` and `codex remote-control` attach a TUI. No ACP.

**Input and follow-up.** Prompt via argv, stdin (pipe or literal `-`), or `codex exec resume <SESSION_ID> "<follow-up>"`; images with `-i/--image <FILE>…`. Each `codex exec` is a fresh process; follow-ups `codex exec resume --last "<prompt>"`, `codex exec resume <SESSION_ID> "<prompt>"`, `codex exec fork` to branch; `codex queue` pushes a message into an already-running session. For a genuine long-lived conversation use the app server (`thread/start`, `turn/start`, `turn/steer`, `turn/interrupt`) or an SDK thread object.

**Output.** Default human text with **progress on stderr and only the final agent message on stdout**. Flags `--json` (stdout becomes JSONL of every event), `-o, --output-last-message <FILE>` (final message to a file, still printed to stdout), `--output-schema <FILE>` (JSON Schema for the final response; accepted by `codex exec` and `codex exec resume`), `--color always|never|auto`. Event types: `thread.started` (`thread_id`), `turn.started`, `turn.completed` (`usage`), `turn.failed`, `item.started`, `item.updated`, `item.completed`, `error`. Item types: `agent_message` (`text`), `reasoning`, `command_execution` (`command`, `status`), `file_changes`, MCP tool call, web search, plan update. Sample: `{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_3","type":"agent_message","text":"…"}}`, `{"type":"turn.completed","usage":{"input_tokens":24763,"cached_input_tokens":24448,"output_tokens":122,"reasoning_output_tokens":0}}`. Final answer: last `item.completed` with `item.type == "agent_message"` → `item.text`, or the `-o` file. App-server events are richer: `thread/started`, `item/started`, `item/completed`, `item/agentMessage/delta`, `item/reasoning/textDelta`, `item/reasoning/summaryTextDelta`, `item/commandExecution/outputDelta`, `item/commandExecution/requestApproval`, `item/fileChange/requestApproval`, `item/permissions/requestApproval`, `item/tool/call`, `item/tool/requestUserInput`, `item/plan/delta`, `turn/started`, `turn/completed`, `turn/plan/updated`, `turn/diff/updated`, `thread/tokenUsage/updated`, `hook/completed`.

**Sessions.** Session identity = thread id (UUIDv7) from `thread.started.thread_id`. Flags `codex resume [SESSION_ID|NAME] [PROMPT] [--last] [--all] [--include-non-interactive]`, `codex fork [--last]`, `codex exec resume [SESSION_ID] [PROMPT] [--last] [--all]`, `codex exec fork`, `--ephemeral` (no persistence), `--thread-source <SOURCE>`; management `codex archive|unarchive|delete <id|name>`, `codex agents`, `codex migrate-rollouts`, `codex queue`. Transcript `$CODEX_HOME/sessions/<YYYY>/<MM>/<DD>/rollout-<ISO8601>-<uuid>.jsonl`; first line `{"timestamp":…,"ordinal":0,"type":"session_meta","payload":{"session_id":…,"id":…,"timestamp":…,"cwd":…,"originator":"codex-tui","cli_version":"0.153.2","source":"cli","thread_source":"user","model_provider":…,"base_instructions":{…}}}`, later lines ordinal-numbered event records. Listing: `$CODEX_HOME/session_index.jsonl`, one line per thread `{"id":"<uuid>","thread_name":"…","updated_at":"<RFC3339>"}` (cheapest programmatic list); also the `codex resume` picker, `thread/list` over app-server, and `$CODEX_HOME/history.jsonl` for prompt history.

**Hooks.** Events (12): `SessionStart`, `SessionEnd`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse`, `PermissionRequest`, `Stop`, `Interrupt`, `PreCompact`, `PostCompact`, `SubagentStart`, `SubagentStop`. Locations (all loaded, higher layers do not replace lower): `~/.codex/hooks.json`, `[hooks]` in `~/.codex/config.toml`, `<repo>/.codex/hooks.json`, `[hooks]` in `<repo>/.codex/config.toml`, plugin-bundled `hooks/hooks.json`, managed hooks from `requirements.toml`/MDM (`hooks.managed_dir`, `hooks.windows_managed_dir`); a layer holding both forms merges them and warns; project hooks load only in a trusted project. Shape `{"hooks":{"<Event>":[{"matcher":"startup|resume","hooks":[{"type":"command","command":"…","statusMessage":"…","additionalContextLimit":5000}]}]}}`. Handler types: `command` (JSON stdin → JSON stdout) and `mcp_tool` (`server`, `tool`, `input` template, `timeout`, `statusMessage`); options `timeout` (**seconds**, default `600`; `SessionEnd` default `1`), `async`, `additionalContextLimit` (default 2500 tokens, `0` = uncapped), `commandWindows`. Payload casing: snake_case stdin — `session_id`, `transcript_path` (may be null), `cwd`, `hook_event_name`, `model`, `permission_mode` (`default|acceptEdits|plan|dontAsk|bypassPermissions`), `turn_id` on turn-scoped events; extras `SessionStart.source` (`startup|resume|clear|compact`), `SessionEnd.reason` (currently only `other`), `UserPromptSubmit.prompt`, `PreToolUse`/`PostToolUse` `tool_name` (canonical `Bash`, `apply_patch`, or `mcp__<server>__<tool>`), `tool_use_id`, `tool_input` (Bash/apply_patch use `tool_input.command`), `PostToolUse.tool_response`, `PermissionRequest.tool_input.description`, `PreCompact`/`PostCompact.trigger` (`manual|auto`), `SubagentStart`/`SubagentStop` `agent_id`, `agent_type`, `agent_transcript_path`, `stop_hook_active`, `last_assistant_message`, `Stop.stop_hook_active`/`.last_assistant_message`; camelCase JSON stdout. Control: exit `0` with JSON on stdout (or nothing) is success; exit `2` blocks with stderr as the reason. Deny `{"decision":"block","reason":"…"}`; allow `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"…","decision":{"behavior":"allow"}}}`; inject `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"additionalContext":"…"}}`; rewrite args on `PreToolUse` `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"permissionDecision":"allow","updatedInput":{…}}}`. Shared fields `continue`, `stopReason`, `systemMessage`, `suppressOutput` (parsed, not implemented) apply to `SessionStart`, `PreCompact`, `PostCompact`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `SubagentStop`, `Stop`; `PreToolUse`/`PermissionRequest` accept only `systemMessage` (returning the others fails that hook and the call proceeds — fail-open); `PostToolUse` accepts `systemMessage`, `continue:false`, `stopReason`. Oversized output spills to `<temp_dir>/hook_outputs/<session_id>/<uuid>.txt` with a head/tail preview plus the path given to the model. Matchers (regex) on `PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse`, `PermissionRequest`, `PreCompact`, `PostCompact`, `SessionStart`, `SessionEnd`, `SubagentStart`, `SubagentStop`; ignored on `UserPromptSubmit`, `Stop`, `Interrupt`; `"*"` or omitted matches all. Trust gating: a non-managed hook must be reviewed and trusted **by content hash** through `/hooks` in the TUI before it runs, and a changed hook is skipped until re-trusted; escape hatch `--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust`; admins force `allow_managed_hooks_only = true` in `requirements.toml`.

**MCP.** Config `[mcp_servers.<id>]` in `config.toml`; CLI `codex mcp add <NAME> (--url <URL> | -- <COMMAND>…)` with `--env KEY=VALUE`, `--bearer-token-env-var`, `--oauth-client-id`, `--oauth-client-registration auto|cimd|dcr`, `--oauth-resource`, plus `codex mcp list|get|remove|login|logout`; ad-hoc `-c mcp_servers.x.command=…`. Transports: stdio (`command`, `args`, `env`, `env_vars`, `cwd`) and streamable HTTP (`url`, `bearer_token_env_var`, `http_headers`, `http_headers_helper`, `env_http_headers`); no SSE transport documented. Auth: OAuth via `[mcp_servers.<id>.oauth]` (`client_id`, `callback_url`, `callback_port`), globals `mcp_oauth_callback_port`, `mcp_oauth_callback_url`, `mcp_oauth_credentials_store`, `codex mcp login|logout <name>`, per-server `scopes`, `oauth_resource`. Scopes: user (`~/.codex/config.toml`), project (`<repo>/.codex/config.toml`, trusted projects only), managed (`requirements.toml`/MDM); no per-session local scope. Tool filtering: `enabled_tools` (allow list), `disabled_tools` (deny, applied after), per-tool `tools.<tool>.approval_mode` and `.output_token_limit`, `default_tools_approval_mode`; timeouts `startup_timeout_sec` (10), `tool_timeout_sec` (60), `mcp_optional_startup_grace_ms`; `enabled = false` disables. Plugin-provided servers configure under `plugins.<plugin>.mcp_servers.<server>` (on/off and tool policy only). Acts as MCP server: yes but **deprecated** — `codex mcp-server` (stdio); use the app server or the Codex plugin for Claude Code. Unreachable server: skipped by default; `required = true` makes `codex exec` fail hard when the server does not initialise.

**Extensibility.** Commands: built-ins only (`/review`, `/hooks`, `/plugins`, `/archive`, `/delete`, …); custom prompt files are deprecated, reusable prompts are skills. Skills: a directory with `SKILL.md` carrying `name` and `description`; discovery `REPO` — `$CWD/.agents/skills`, every ancestor `.agents/skills` up to the git root, `$REPO_ROOT/.agents/skills`; `USER` — `$HOME/.agents/skills`; `ADMIN` — `/etc/codex/skills`; `SYSTEM` — bundled; plus `$CODEX_HOME/skills/` and explicit `skills.config.<index>.path`/`.enabled`; budget `skills.max_context_tokens`; symlinks followed; same-named skills are not merged. Subagents: `[agents]` with `enabled`, `max_concurrent_threads_per_session`, `max_threads`, `default_subagent_model`, `default_subagent_reasoning_effort`, `interrupt_message`, per-agent `agents.<name>.description` / `.config_file`. Plugins: `codex plugin add|list|remove|marketplace`, `/plugins` in the TUI, installed under `$CODEX_HOME/plugins/`; bundle MCP servers, hooks and skills; governance `marketplaces.restrict_to_allowed_sources`, `marketplaces.allowed_sources.<name>.{source,url,ref,host_pattern,path}`. Instruction files: `AGENTS.md`; precedence (1) global `$CODEX_HOME/AGENTS.override.md`, else `$CODEX_HOME/AGENTS.md` — only the first non-empty file; (2) project scope — walk from the project/git root down to cwd and in each directory take `AGENTS.override.md`, else `AGENTS.md`, else the first hit in `project_doc_fallback_filenames`, at most one file per directory; (3) concatenate root-first so files nearer cwd override. Empty files skipped; total capped by `project_doc_max_bytes` (32 KiB); related keys `project_root_markers`, `instructions`, `developer_instructions`, `model_instructions_file`.

**Permissions.** Approvals `-a/--ask-for-approval on-request|never`; config `approval_policy` with `approval_policy.granular.{sandbox_approval,rules,mcp_elicitations,request_permissions,skill_approval}`, `approvals_reviewer`, `auto_review.policy`; `--approve-for-me` routes approvals through automatic review under workspace-write. Sandbox `-s/--sandbox read-only|workspace-write|danger-full-access` (config `sandbox_mode`; `codex exec` defaults to `read-only`); tuning `sandbox_workspace_write.{writable_roots,network_access,exclude_tmpdir_env_var,exclude_slash_tmp}`, `--add-dir <DIR>` adds writable roots, `-C/--cd <DIR>` sets the working root; Seatbelt on macOS, Landlock/seccomp on Linux, native Windows sandbox (`windows.sandbox`, `windows.sandbox_private_desktop`); `codex sandbox <cmd>` runs an arbitrary command in the same sandbox. Bypass `--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox`; deprecated alias `codex exec --full-auto` (warns). Beta permission profiles `default_permissions`, `permissions.<name>.{description,extends,workspace_roots,filesystem,network.*}`; admin allow lists `allowed_approval_policies`, `allowed_sandbox_modes`, `allowed_permission_profiles`, `guardian_policy_config`. execpolicy `.rules` files (user + project) gate commands allowed outside the sandbox; `--ignore-rules` skips them; config `rules.prefix_rules[].{pattern,decision,justification}`. Folder trust: `projects."<abs path>".trust_level = "trusted"` in `~/.codex/config.toml` pre-answers the prompt; untrusted projects still load user/system hooks but not project config, project hooks or project `.rules`; `--skip-git-repo-check` bypasses the "must be inside a git repo" guard.

**Environment.** Layers `$CODEX_HOME/config.toml` (user) → profiles `$CODEX_HOME/<name>.config.toml` via `-p/--profile` → project `<repo>/.codex/config.toml` (trusted only) → managed `requirements.toml`. Project config cannot override `openai_base_url`, `chatgpt_base_url`, `apps_mcp_product_sku`, `model_provider`, `model_providers`, `notify`, `profile`, `profiles`, `experimental_realtime_ws_base_url`, `otel`. Ad-hoc `-c key=value` (dotted path, TOML-parsed), `--enable/--disable <FEATURE>` (≡ `features.<name>`), `--strict-config`, `--ignore-user-config`, `--ignore-rules`. Env vars: `CODEX_HOME` (default `~/.codex`; the directory must already exist if you set the var), `CODEX_SQLITE_HOME`, `CODEX_API_KEY`, `CODEX_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `CODEX_CA_CERTIFICATE` (beats `SSL_CERT_FILE`), `OPENAI_FEDERATION_RULE_ID`, `OPENAI_IDENTITY_TOKEN_FILE`, `OPENAI_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_CONTEXT`, `RUST_LOG`, installer `CODEX_NON_INTERACTIVE`, `CODEX_INSTALL_DIR`; provider key variable names are chosen via `model_providers.<id>.env_key`. Config dir holds `config.toml`, `<profile>.config.toml`, `auth.json`, `sessions/`, `session_index.jsonl`, `history.jsonl`, `skills/`, `plugins/`, `rules/`, `memories/`, `log/`, `shell_snapshots/`, SQLite (`state_*.sqlite`, `logs_*.sqlite`, `queue_*.sqlite`, `goals_*.sqlite`) relocatable via `CODEX_SQLITE_HOME`/`sqlite_home`; project layer `<repo>/.codex/config.toml`, `<repo>/.codex/hooks.json`. Model flag `-m/--model`; config `model`, `model_reasoning_effort`, `model_reasoning_summary`, `model_verbosity`, `model_context_window`, `review_model`, `plan_mode_reasoning_effort`; `--oss` with `--local-provider lmstudio|ollama`; custom `[model_providers.<id>]` (`base_url`, `wire_api`, `env_key`, `auth.command`, `request_max_retries`). Quieting traffic: `analytics.enabled = false` (app-server analytics off unless `--analytics-default-enabled`), `feedback.enabled = false`, `check_for_update_on_startup = false`, `otel.metrics_exporter = "none"` (default `statsig`), `otel.exporter = "none"`. Per-action home isolation: `CODEX_HOME` (plus `CODEX_SQLITE_HOME`).

**Observability.** OTel user-level `[otel]`: `environment` (default `dev`), `exporter` (`none|otlp-http|otlp-grpc`), `trace_exporter`, `metrics_exporter` (`none|statsig|otlp-http|otlp-grpc`, default `statsig`), `log_user_prompt`, plus `otel.exporter.<id>.{endpoint,protocol,headers}` and `.tls.{ca-certificate,client-certificate,client-private-key}`. Logs: `RUST_LOG` (`codex exec` defaults to `error`); a plaintext `codex-tui.log` only when `log_dir` is set, otherwise bounded SQLite stores under `CODEX_HOME`; `codex doctor` checks install/config/auth/runtime. Usage: `turn.completed.usage` → `input_tokens`, `cached_input_tokens`, `output_tokens`, `reasoning_output_tokens`; **no dollar cost is emitted**; app-server adds `thread/tokenUsage/updated`, `account/rateLimits/read|updated`, `account/usage/read`. Exit codes: `0` on success, non-zero on failure (e.g. a `required = true` MCP server that fails to start); `codex login status` exits `0` when credentials exist; trustworthy as pass/fail, but a per-code table is unknown. `notify = ["<command>", …]` runs an external command with a JSON payload on notification events (user-level config only).

**Gotchas.**
- Progress on stderr, answer on stdout — never merge the streams. With `--json`, stdout is JSONL and stderr stays log output.
- `codex exec` requires a git repo unless `--skip-git-repo-check`.
- Default sandbox for `codex exec` is `read-only`; edits silently fail unless you pass `--sandbox workspace-write`.
- Hooks installed by automation never run until reviewed in the TUI — pass `--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust` or ship them as managed hooks.
- Project trust gates project `config.toml`, hooks and `.rules`; pre-seed `projects."<path>".trust_level = "trusted"`.
- The TUI uses the alternate screen by default — use `--no-alt-screen` under a PTY; related `tui.alternate_screen`, `tui.animations`, `tui.raw_output_mode`, `tui.terminal_title`, `tui.notifications`.
- `CODEX_HOME` state grows fast (`logs_*.sqlite` reached ~5 GB on this machine); point `CODEX_SQLITE_HOME`/`sqlite_home` elsewhere, or use `--ephemeral`.
- Never export `CODEX_API_KEY`/`OPENAI_API_KEY` job-wide in a job that also runs repository-controlled code.
- Every `codex exec resume` is a cold process — MCP servers reconnect each turn, so mind `startup_timeout_sec` and `required = true`.

**Driver notes.**
- Window mode: PTY-spawn `codex --no-alt-screen`; set `tui.animations`/`tui.raw_output_mode` for a stable capture surface.
- Session mode: `codex exec --json [--sandbox workspace-write] [--skip-git-repo-check]`, capture `thread.started.thread_id`, then `codex exec resume <id> "<prompt>" --json` per turn; budget for MCP server restarts each turn.
- Batch mode: `codex exec --json -o <file> --output-schema <schema>` with `-a never`, `--ephemeral` for throwaway state, `CODEX_HOME`/`CODEX_SQLITE_HOME` pointed at a per-action directory.
- Pre-seed `projects."<path>".trust_level = "trusted"` and either bypass hook trust with `--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust` or install hooks as managed — otherwise driver-installed hooks silently never fire.
- Read the final answer from the last `item.completed` with `item.type == "agent_message"` (or `-o`); never from merged stdout+stderr.
- Most stable surface: `codex app-server` (JSON-RPC 2.0 over stdio/unix/ws, generated TS and JSON-Schema bindings, `turn/steer` and `turn/interrupt`); the TS/Python SDKs are thin wrappers over it. Avoid the deprecated `codex mcp-server`.

**Sources.** https://github.com/openai/codex · https://learn.chatgpt.com/llms.txt · https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/non-interactive-mode.md · https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/hooks.md · https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/app-server.md · https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/config-file/config-reference.md

---

### Gemini CLI (Google, Apache-2.0, v0.55.1, `npm install -g @google/gemini-cli`)

**Launch.** TUI: `gemini` or `gemini [query..]` (a bare query still starts interactive). Headless: `-p, --prompt "<text>"`, also entered automatically in a non-TTY environment; `-i, --prompt-interactive "<text>"` runs the prompt then stays interactive. Stdin: yes — `-p` text is appended to whatever arrives on stdin. SDK/server/ACP: no first-party agent-driving SDK package; **ACP** via `--acp` (canonical; `--experimental-acp` is the deprecated alias) — Agent Client Protocol, JSON-RPC 2.0 over stdio with `initialize`, `authenticate`, `newSession`, `loadSession`, `prompt`, `cancel`, `setSessionMode`, `unstable_setSessionModel`; the filesystem is proxied through the client and the client may register its own MCP server during `initialize`, which Gemini then connects to as a client. Worktrees `-w, --worktree [name]` (requires `experimental.worktrees: true`). Latest release `v0.58.0` (2026-09-01); npm dist-tags `latest=0.58.0`, `preview=0.59.0-preview.0`, `nightly=0.60.0-nightly.20260908`.

**Input and follow-up.** Prompt via positional `query..`, `-p/--prompt`, `-i/--prompt-interactive`, or stdin. No live stdin channel for headless multi-turn; follow-ups via `-r/--resume latest|<index>|<uuid>`, `--session-file <path>`, or `--session-id <uuid>` (caller-chosen UUID, `^[a-zA-Z0-9-_]+$`); for real multi-turn use `--acp`. Combinations rejected at parse time: positional query + `-p`; `-p` + `-i`; `-y` + `--approval-mode`; any two of `--resume` / `--session-id` / `--session-file`. Turn cap `model.maxSessionTurns` (default `-1`); hitting it non-interactively exits `53`.

**Output.** `-o, --output-format text|json|stream-json`; `--raw-output` disables ANSI sanitisation (`--accept-raw-output-risk` silences the warning). `output.format` in settings.json accepts only `text|json` — `stream-json` is CLI-flag-only. `json` (`JsonOutput`, pretty-printed): `{session_id?, response?, stats?, error?:{type,message,code?}, warnings?:string[]}` where `stats` is the full `SessionMetrics` `{models:{<name>:{api:{totalRequests,totalErrors,totalLatencyMs,errorsByType?}, tokens:{input,prompt,candidates,total,cached,thoughts,tool}, roles}}, tools:{totalCalls,totalSuccess,totalFail,totalDurationMs,totalDecisions,byName}, files:{totalLinesAdded,totalLinesRemoved}}`. `stream-json` NDJSON, every event has `type` and `timestamp` (ISO-8601): `init {session_id, model}`, `message {role:"user"|"assistant", content, delta?}`, `tool_use {tool_name, tool_id, parameters}`, `tool_result {tool_id, status:"success"|"error", output?, error?:{type,message}}`, `error {severity:"warning"|"error", message}`, `result {status:"success"|"error", error?:{type,message}, stats?}`. Sequence `init` → `message`(user) → n× assistant `message` deltas / `tool_use` / `tool_result` / `error` → exactly one terminal `result`. `result.stats` (`StreamStats`, flattened): `{total_tokens, input_tokens, output_tokens, cached, input, duration_ms, tool_calls, models:{<model>:{total_tokens,input_tokens,output_tokens,cached,input}}}`. `tool_result.output` is present only when the display result is a string. Final answer: `response` in `json` mode; in `stream-json` **the `result` event has no `response` field** — accumulate assistant text from `message` events with `role:"assistant"`, `delta:true` (`docs/cli/headless.md` claims otherwise; the source is authoritative).

**Sessions.** Flags `-r/--resume [latest|<index>|<uuid>]`, `--list-sessions`, `--delete-session <index|id>`, `--session-id <uuid>`, `--session-file <path>`; in session `/resume` opens the Session Browser (`/` search, Enter resume, `x` delete), plus `/resume save <name>`, `/resume list`, `/resume resume <name>` for named checkpoints (`/chat …` alias). Storage `~/.gemini/tmp/<project_hash>/chats/`, project-scoped (`<project_hash>` derives from the project root); saved content is prompts, responses, all tool executions with inputs and outputs, token usage, reasoning summaries; a parallel `~/.gemini/history/<project>/` tree carries a `.project_root` marker. Retention `general.sessionRetention.{enabled,maxAge,maxCount,minRetention}` — defaults `true`, `"30d"`, unlimited, `"1d"`; deleting a session also removes its plans, task trackers, tool outputs and activity logs.

**Hooks.** Events (11): `SessionStart`, `SessionEnd`, `BeforeAgent`, `AfterAgent`, `BeforeModel`, `AfterModel`, `BeforeToolSelection`, `BeforeTool`, `AfterTool`, `PreCompress`, `Notification`. Config: the `hooks` object in `settings.json`; merge precedence highest first project `.gemini/settings.json` → user `~/.gemini/settings.json` → system `/etc/gemini-cli/settings.json` → extension hooks (also `<ext>/hooks/hooks.json`); `gemini hooks migrate` imports Claude Code hooks; master switches `hooksConfig.enabled`, `hooksConfig.disabled` (list of commands), `hooksConfig.notifications`. Shape `hooks.<Event>[] = { matcher, sequential, hooks: [ {name, type:"command", command, timeout, description} ] }`; only `type: "command"` exists; `sequential: true` serialises a group (default parallel). Timeout is in **milliseconds**, default `60000`. Payload casing: snake_case stdin — base `session_id`, `transcript_path`, `cwd`, `hook_event_name`, `timestamp`; per event `tool_name`, `tool_input`, `mcp_context`, `original_request_name` (`BeforeTool`), `tool_response{llmContent,returnDisplay,error}` (`AfterTool`), `prompt` (`BeforeAgent`), `prompt`/`prompt_response`/`stop_hook_active` (`AfterAgent`), `llm_request` (`BeforeModel`, `BeforeToolSelection`), `llm_request`+`llm_response` (`AfterModel`), `source` (`startup|resume|clear`) on `SessionStart`, `reason` (`exit|clear|logout|prompt_input_exit|other`) on `SessionEnd`, `notification_type` (`"ToolPermission"`)/`message`/`details` on `Notification`, `trigger` (`auto|manual`) on `PreCompress`; camelCase output fields. Control: exit `0` → stdout parsed as JSON and **stdout must contain nothing else** (a single stray `echo` breaks parsing, after which the CLI defaults to allow and treats the whole output as `systemMessage` — fail-open); exit `2` = hard block with stderr as the reason; any other exit = non-fatal warning, action proceeds; log on stderr only. Common output fields `systemMessage`, `suppressOutput`, `continue` (`false` kills the whole agent loop), `stopReason`, `decision` (`"allow"`/`"deny"`, alias `"block"`), `reason`. Per-event powers: `BeforeTool` — `hookSpecificOutput.tool_input` merges over and overrides the model's arguments, deny sends `reason` to the model as a tool error; `AfterTool` — deny `reason` replaces the tool result, `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` appends, `hookSpecificOutput.tailToolCallRequest {name,args}` runs a follow-up tool whose result replaces the original; `BeforeAgent` — `additionalContext` appends to the prompt for that turn, `decision:"deny"` discards the user message, `continue:false` blocks but keeps it in history; `AfterAgent` — `decision:"deny"` forces a retry with `reason` as the new prompt, `hookSpecificOutput.clearContext` wipes LLM memory; `BeforeModel` — `hookSpecificOutput.llm_request` overrides the outgoing request, `hookSpecificOutput.llm_response` short-circuits the model call; `AfterModel` — fires per streamed chunk, `llm_response` replaces the chunk; `BeforeToolSelection` — `toolConfig.mode` (`AUTO|ANY|NONE`, `NONE` wins) and `toolConfig.allowedFunctionNames` (whitelists from several hooks are unioned), no `decision`/`continue`/`systemMessage`; `SessionStart` advisory but can inject `additionalContext`; `SessionEnd` (not awaited), `Notification`, `PreCompress` advisory only. Stable model API `LLMRequest {model, messages[{role:"user|model|system", content}], config, toolConfig{mode, allowedFunctionNames}}`, `LLMResponse {candidates[{content{role,parts[]}, finishReason}], usageMetadata{totalTokenCount}}`. Matchers: regex for `BeforeTool`/`AfterTool` against tool names (MCP tools are `mcp_<server>_<tool>`), exact strings for lifecycle events, `"*"`/`""` for all. Trust gating of hooks: none stated.

**MCP.** Config `mcpServers.<NAME>` in `settings.json` — `command`, `args`, `env`, `cwd` (stdio); `url` (**SSE**), `httpUrl` (**streamable HTTP**), `headers`; plus `timeout` (ms), `trust` (bypass all confirmations for that server), `description`, `includeTools` (allow list), `excludeTools` (deny list, wins over `includeTools`); precedence when several are set `httpUrl` > `url` > `command`; settings-level `mcp.serverCommand`, `mcp.allowed`, `mcp.excluded`. CLI `gemini mcp add [-s|--scope user|project] [-t|--transport stdio|sse|http] [-e KEY=val] [-H "K: V"] [--timeout ms] [--trust] [--description] [--include-tools] [--exclude-tools] <name> <commandOrUrl> [args…]`, `gemini mcp remove|list|enable|disable <name>`; default scope is **project**. Session filter `--allowed-mcp-server-names <names…>`. Scopes: user (`~/.gemini/settings.json`), project (`<project>/.gemini/settings.json`), system files, extension-contributed servers. Auth: OAuth not documented for MCP servers — unknown, only static `headers` are described. Tool naming `mcp_<serverAlias>_<toolName>`; never put `_` in a server alias — the policy engine splits on the first underscore after `mcp_` and security policies fail silently. Acts as MCP server: **no** — `gemini mcp` registers only `add|remove|list|enable|disable`; the sole `new McpServer(...)` in the repo is the VS Code companion exposing the IDE's tools to Gemini. Use `--acp`, or wrap `gemini -p` in your own MCP server. Unreachable server: behavior unknown from the source notes.

**Extensibility.** Custom slash commands: TOML — user `~/.gemini/commands/*.toml`, project `<root>/.gemini/commands/*.toml` (project wins), extension `<ext>/commands/*.toml` (lowest; on conflict exposed as `/<extname>.<cmd>`); path separators become `:` (`.gemini/commands/git/commit.toml` → `/git:commit`); fields `prompt` (required), `description`; injection `{{args}}` (raw outside `!{}`, shell-escaped inside), `!{shell command}` (confirmed, output injected, failure appends `[Shell command exited with code N]`), `@{path}` (file or recursive directory content); processing order `@{}` → `!{}` → `{{args}}`; `/commands list`, `/commands reload`. Skills: `SKILL.md` with required `name` and `description` frontmatter, conventional `scripts/`, `references/`, `assets/`; tiers low → high built-in → extension `<ext>/skills/` → user `~/.gemini/skills/` or `~/.agents/skills/` → workspace `.gemini/skills/` or `.agents/skills/` (within a tier `.agents/skills/` wins); the model activates one through the `activate_skill` tool; CLI `gemini skills list [--all] | install <source> [--path <subdir>] [--consent] | link | uninstall | enable | disable`, `--scope user|workspace` (default `user`); settings `skills.enabled`, `skills.disabled`. Extensions: `<home>/.gemini/extensions/<name>/` with `gemini-extension.json` — `name`, `version`, `description`, `mcpServers` (all MCP options except `trust`), `contextFileName`, `excludeTools` (supports `"run_shell_command(rm -rf)"`), `migratedTo`, `plan.directory`, `settings[{name,description,envVar,sensitive}]` (stored in an extension-local `.env`, keychain when `sensitive`), `themes`; conventional layout `commands/*.toml`, `hooks/hooks.json` (not declared in the manifest), `skills/<name>/SKILL.md`, `agents/*.md`, `policies/*.toml`; variables `${extensionPath}`, `${workspacePath}`, `${/}`; extensions do **not** inherit your shell environment — only `HOME`, `PATH`, `TMPDIR` plus declared `settings[].envVar`; CLI `gemini extensions install|uninstall|list|update|enable|disable|link|new|validate|config` (`install` takes `--ref`, `--auto-update`, `--pre-release`, `--consent`, `--skip-settings`); session flags `-e/--extensions` (`-e none` disables all), `-l/--list-extensions`. Subagents: `.gemini/agents/*.md` (project), `~/.gemini/agents/*.md` (user), `<ext>/agents/*.md`; markdown + YAML frontmatter, body is the system prompt; fields `name` (required slug, becomes the tool name), `description` (required), `kind` (`local`|`remote`), `tools` (wildcards `*`, `mcp_*`, `mcp_<server>_*`; omitted means inherit everything), `mcpServers`, `model` (default `inherit`), `temperature` (0–2, default 1), `max_turns` (30), `timeout_mins` (10); subagents cannot call subagents; settings `agents.overrides`. Instruction files: `GEMINI.md` — (1) global `~/.gemini/GEMINI.md`; (2) workspace directories and their parents, stopping at the first directory containing a `context.memoryBoundaryMarkers` entry (default `[".git"]`); (3) just-in-time — when a tool touches a file, `GEMINI.md` in that directory and its ancestors up to the trusted root; all hits concatenated; custom filename via `context.fileName` (string or array); **`AGENTS.md` is not read by default** — add it to `context.fileName` (agent skills do natively honour `.agents/skills/`); imports `@./file.md`, `@../file.md`, `@/abs/path.md`, cycle-detected, max depth 5, ignored inside code blocks; `/memory show`, `/memory reload`; system prompt override `GEMINI_SYSTEM_MD` (`true`/`1` → `./.gemini/system.md`, or a path), `GEMINI_WRITE_SYSTEM_MD` dumps the built-in prompt.

**Permissions.** Approval modes `--approval-mode default|auto_edit|yolo|plan`; `-y/--yolo` is the deprecated alias for `yolo` and is mutually exclusive with `--approval-mode`; `general.defaultApprovalMode` cannot be set to `yolo` (flag only); kill switches `security.disableYoloMode`, `admin.secureModeEnabled`, `security.disableAlwaysAllow`. Policy engine (replaces deprecated `--allowed-tools` and `tools.exclude`), TOML, every `.toml` in a policy directory is loaded — tiers Default base 1 (bundled `packages/core/src/policy/policies/*.toml`), Extension 2 (`<ext>/policies/*.toml`), Workspace 3 (`$WORKSPACE/.gemini/policies/*.toml`, **currently non-functional, issue #18186**), User 4 (`~/.gemini/policies/*.toml`), Admin 5 (`/etc/gemini-cli/policies`, `/Library/Application Support/GeminiCli/policies`, `C:\ProgramData\gemini-cli\policies`); `final_priority = tier_base + toml_priority/1000` (toml priority 0–999, highest wins). `[[rule]]` fields `toolName` (string or array; wildcards `*`, `mcp_server_*`, `mcp_*_toolName`, `mcp_*`), `subagent`, `mcpName`, `toolAnnotations` (e.g. `{readOnlyHint = true}`), `argsPattern` (regex over stable-JSON args), `commandPrefix` / `commandRegex` (mutually exclusive), `decision` (`allow|deny|ask_user`), `priority`, `denyMessage`, `modes` (subset of `default|autoEdit|yolo|plan`), `interactive`, `allowRedirection`; also `[[safety_checker]]` (`mcpName`, `toolName`, `priority`, `[safety_checker.checker]{type="in-process",name,required_context}`). CLI `--policy` and `--admin-policy` (comma-separated or repeatable); supplemental admin paths are ignored entirely if any `.toml` exists in the standard system directory, and that directory must be root-owned and not group/other-writable on POSIX or it is skipped wholesale; extension policies have their `allow` decisions and `yolo` modes stripped. Auto-approve everything non-interactively: `gemini -p "…" --approval-mode yolo --skip-trust` (bundled `yolo.toml` is `toolName="*"`, `decision="allow"`, `priority=998`, `modes=["yolo"]`, `allowRedirection=true`); caveats — `non-interactive.toml` hard-denies `ask_user` at priority 999 when `interactive=false`, and `config.ts` force-removes `ask_user` from the registry in headless and ACP mode so no rule can restore it, and in headless mode any `ask_user` decision becomes `deny`. Equivalent without yolo: a user-tier `~/.gemini/policies/auto.toml` with `toolName="*"`, `decision="allow"`, `priority=999`, `allowRedirection=true`. Tool gating settings `tools.core` (built-in allowlist), `tools.allowed` (skip confirmation, e.g. `"run_shell_command(git)"`), `tools.confirmationRequired` (wins over allowed/core), `tools.exclude` (deprecated), `tools.discoveryCommand`, `tools.callCommand`; built-in tool names `run_shell_command`, `glob`, `grep_search` (legacy `search_file_content`), `list_directory`, `read_file`, `read_many_files`, `replace`, `write_file`, `ask_user`, `write_todos`, `activate_skill`, `get_internal_docs`, `enter_plan_mode`, `exit_plan_mode`, `google_web_search`, `web_fetch`, `list_mcp_resources`, `read_mcp_resource`, `complete_task`, `update_topic`, `tracker_*`. Sandbox: enable via `-s/--sandbox` > `GEMINI_SANDBOX` > `tools.sandbox`; `GEMINI_SANDBOX` values `true|false|docker|podman|sandbox-exec|runsc|lxc` (plus a custom command string; `tools.sandbox` also names `windows-native`); default image `ghcr.io/google/gemini-cli:latest`, override with `GEMINI_SANDBOX_IMAGE` or `{"tools":{"sandbox":{"command":"docker","image":"…"}}}`; local build from `.gemini/sandbox.Dockerfile` with `BUILD_SANDBOX=1` (only when running from source); macOS Seatbelt via `SEATBELT_PROFILE` (`permissive-open` default, `permissive-proxied`, `restrictive-open`, `restrictive-proxied`, `strict-open`, `strict-proxied`, or a name resolving to `.gemini/sandbox-macos-<name>.sb`); extras `SANDBOX_MOUNTS` (`from:to:opts`, opts default `ro`, `from` must be absolute), `SANDBOX_FLAGS`, `SANDBOX_SET_UID_GID`; the cwd is bind-mounted at the same absolute path inside the container; related settings `tools.sandboxAllowedPaths`, `tools.sandboxNetworkAccess`, `security.toolSandboxing`. Folder trust: `security.folderTrust.enabled` (default `true`), decisions persist in `~/.gemini/trustedFolders.json` (`{"/abs/path":"TRUST_FOLDER"}`), relocatable with `GEMINI_CLI_TRUSTED_FOLDERS_PATH`; pre-answer with `--skip-trust` (per session), `GEMINI_CLI_TRUST_WORKSPACE=true`, `security.folderTrust.enabled: false`, or by writing `trustedFolders.json`.

**Environment.** Settings precedence low → high: hardcoded defaults → system defaults file → user file → project file → system overrides file → environment variables → CLI arguments. System defaults `/etc/gemini-cli/system-defaults.json` (Linux), `C:\ProgramData\gemini-cli\system-defaults.json`, `/Library/Application Support/GeminiCli/system-defaults.json`, path override `GEMINI_CLI_SYSTEM_DEFAULTS_PATH`; user `~/.gemini/settings.json`; project `<root>/.gemini/settings.json`; system overrides `/etc/gemini-cli/settings.json` (etc.) beat every other file, path override `GEMINI_CLI_SYSTEM_SETTINGS_PATH`. Schema `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/main/schemas/settings.schema.json`; values support `$VAR`, `${VAR}`, `${VAR:-default}`. Setting groups a driver touches: `tools.*`, `mcp.*`, `mcpServers.*`, `security.*` (incl. `security.auth.selectedType`, `security.folderTrust.enabled`, `security.environmentVariableRedaction.*`), `general.*` (incl. `sessionRetention`, `defaultApprovalMode`, `enableAutoUpdate`), `model.*`, `modelConfigs`, `context.*` (incl. `context.fileName`, `context.memoryBoundaryMarkers`, `context.fileFiltering.*`), `contextManagement.*`, `telemetry.*`, `privacy.usageStatisticsEnabled`, `skills.*`, `hooks`, `hooksConfig.*`, `advanced.*`, `experimental.*`, `output.format`, top-level `policyPaths`, `adminPolicyPaths`, `admin.*`, `agents.*`, `ui.*`, `ide.enabled`, `billing.*`. `.env` loading: cwd `.env`, then upward until a `.git` root or the home directory, then `~/.env`; `DEBUG` and `DEBUG_MODE` are excluded from project `.env` files (`advanced.excludedEnvVars`); `.gemini/.env` is never filtered; `advanced.ignoreLocalEnv` disables generic `.env` files. Key env vars `GEMINI_API_KEY`, `GEMINI_MODEL`, `GOOGLE_API_KEY`, `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT`, `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS`, `GOOGLE_GENAI_API_VERSION`, `GOOGLE_GEMINI_BASE_URL`, `GOOGLE_VERTEX_BASE_URL`, `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`, `GEMINI_CLI_SURFACE`, `GEMINI_CLI_TRUST_WORKSPACE`, `GEMINI_CLI_TRUSTED_FOLDERS_PATH`, `GEMINI_CLI_SYSTEM_SETTINGS_PATH`, `GEMINI_CLI_SYSTEM_DEFAULTS_PATH`, `GEMINI_CLI_IDE_PID`, `GEMINI_SANDBOX`, `GEMINI_SANDBOX_IMAGE`, `SEATBELT_PROFILE`, `SANDBOX_MOUNTS`, `SANDBOX_FLAGS`, `SANDBOX_SET_UID_GID`, `GEMINI_SYSTEM_MD`, `GEMINI_WRITE_SYSTEM_MD`, plus the `GEMINI_TELEMETRY_*` family. Auth `security.auth.selectedType` ∈ `oauth-personal`, `gemini-api-key`, `vertex-ai`, `cloud-shell`, `compute-default-credentials`, `gateway`; also `security.auth.enforcedType`, `security.auth.useExternal`. Disabling nonessential traffic: `privacy.usageStatisticsEnabled: false`, `telemetry.enabled: false`, `general.enableAutoUpdate: false`. Model flag `-m/--model` (default alias `auto`), `GEMINI_MODEL`, settings `model.name`, `modelConfigs`, `model.maxSessionTurns`, `model.compressionThreshold`. Per-action home isolation: `GEMINI_CLI_HOME` — it sets the *parent* directory and the CLI creates `.gemini` inside it.

**Observability.** OTel settings ↔ env pairs: `telemetry.enabled` ↔ `GEMINI_TELEMETRY_ENABLED`; `.traces` ↔ `GEMINI_TELEMETRY_TRACES_ENABLED`; `.target` (`local|gcp`, default `local`) ↔ `GEMINI_TELEMETRY_TARGET`; `.otlpEndpoint` (default `http://localhost:4317`) ↔ `GEMINI_TELEMETRY_OTLP_ENDPOINT`; `.otlpProtocol` (`grpc|http`) ↔ `GEMINI_TELEMETRY_OTLP_PROTOCOL`; `.logPrompts` (default true) ↔ `GEMINI_TELEMETRY_LOG_PROMPTS`; `.outfile` ↔ `GEMINI_TELEMETRY_OUTFILE`; `.useCollector` ↔ `GEMINI_TELEMETRY_USE_COLLECTOR`; `.useCliAuth` ↔ `GEMINI_TELEMETRY_USE_CLI_AUTH`; plus `OTLP_GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT`. These are `GEMINI_TELEMETRY_*`, not `OTEL_*`. Events: `gemini_cli.config`, `.user_prompt`, `.tool_call`, `.api_request`, `.api_response`, `.api_error`, `.slash_command`, `.model_routing`, `.chat_compression`, `.conversation_finished`, `.hook_call`, `.extension_install/_uninstall/_enable/_disable`, `.agent.start`, `.agent.finish`, `.ide_connection`, `.startup_stats`, `gen_ai.client.inference.operation.details`. Metrics: `gemini_cli.session.count`, `.tool.call.count`, `.tool.call.latency`, `.api.request.count`, `.api.request.latency`, `.token.usage`, `.file.operation.count`, `.lines.changed`, `.agent.run.count`, `.agent.duration`, `.agent.turns`, `.startup.duration`, `.memory.usage`, `.cpu.usage`, `.tool.queue.depth`, plus `gen_ai.client.token.usage`, `gen_ai.client.operation.duration`. Span attributes `gen_ai.operation.name` (`tool_call|llm_call|user_prompt|system_prompt|agent_call|schedule_tool_calls`), `gen_ai.agent.name = gemini-cli`, `gen_ai.conversation.id` = session id, `gen_ai.tool.name`, `gen_ai.tool.call_id`, `gen_ai.usage.input_tokens`/`output_tokens`; detailed attributes require `telemetry.traces = true`. Cost: no USD figure, only token counts in `stats`. Exit codes (documented, trustworthy): `0` success, `1` general error or API failure, `42` input error (invalid prompt or arguments), `53` turn limit exceeded. Debug `-d/--debug` (F12 debug console in the TUI), `general.debugKeystrokeLogging`.

**Gotchas.**
- Folder trust is the first blocker in a fresh checkout: pass `--skip-trust`, set `GEMINI_CLI_TRUST_WORKSPACE=true`, or pre-write `~/.gemini/trustedFolders.json`.
- `ask_user` is force-removed from the registry in headless and ACP mode and hard-denied at policy priority 999 — a run that expects to ask the user simply fails.
- Workspace-tier policy files (`$WORKSPACE/.gemini/policies/*.toml`) are currently non-functional (issue #18186); put driver policies in the user or admin tier.
- `--consent` is required to install skills and extensions without an interactive confirmation.
- Hook stdout must be pure JSON; one stray `print` silently degrades to allow.
- Never use `_` in an MCP server alias — policy rules against that server fail silently.
- `stream-json`'s `result` event carries no answer text; accumulate assistant `message` deltas instead.
- Extensions do not inherit the shell environment; declare what they need in `settings[].envVar`.
- Mutually exclusive flags are rejected at parse time (`--resume`/`--session-id`/`--session-file`; positional query + `-p`; `-p` + `-i`; `-y` + `--approval-mode`).
- `--include-directories` is capped at 5 entries.
- Doc contradictions: `--acp` vs `--experimental-acp`; `--policy`/`--admin-policy` missing from the CLI reference table; `--yolo` deprecation noted on one page only; `output.format` accepting `stream-json` only as a flag; `--worktree`/`--session-id`/`--session-file`/`--raw-output`/`--skip-trust` missing from the configuration page's CLI list.

**Driver notes.**
- Window mode: PTY-spawn `gemini`, or `gemini -i "<prompt>"` to run a first prompt and stay interactive; pre-answer folder trust before launch or the first frame is a prompt.
- Session mode: one process per turn with `gemini -p "…" -o stream-json --session-id <uuid>` (driver-chosen UUID) and `-r/--resume <uuid>` on later turns; `--session-id`, `--resume` and `--session-file` are mutually exclusive, so pick one scheme.
- Batch mode: `gemini -p "…" -o json --approval-mode yolo --skip-trust`; take `response` from the `json` payload, and use exit `0/1/42/53` directly since the codes are documented.
- Assemble the final answer in `stream-json` from `message` events with `role:"assistant"`, `delta:true` — the terminal `result` has no answer text.
- Isolate per-action config with `GEMINI_CLI_HOME` (parent dir; `.gemini` is created inside) plus `GEMINI_CLI_TRUSTED_FOLDERS_PATH`; put auto-approve policy in the user tier, not the workspace tier.
- Most stable surface: `--acp` (JSON-RPC 2.0 over stdio, client-proxied filesystem, `loadSession`, `cancel`, `setSessionMode`) for stateful driving; `-p -o stream-json` for one-shot runs. There is no SDK and no MCP server mode.

**Sources.** https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli · https://raw.githubusercontent.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/main/docs/cli/headless.md · https://raw.githubusercontent.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/main/docs/cli/acp-mode.md · https://raw.githubusercontent.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/main/docs/hooks/reference.md · https://raw.githubusercontent.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/main/docs/reference/policy-engine.md · https://raw.githubusercontent.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/main/schemas/settings.schema.json

---

### Cursor CLI / Cursor Agent (Anysphere, proprietary, v2026.09.02-c22c1a3, `curl https://cursor.com/install -fsS | bash`)

**Launch.** TUI: `agent` (alias `cursor-agent`; both symlinks in `~/.local/bin`), `agent "prompt"`; Shift+Tab cycles Agent/Plan/Ask. Headless: `-p`/`--print`, must add `--force`/`--yolo` to actually apply edits. Stdin: prompt may be piped (`echo … | agent -p`); no structured stdin protocol. SDK/server/ACP: `agent acp` (hidden, "Start ACP server mode"); TS `@cursor/sdk` v1.0.31 (Node ≥22.13, `Agent.create({model,api_key,local:{cwd}})`, `agent.send`, `Agent.prompt`, `TurnEndedUpdate`), Python `cursor-sdk` (≥3.10); `agent worker start` cloud worker (`--auth-token-file`, `--management-addr` exposing `/healthz` `/readyz` `/metrics`, `--pool`, `--computer-use`).

**Input and follow-up.** Positional argv or stdin; no `--prompt-file`. Files/images referenced by path in prompt text. One turn per `-p` process; follow up with `agent -p --resume <chatId> "…"` or `agent --continue` (= `--resume=-1`). `agent create-chat` pre-allocates a chat ID before the first turn.

**Output.** `--output-format text|json|stream-json` (default `text`), `--stream-partial-output`. `json`: `{"type":"result","subtype":"success","is_error":false,"duration_ms","duration_api_ms","result","session_id","request_id?"}`. stream-json NDJSON: `system`/`init` (`apiKeySource` env|flag|login, `cwd`, `session_id`, `model`, `permissionMode`); `user` (`message.role`, `message.content[]`); `assistant` (`message.content[]`, optional `timestamp_ms`, `model_call_id`); `tool_call`/`started` (`call_id`, `tool_call.<kind>ToolCall.args`); `tool_call`/`completed` (+`result`); `result`/`success`. Tool payloads keyed by kind: `readToolCall`, `writeToolCall` (`result.success.totalLines`, `.linesCreated`, `.fileSize`). Final answer: `result.result`; whole stdout in text mode. Also `agent status --format json`, `agent about --format json`, `agent worker debug --json`.

**Sessions.** Chats/threads. `--resume [chatId]`, `--continue`; `agent resume`, `agent ls`, `agent create-chat`, `/resume`. `session_id` in stream-json, `/copy-conversation-id`. Transcripts under `~/.cursor/chats/` (some builds `~/.config/cursor/chats/`), a per-session SQLite-ish DB, schema undocumented; hook payloads expose `transcript_path` (null when disabled) and `agent_transcript_path` for subagents. CLI/IDE/ACP sessions are not one unified store.
