**Hooks.** Events — agent: `sessionStart`, `sessionEnd`, `preToolUse`, `postToolUse`, `postToolUseFailure`, `subagentStart`, `subagentStop`, `beforeShellExecution`, `afterShellExecution`, `beforeMCPExecution`, `afterMCPExecution`, `beforeReadFile`, `afterFileEdit`, `beforeSubmitPrompt`, `preCompact`, `stop`, `afterAgentResponse`, `afterAgentThought`; Tab: `beforeTabFileRead`, `afterTabFileEdit`; app: `workspaceOpen`. Config `hooks.json`, `{"version":1,"hooks":{"<event>":[entry]}}`; precedence enterprise MDM (`/Library/Application Support/Cursor/hooks.json`, `/etc/cursor/hooks.json`, `C:\ProgramData\Cursor\hooks.json`) → team → project `.cursor/hooks.json` → user `~/.cursor/hooks.json`; all matching hooks run; hot-reloaded; project hooks cwd = project root, user hooks cwd = `~/.cursor/`. Claude Code hooks also loadable. Entry: `command`, `type` (`command` default | `prompt`), `timeout` (s), `loop_limit` (default 5 Cursor / null Claude), `failClosed` (default false), `matcher`. Types: command (JSON stdin → JSON stdout) and prompt (LLM-evaluated, returns `{ok, reason?}`, `$ARGUMENTS` substitution); no HTTP; cloud agents run command hooks only. Payload casing: snake_case (`hook_event_name`, `conversation_id`, `generation_id`, `model`, `model_id`, `model_params[]`, `cursor_version`, `workspace_roots[]`, `user_email`, `transcript_path`). Decisions: `preToolUse` → `{"permission":"allow"|"deny","user_message","agent_message","updated_input"}`; `beforeShellExecution`/`beforeMCPExecution`/`beforeReadFile`/`subagentStart` → `permission` allow|deny|ask; `beforeSubmitPrompt` → `{"continue":bool}`; `postToolUse` → `{"updated_mcp_tool_output","additional_context"}`; `stop`/`subagentStop` → `{"followup_message"}` (auto-submits, loop cap 5); `sessionStart` → `{"env":{K:V},"additional_context"}` (env propagates to all later hooks); `workspaceOpen` → `{"pluginPaths":[…]}`. Exit 0 = use JSON, exit 2 = block (= deny), other non-zero = fail-open unless `failClosed:true`. Timeout per entry in seconds, default "platform default". Matchers (regex): tool type `Shell|Read|Write|Grep|Delete|Task|MCP:<tool_name>`; subagent type `generalPurpose|explore|shell`; full command string; `TabRead|Read`; `TabWrite|Write`; `UserPromptSubmit`; `Stop`; `AgentResponse`; `AgentThought`. Hook env: `CURSOR_PROJECT_DIR`, `CURSOR_VERSION`, `CURSOR_USER_EMAIL`, `CURSOR_TRANSCRIPT_PATH`, `CURSOR_CODE_REMOTE`, `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`.

**MCP.** `<project>/.cursor/mcp.json` and `~/.cursor/mcp.json` (parent dirs walked). stdio / SSE / Streamable HTTP. Supports Tools, Prompts, Resources, Roots, Elicitation, MCP Apps. Interpolation `${workspaceFolder}`, `${workspaceFolderBasename}`, `${pathSeparator}`/`${/}`. OAuth automatic DCR, static creds supported, fixed redirect `https://www.cursor.com/agents/mcp/oauth/callback`; `agent mcp login <id>`. Flags: `--approve-mcps` (essential headless); `agent mcp list|list-tools|login|enable|disable`. Scopes: project / global / team marketplace. Filtering: `Mcp(server:tool)` tokens (`Mcp(datadog:*)`, `Mcp(*:search)`, `Mcp(*:*)`) plus `agent mcp disable`. Acts as MCP server: no — exposes ACP instead ("For custom clients communicating with Cursor CLI directly, use ACP").

**Extensibility.** Rules `.cursor/rules/*.mdc` (frontmatter `description`, `globs`, `alwaysApply`; plain `.md` in that dir is ignored), user rules, team rules, `AGENTS.md`; CLI also reads `AGENTS.md` and `CLAUDE.md` at project root. Skills: `.cursor/skills/**/SKILL.md`, `/` menu + Enter to attach or Alt/Option+Enter as a Custom Mode. Subagents `generalPurpose`, `explore`, `shell` via `Task`. Plugins: marketplace, `--plugin-dir <path>` (repeatable), `/plugin`, `workspaceOpen` → `pluginPaths`; `${CURSOR_PLUGIN_ROOT}` expansion. Slash commands: `/model`, `/resume`, `/summarize` (`/compress`), `/rewind`, `/vim`, `/line-numbers`, `/show-thinking`, `/status-indicators`, `/shell` (`/sh`, `/run`), `/about`, `/setup-terminal`, `/help`, `/feedback`, `/open`, `/copy-request-id`, `/copy-conversation-id`, `/logout`, `/quit`, `/exit`, `/mcp`, `/plugin`, `/config`, `/copy`, `/sandbox`, `/bedrock`. `agent generate-rule`.

**Permissions.** `approvalMode` = `allowlist`|`auto-review`|`unrestricted`. Tokens in `permissions.allow`/`deny` (deny wins): `Shell(base)` / `Shell(cmd:args)`, `Read(glob)`, `Write(glob)`, `WebFetch(domain)`, `Mcp(server:tool)`; global `~/.cursor/cli-config.json`, project `<project>/.cursor/cli.json` (permissions only). Auto-approve: `-f`/`--force`/`--yolo`, `--approve-mcps`. Sandbox: `--sandbox enabled|disabled`, `sandbox.mode`, `sandbox.networkAccess`, `/sandbox`, `agent sandbox enable|disable|reset|run` with `--allow-paths`, `--readonly-paths`, `--blocked-patterns`, `--network` (default false), `--sb-debug`. Trust: `--trust` ("headless mode only") plus `--workspace <path>`. Isolation `-w`/`--worktree [name]` under `~/.cursor/worktrees/<repo>/<name>`, `--worktree-base`, `--skip-worktree-setup`.

**Environment.** `CURSOR_API_KEY` (or `--api-key`), `CURSOR_CONFIG_DIR`, `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`, `HTTP_PROXY`/`HTTPS_PROXY`, `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1`, `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`. Settings: global `~/.cursor/cli-config.json` (`version:1`; keys `channel`, `model`, `maxMode`, `approvalMode`, `sandbox.*`, `display.*`, `network.useHttp1ForAgent`, `attribution.*`) ← project `.cursor/cli.json` (permissions only); pure JSON, no comments; corrupt files renamed `*.bad`. Model: `--model`, `--list-models`/`agent models`, `/model`, `model` key; modes `--mode plan|ask`, `--plan`. Headers `-H, --header`. Config-home isolation: `CURSOR_CONFIG_DIR` (or `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`).

**Observability.** No OTel for the CLI; worker mode exposes Prometheus `/metrics` + `/healthz` `/readyz`. Logs: only `agent sandbox run --sb-debug` (path printed) and `agent worker start --verbose`; no `--log-file`/`--log-level`. Usage: no token/cost fields; `result` carries `duration_ms`/`duration_api_ms` only. Exit codes: `0` on success is the documented contract (official script branches on `$? -eq 0`); no non-zero table.

**Gotchas.**
- Two binary names (`agent`, `cursor-agent`); `~/.local/bin` may not be on PATH.
- `-p` without `--force`/`--yolo` only proposes edits — the classic "it did nothing".
- Workspace trust prompt blocks headless; pass `--trust` + `--workspace`.
- MCP approval prompt blocks headless; pass `--approve-mcps` or pre-`agent mcp enable`.
- With `--stream-partial-output`, assistant lines carrying `model_call_id` are buffered re-flushes; filter on `timestamp_ms && !model_call_id` or text doubles.
- Auto-update on launch; pin by invoking `~/.local/share/cursor-agent/versions/<ver>/cursor-agent` or setting `channel`.
- Zscaler-style proxies need `network.useHttp1ForAgent: true` (HTTP/2 bidi streaming otherwise required).
- Shift+Enter newline depends on terminal (tmux needs Ctrl+J); terminal size requirements undocumented.
- `agent ls` does not see IDE chats.

**Driver notes.**
- Window mode: PTY-drive `agent`; expect no readiness marker, mode cycling via Shift+Tab, and terminal-dependent Shift+Enter — prefer `-p` wherever possible.
- Session mode: one `agent -p --force --output-format stream-json --resume <chatId>` per turn; pre-allocate the id with `agent create-chat` so the driver owns it before turn 1.
- Batch mode: always add `--trust --workspace <path> --approve-mcps --force`; parse NDJSON and take `result.result`; treat exit 0 as the only success signal.
- Delta handling is mandatory if `--stream-partial-output` is on (filter `model_call_id` lines).
- Most stable surface: `--output-format stream-json` today; `agent acp` if bidirectional control is needed (hidden but documented).
- Pin the binary path to a version directory; auto-update otherwise shifts behavior mid-fleet.

**Sources.**
- https://cursor.com/docs/cli/reference/parameters
- https://cursor.com/docs/cli/reference/output-format
- https://cursor.com/docs/cli/headless
- https://cursor.com/docs/hooks
- https://cursor.com/docs/cli/mcp
- https://cursor.com/docs/cli/acp

### GitHub Copilot CLI (GitHub/Microsoft, proprietary "SEE LICENSE IN LICENSE.md", v1.0.83, `npm install -g @github/copilot`)

**Launch.** TUI: `copilot` (tabs, sessions sidebar, timeline, diff mode; Shift+Tab cycles standard/plan/autopilot). Headless: `-p PROMPT` / `--prompt=PROMPT`, plus `-s`/`--silent` to print only the agent response. Stdin: `echo "prompt" | copilot` supported. SDK/server/ACP: `copilot --acp` (+`--stdio` default or `--port N`, mutually exclusive, NDJSON both ways; server-level `--available-tools`, `--excluded-tools`, `--effort` apply to every session of every client); SDK `@github/copilot-sdk` (npm, MIT, v1.0.13, JSON-RPC control; programmatic hook `modifiedPrompt` on `userPromptSubmitted` is SDK-only). Interactive-with-seed: `-i PROMPT` / `--interactive=PROMPT`. Alternate installs: `curl -fsSL https://gh.io/copilot-install | bash`, `brew install copilot-cli`, `winget install GitHub.Copilot`.

**Input and follow-up.** argv (`-p`, `-i`), stdin pipe, `--attachment PATH` (repeatable). One turn per `-p`; exit summary prints a `copilot --resume=SESSION-ID` hint. Chain with `--resume=<id>` / `--continue` / `--session-id <uuid>`. Interactive extras: `@FILENAME`, `#NUMBER` (issue/PR), `!COMMAND`, `$` shell, `Ctrl+G` edit in `$EDITOR`.

**Output.** `--output-format=text|json` — json "outputs JSONL: one JSON object per line"; `-s`/`--silent`; `--stream=on|off`. Event schema for `--output-format=json` is not published. The de-facto richer stream is `~/.copilot/session-state/<session-id>/events.jsonl`, an append-only per-session log (20+ event types) written for every session with zero setup — it powers `--resume` but is **not** a public API (github/copilot-cli#3551). Final answer: with `-p -s` the response is the whole stdout; in json mode the last JSONL object carries the completion. Transcript export `--share=PATH` (default `./copilot-session-<ID>.md`), `--share-gist`. Also `copilot plugins list --json`.

**Sessions.** UUID ids, `--name`/`-n`. `-r`/`--resume[=VALUE]` (id, id prefix, or name; bare form opens a picker and requires a TTY), `--continue` (most recent in cwd, else globally most recent), `--session-id ID` (exact; creates new only for a valid UUID; must not combine with `--resume`/`--continue`/`--connect`), `--connect[=SESSION-ID]`. `--resume` and `--continue` conflict with each other and with `-w/--worktree`. Storage `~/.copilot/session-state/<id>/events.jsonl` plus artifacts; index `~/.copilot/session-store.db` (SQLite, rebuild with `/chronicle reindex`); command history `~/.copilot/command-history-state/`. Listing: `/resume` picker, sessions sidebar, `/chronicle`; no `copilot sessions --json` — enumerate `~/.copilot/session-state/`. Remote sync with `remote:"on"` (`--remote`, `--no-remote`, `--remote-export`). Crash recovery offers to restore a session whose process disappeared.

**Hooks.** Events: `sessionStart`, `sessionEnd`, `userPromptSubmitted`, `userPromptTransformed`, `preToolUse`, `postToolUse`, `postToolUseFailure`, `permissionRequest` (CLI only), `agentStop`, `subagentStart`, `subagentStop`, `preCompact`, `notification` (CLI only), `errorOccurred`. Locations, all loaded and combined (policy → user → project → plugins): policy `/etc/github-copilot/policy.d/*.json`, `C:\ProgramData\GitHub\Copilot\policy.d\*.json`, registry `HKLM\Software\Policies\GitHub\Copilot` (subkey `Policy` REG_SZ JSON; POSIX files must be root-owned, not group/world-writable); repo `.github/hooks/*.json`; user `~/.copilot/hooks/*.json`; inline `hooks` key in `.github/copilot/settings.json`, `.github/copilot/settings.local.json`, `~/.copilot/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.local.json`; plugin `hooks.json` or `hooks/hooks.json`. Shape `{"version":1,"disableAllHooks":false,"hooks":{"<event>":[entry]}}`; a malformed item in a directory-loaded file is dropped, structural errors reject the file, inline settings hooks are strict. Types: **command** `{"type":"command","bash","powershell","command","cwd","env","timeoutSec":30}` or CLI-only `{"type":"command","exec":"prog","args":[…]}` (no shell); **http** `{"type":"http","url","headers","allowedEnvVars",...}` — https only, `http://localhost|127.*|[::1]` needs `COPILOT_HOOK_ALLOW_LOCALHOST=1`, `preToolUse`/`permissionRequest` must be https; **prompt** `{"type":"prompt","prompt":"text or /slash"}` — only on `sessionStart`, only for new interactive sessions. Payload casing is selected by event-name spelling: camelCase event → camelCase fields + numeric ms `timestamp`; PascalCase event → VS Code/Claude-compatible snake_case + `hook_event_name` + ISO-8601 `timestamp`. All carry `sessionId`/`session_id`, `timestamp`, `cwd`; most carry `transcriptPath`/`transcript_path`. Decisions: `preToolUse` → `{"permissionDecision":"allow"|"deny"|"ask","permissionDecisionReason","modifiedArgs"}` (reason required on deny); `permissionRequest` → `{"behavior":"allow"|"deny","message","interrupt":true}` (empty output falls through; for `requestSandboxBypass:true` an `allow` never short-circuits the interactive confirmation, only `deny` propagates); `postToolUse` → `{"modifiedResult":{"resultType":"success","textResultForLlm"},"additionalContext"}` (joined by blank line, capped 10 KB); `agentStop`/`subagentStop` → `{"decision":"block"|"allow","reason","modifiedResponse"}` — `block` forces another turn using `reason`, runaway guard ends the turn after 8 consecutive blocks, `stop_hook_active` marks a forced turn, `modifiedResponse` is subagentStop-only, last writer wins, discarded if `block` also returned; `sessionStart`/`notification`/`subagentStart` → `{"additionalContext"}`; `userPromptTransformed` rewrites model-facing content (cannot block). Progress lines `{"type":"progress","message","temporary":true}` are stripped; everything else is concatenated and parsed with a single `JSON.parse`. Exit codes: `0` = parse stdout; `2` = warning by default, but **deny** for `preToolUse` and `permissionRequest` (overriding a stdout `allow`) and `additionalContext` for `postToolUseFailure`; other non-zero = fail-open except `preToolUse` which is fail-closed ("Denied by preToolUse hook (hook errored)"). **Timeouts are always fail-open for every event, including preToolUse and admin policy hooks** (default 30 s, `timeoutSec`, alias `timeout`). Matchers: regex compiled as `^(?:PATTERN)$`, invalid regex → hook skipped; `notification` → `notification_type` (`shell_completed`, `shell_detached_completed`, `agent_completed`, `agent_idle`, `permission_prompt`, `elicitation_dialog`); `permissionRequest`/`preToolUse`/`postToolUse` → `toolName` (`ask_user`, `bash`, `create`, `edit`, `glob`, `grep`, `powershell`, `task`, `view`, `web_fetch`); `preCompact` → `trigger`; `subagentStart` → `agentName`. Kill switch `disableAllHooks: true` (that file only, or top-level in repo/user settings.json); policy hooks unaffected.

**MCP.** User `~/.copilot/mcp-config.json`; project `.mcp.json` or `.github/mcp.json` (project overrides user on name conflict); per-session `--additional-mcp-config=JSON` (or `@path`) which augments and overrides same-named servers. Management: `copilot mcp`, `copilot plugins list --kind mcp`, `copilot plugins enable|disable|remove --mcp <name>`, `/mcp`. Transports: stdio and remote HTTP (a remote GitHub MCP server ships built in); explicit SSE/Streamable-HTTP naming not in the reference. OAuth: yes — tokens in the OS keychain, fallback `~/.copilot/mcp-oauth-config/`, secret placeholders `~/.copilot/mcp-secrets/`. Scopes: user → repository → session; enable/disable settings scope MDM → user → repo → local → env → flags. Filtering: `--allow-tool=`/`--deny-tool=` with kinds `shell`, `write`, `read`, `url`, `memory`, `<MCP-SERVER>` and parenthesized filters (`shell(git:*)`, `write(README.md)`, `url(https://*.github.com)`, `github(create_issue)`); `--available-tools=` (allowlist), `--excluded-tools=`; `--disable-mcp-server`, `--enable-mcp-server`, `--disable-builtin-mcps`, `disabledMcpServers`/`enabledMcpServers`; GitHub subset via `--add-github-mcp-tool`, `--add-github-mcp-toolset`, `--enable-all-github-mcp-tools`; `--allow-all-mcp-server-instructions`. Acts as MCP server: no — exposes ACP and a JSON-RPC SDK instead. Unreachable server: not documented.

**Extensibility.** Instructions: `~/.copilot/copilot-instructions.md`, `~/.copilot/instructions/**/*.instructions.md`, repo `.github/copilot-instructions.md` and `*.instructions.md`, `AGENTS.md`; `copilot init` scaffolds; `--no-custom-instructions` disables `AGENTS.md` and related; dedup by filename minus `.md`/`.agent.md`, lowest (most specific) level wins. Custom agents: `.agent.md` in `~/.copilot/agents/` and `.github/agents/` (project wins), plus org/enterprise remote agents (`customAgents.defaultLocalOnly`); frontmatter `name`, `model`, `tools` (omit or `["*"]` = all, `[]` = none, MCP tools as `server/tool`), inline `mcp-servers`; body max 30,000 chars; `--agent=AGENT`, `/agent`. Built-in subagents `explore`, `task`, `code-review`, `general-purpose`, `research`, `security-review`, `rubber-duck` (last cannot be disabled); `subagents.agents.<name>.{model,effortLevel,contextTier}`, `subagents.disabledSubagents`, `subagents.maxConcurrency` (cap 32), `subagents.maxDepth` (default 6, cap 256), `/subagents`, `/fleet`. Skills `~/.copilot/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` + project skills (project wins), `skillDirectories`, `copilot skill list|add|remove`, `/skills list`, `disabledSkills`. Plugins: `copilot plugin`, `copilot plugins list|enable|disable|remove` (`--plugin` default, `--mcp`, `--skill`), `--plugin-dir=` (repeatable), `enabledPlugins`, `extraKnownMarketplaces` (`source: directory|git|github`, `autoUpdate`); a repo-enabled plugin is scoped to that repo. Extensions in `~/.copilot/extensions/` run as subprocesses against `@github/copilot-sdk` (logs `~/.copilot/logs/extensions/`). LSP `~/.copilot/lsp-config.json`, `/lsp`. Statusline `statusLine: {type:"command", command, padding?, refreshInterval?}` (session JSON on stdin). Slash commands: `/add-dir`, `/agent`, `/allow-all [off|auto|show]`, `/app`, `/ask`, `/autopilot`, `/chronicle`, `/clear`, `/compact`, `/context`, `/copy`, `/cwd` (`/cd`), `/delegate`, `/diagnose`, `/diff`, `/env`, `/exit` `/quit`, `/feedback`, `/fleet`, `/help`, `/ide`, `/keep-alive`, `/limits`, `/lsp`, `/model`, `/permissions`, `/pr`, `/resume`, `/sandbox`, `/session`, `/settings`, `/skills list`, `/statusline`, `/subagents`, `/tasks`, `/theme`, `/worktree`, `/every`, `/after`.

**Permissions.** Modes standard / plan / autopilot (`--mode=interactive|plan|autopilot`, `--plan`, `--autopilot`, `--max-autopilot-continues=N`, `stayInAutopilot`; `--plan --mode autopilot` = plan-then-autopilot, also `COPILOT_PLAN_THEN_AUTOPILOT`). Auto-approve `--allow-all`/`--yolo` (= `--allow-all-tools --allow-all-paths --allow-all-urls`), `--allow-all-tools` (env `COPILOT_ALLOW_ALL`), `--allow-all-paths`, `--allow-all-urls`, `/allow-all`; an admin can suppress all with `permissions.disableBypassPermissionsMode: "disable"` or `"allow-auto-only"`. Fine-grained `--allow-tool`, `--deny-tool`, `--allow-url`, `--deny-url` (deny wins), `--available-tools`, `--excluded-tools`, `--add-dir=PATH` (also loads that dir's `.github/skills` + `.github/agents` as trusted), `--disallow-temp-dir`, `--no-ask-user`, `--secret-env-vars=VAR` (`GITHUB_TOKEN`/`COPILOT_GITHUB_TOKEN` redacted by default). Saved approvals `~/.copilot/permissions-config.json` (resolved `--config-dir` → `COPILOT_HOME` → `~/.copilot`, only the first): `{"locations":{"/abs/repo/root":{"tool_approvals":[{"kind":"commands","commandIdentifiers":["git:*"]}],"allowed_directories":["/abs/other"]}}}`; `kind` ∈ `commands`|`read`|`write`|`mcp` (`serverName`, `toolName`, null = whole server)|`mcp-sampling`|`memory`|`custom-tool`|`extension-management`|`extension-permission-access`; keys are the git root (worktrees resolve to the main repo) or normalized cwd; matching is literal except a trailing `:*`; no deny/ask rules, no default modes, no URL rules — this is how to pre-answer trust prompts. Sandbox `--sandbox`/`--no-sandbox`, `sandbox.enabled` (default false), `/sandbox`, backed by Microsoft eXecution Container: Seatbelt (macOS), Bubblewrap (Linux), ProcessContainer (Windows); keys `sandbox.allowBypass`, `sandbox.auth.git`, `sandbox.auth.gh`, `sandbox.userPolicy.network.allowLocalNetwork`, `sandbox.userPolicy.network.proxy{url,username,password}` (Linux netns-enforced, macOS cooperative via `HTTP_PROXY`/`HTTPS_PROXY`/`ALL_PROXY`, Windows unsupported → command fails), `sandbox.userPolicy.deniedPaths` (rejected on Windows), `sandbox.userPolicy.seatbelt.keychainAccess`, admin-only `sandbox.failIfUnavailable`; MDM sandbox settings are a floor users can only tighten. Isolation `-w`/`--worktree[=NAME]` under `<repo>.worktrees/`, `worktreeBaseRef: "head"|"defaultBranch"`, `-C DIRECTORY`.

**Environment.** `COPILOT_HOME`, `COPILOT_CACHE_HOME`, auth precedence `COPILOT_GITHUB_TOKEN` > `GH_TOKEN` > `GITHUB_TOKEN`, `COPILOT_ALLOW_ALL`, `COPILOT_MODEL`, `COPILOT_PLAN_THEN_AUTOPILOT`, `COPILOT_HOOK_ALLOW_LOCALHOST`, `COPILOT_EDITOR`/`VISUAL`/`EDITOR`, `COPILOT_PROVIDER_*` (BYOK), `HTTP_PROXY`/`HTTPS_PROXY`; `copilot help environment` prints the list. Settings precedence (later wins): defaults → MDM → user `~/.copilot/settings.json` → repo `.github/copilot/settings.json` → local `.github/copilot/settings.local.json` → env → flags; `.claude/settings.json`/`.claude/settings.local.json` read for the cross-tool subset (`companyAnnouncements`, `disableAllHooks`, `enabledPlugins`, `extraKnownMarketplaces`, `hooks`). Repo-level settings restricted to an allowlist with directional merges (union-only `deniedUrls`/`disabledMcpServers`/`disabledSkills`, tighten-only `respectGitignore`, replace for `model`/`effortLevel`/`contextTier` and only in a trusted working directory). JSONC; unknown top-level keys surface in `/settings` → Problems. Model: `--model=MODEL` (`auto` allowed), `COPILOT_MODEL`, `model` key, `/model`; precedence custom-agent definition → `--model` → `COPILOT_MODEL` → settings → default; `--effort`/`--reasoning-effort` ∈ `low|medium|high|xhigh|max`; `--context TIER` ∈ `default|long_context`; repo allowlist `.github/allowed_models.txt` (glob per line plus `fallback:`). Config-home isolation: `COPILOT_HOME` (cache needs `COPILOT_CACHE_HOME` separately).

**Observability.** OTel first-class, GenAI semantic conventions: enabled by `COPILOT_OTEL_ENABLED=true`, or `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT`, or `COPILOT_OTEL_FILE_EXPORTER_PATH`; `COPILOT_OTEL_EXPORTER_TYPE` (`otlp-http`|`file`), `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_PROTOCOL` (`http/json`|`http/protobuf`), `OTEL_SERVICE_NAME` (default `github-copilot`), `OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_HEADERS`, `OTEL_INSTRUMENTATION_GENAI_CAPTURE_MESSAGE_CONTENT` (default false), `OTEL_LOG_LEVEL`, `COPILOT_OTEL_SOURCE_NAME` (default `github.copilot`); traces/metrics cover agent interactions, LLM calls, tool executions, token usage; MDM can push a `telemetry` baseline. Logs `~/.copilot/logs/process-{timestamp}-{pid}.log`, `--log-dir=`, `--log-level=none|error|warning|info|debug|all|default`; `/session` prints log path, session id, duration, cwd; `/diagnose`. Usage: `--usage-output-file`, `--max-ai-credits=CREDITS` (`/limits`), `/context`, status-line items `showContextWindow`, `showQuota`, `showAiUsed`, `showCodeChanges`; non-silent `-p` prints usage stats. Exit codes: no documented table; documented failure — bare `--resume` with multiple sessions and no TTY exits with an error rather than silently starting a new session.

**Gotchas.**
- Bare `--resume` needs a TTY; under `-p`, non-TTY `-i`, or piped stdin it errors. Always `--resume=<ID>` or `--continue`.
- Permissions are the #1 headless blocker: grant `--allow-tool=`/`--allow-all-tools` or pre-seed `permissions-config.json` keyed by the exact git-root path (a key mismatch silently means "not approved"); add `--no-ask-user` or the agent stalls on `ask_user`.
- `permissionRequest` hooks are the clean programmatic CI gate — they fire before the whole permission service and need no TTY.
- preToolUse is fail-closed on crash but fail-open on timeout: a slow hook blocks nothing.
- `--allow-all` can be disabled by policy; MDM sandbox settings are a floor and `--no-sandbox` only warns on stderr under `-p`.
- `COPILOT_HOME` does not move the cache dir (`COPILOT_CACHE_HOME` does).
- Auto-update at session start unless `--no-auto-update` / `autoUpdate:false`; SDK and server sessions never auto-update marketplace plugins.
- `events.jsonl` is not a public API (#3551).
- Prompt hooks never fire under `-p` or on resume.
- In ACP stdio mode stdout is reserved for NDJSON — no logs there; use TCP mode if stdout is needed.
- TUI taming flags: `--alt-screen`, `--no-color`, `--no-mouse`, `--screen-reader`, `--banner`/`--no-banner`, `--streamer-mode`; `Ctrl+C` clears input/exits, `Ctrl+D` shuts down, `Ctrl+Z` suspends.

**Driver notes.**
- Window mode: PTY-drive `copilot` (or `-i PROMPT` to seed); pass `--no-color --no-mouse --no-banner` for a parseable capture; expect no readiness marker.
- Session mode: `copilot -p "…" --resume=<ID> --output-format=json --allow-tool=… --no-ask-user`, one process per turn; capture the id from the exit summary or supply `--session-id <uuid>` yourself.
- Batch mode: pre-seed `~/.copilot/permissions-config.json` under the exact git root, set `COPILOT_HOME` + `COPILOT_CACHE_HOME` for isolation, disable auto-update, and register a `permissionRequest` hook as the policy gate.
- Read `~/.copilot/session-state/<id>/events.jsonl` for the rich event view but version-pin against churn — the `--output-format=json` schema is unpublished.
- Most stable surface: `copilot --acp` (fully specified NDJSON) or the MIT `@github/copilot-sdk`; the `-p` JSONL schema is the least documented of the three.

**Sources.**
- https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-command-reference
- https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-programmatic-reference
- https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/hooks-reference
- https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/acp-server
- https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-config-dir-reference
- https://github.com/github/copilot-cli/issues/3551

### Amp (Sourcegraph, proprietary "SEE LICENSE IN LICENSE.md" / "Amp Commercial License" for the SDK, v@ampcode/cli 0.0.1788883237-g0b98e3, `curl -fsSL https://ampcode.com/install.sh | bash` or `npm install -g @ampcode/cli`)

**Launch.** TUI: `amp` (command palette `Ctrl+O`, `@` fuzzy file mentions, `Ctrl+P`/`Ctrl+N` history, `Ctrl+C` interrupt/exit); a "No-TUI Runner" variant exists. Headless: `-x`/`--execute [message]` — sends, waits for end of turn, prints only the final assistant message, exits; **automatically enabled when stdout is redirected**. Stdin: fully supported and composable (`echo "prompt" | amp -x`, `cat file | amp -x "question"`, `amp < prompt.txt > out.txt`); structured `--stream-json-input` (requires `--execute` and `--stream-json`) reads JSON Lines user messages until stdin closes — the only real multi-turn stdin protocol of these ten. SDK/server/ACP: SDK `@ampcode/sdk` (TS) plus a Python SDK — `execute({prompt,…})` returns an async iterable of `system`/`assistant`/`result`, options include thread continuation, `mode` (`low|medium|high|ultra`), effort (`none`…`max`), `visibility` (`workspace` default, `private`, `unlisted`, `group`), `settingsFile`, `mcpConfig`, `skills`; no ACP. Orbs: `-ox` runs the prompt on Amp's servers and prints the thread URL; `--executor local|orb|runner:<name>`. Node ≥ 22; the plugin API runs on Bun.

**Input and follow-up.** argv after `-x`, stdin pipe, or shell redirection; piped data and an `-x` prompt combine. Multi-turn either within one process (`--stream-json-input`) or across processes (`amp threads continue [thread] -x "next" --stream-json`). Input line: `{"type":"user","steer":false,"message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"…"},{"type":"image","source_path":"file:///…","source":{"type":"base64","media_type":"image/png","data":"…"}}]}}`; `source_path` optional, `media_type` must match the bytes, image blocks are omitted from echoed user messages on stdout. `steer: true` handles a queued message at the next interruption point (mid-turn steering).

**Output.** `--stream-json` (requires `--execute`), `--stream-json-input`, `--stream-json-thinking` (adds `thinking`/`redacted_thinking`, **breaks Claude Code compatibility**); default plain text = final assistant message only. NDJSON, deliberately Claude Code-compatible: `{"type":"system","subtype":"init",cwd,session_id,tools[],mcp_servers[{name,status}],agent_mode?}` with `status` ∈ `awaiting-approval|authenticating|connecting|reconnecting|connected|denied|failed|blocked-by-registry`; `{"type":"system","subtype":"error_max_turns"|"error_during_execution",error,session_id}`; `{"type":"user",message:{role,content:[{type:"text"}|{type:"tool_result",tool_use_id,content,is_error}]},parent_tool_use_id,session_id}`; `{"type":"assistant",message:{content:[text|tool_use{id,name,input}|thinking|redacted_thinking],stop_reason:"end_turn"|"max_tokens"|"stop_sequence"|"tool_use"|"pause_turn"|"refusal"|null,usage?{input_tokens,cache_creation_input_tokens,cache_read_input_tokens,cache_creation{ephemeral_5m_input_tokens,ephemeral_1h_input_tokens},output_tokens,max_tokens,service_tier}},parent_tool_use_id,session_id}`; `{"type":"result","subtype":"success",duration_ms,duration_api_ms?,is_error:false,num_turns,result,session_id,usage?,permission_denials?}` and the `error_during_execution`/`error_max_turns` variants. Final answer: `result.result`. `session_id` **is** the thread ID (`T-<uuid>`), same id used by `amp threads continue`, the ampcode.com URL and the `Amp-Thread-ID:` commit trailer. Also `amp mcp remote … --json`.

**Sessions.** Threads, id `T-<uuid>`. `amp threads new|continue [thread]|fork|list|share|compact`; `amp connect` links CLI to the web UI; `--visibility private|public|workspace|group`, `amp.defaultVisibility` maps repo origins to defaults; `amp.thread.autoArchiveOnQuit` (default false). Storage is **server-side on ampcode.com**, synced across CLI/web/macOS/iOS; export per-thread from the web More Actions menu (Markdown, or full JSON for the creator). No documented local on-disk transcript path.

**Hooks.** No `hooks.json`. Lifecycle interception is the **Plugin API**: TypeScript modules under `.amp/plugins/<name>/index.ts` (workspace) or global plugin dirs importing `@ampcode/plugin`. Events (`PluginEventMap`): `session.start`, `tool.call`, `tool.result`, `agent.start`, `agent.end`; those expecting a response (`PluginRequestResultMap`): `tool.call` → `ToolCallResult`, `tool.result` → `ToolResultResult`, `agent.start` → `AgentStartResult`, `agent.end` → `AgentEndResult`. Registration `amp.on('agent.start', async (event, ctx) => {…})` in the default-exported async setup function. Config shape: a plugin directory (optionally bundling `skills/<name>/SKILL.md` and `mcp.json`); no JSON hook manifest. Hook types: in-process TypeScript/Bun functions only — no shell-command, HTTP, or prompt type; context gives `logger` (attaches to the handler's trace span), `$` (Bun shell), `ui` (`ctx.ui.notify`), `ai` (`ctx.ai.ask` — an LLM call inside a hook), `system`. Payload casing is typed TS, not stdin JSON: `SessionStartEvent {thread:{id}}`; `AgentStartEvent {thread:{id}, message, id}` → `{message?:{content, display?}}` (context injection; `display:true` shows it, default false); `ToolCallEvent` → `{action:'allow'}` | `{action:'reject-and-continue', message}` | `{action:'modify', input}` — the allow/deny/rewrite gate; `ToolResultEvent` → post-tool mutation; `AgentEndEvent {status:'success'|'error'|'cancelled', messages}` → `{action:'continue', userMessage}` forces another turn. Thread handle exposes `cancel()` (during `agent.start`, prevents the turn) and `setVisibility()`. No exit codes. Timeouts: undocumented for handlers; `--plugin-ready-timeout [seconds]` (bare = 10 s, max 300, `0` disables) — without it `-x` can start the turn before plugins load and `agent.start`/`agent.end` are **silently skipped**. Matchers: none — filter inside the handler. Declarative alternatives: `amp.permissions` and `amp.mcpPermissions`.

**MCP.** `amp.mcpServers` in `~/.config/amp/settings.json` (user), `.amp/settings.json` (workspace), or `--mcp-config '<JSON or path>'`; fields `command`/`args`/`env` or `url`/`headers`; `${VAR_NAME}` interpolation. Transports stdio, remote HTTP, SSE. Precedence: CLI `--mcp-config` → workspace → user → skills (a skill can bundle `mcp.json`). CLI: `amp mcp add <name> -- <cmd…>`, `--env K=V`, `amp mcp add <name> <url>`, `amp mcp remove`, `amp mcp doctor`, `amp mcp approve <name>`, `amp tools list|show|make|use`. Remote definitions stored server-side: `amp mcp remote --personal|--workspace <ws>|--project <proj>|--current-project add|update|remove|check|list|tools|login|logout <name> [--json]`, `--auth none|bearer|oauth`, secrets only via `--bearer-token-file` / `--oauth-client-secret-file` (or `-` for stdin, never as CLI args). OAuth: automatic DCR; manual `amp mcp oauth login <name> --server-url … --client-id … --client-secret … --scopes …`, redirect `http://localhost:8976/oauth/callback`, `amp mcp oauth logout`; **browser OAuth is unavailable inside orbs**. Scopes: user / workspace / CLI-flag / skill-bundled plus server-side personal / workspace / project. Filtering: `amp.tools.disable` (`builtin:<name>` disables only the built-in, `*` globs), `amp.mcpPermissions` first-match rules (`{"matches":{"command":"npx","args":"* @playwright/mcp@*"},"action":"allow"}` or `{"matches":{"url":"…"},"action":"allow"|"reject"}`; no match = allow), Enterprise MCP Registry Allowlist (`blocked-by-registry`). Acts as MCP server: no. Workspace-declared servers **require explicit approval** (`amp mcp approve <name>`; `amp mcp doctor` shows "awaiting approval"); servers from global settings or `--mcp-config` need none.

**Extensibility.** `AGENTS.md` with a precise inclusion model: always included are `AGENTS.md` in cwd/workspace roots and all parents up to `$HOME`, `$HOME/.config/amp/AGENTS.md`, `$HOME/.config/AGENTS.md`, `/etc/ampcode/AGENTS.md`, `/Library/Application Support/ampcode/AGENTS.md`, `%ProgramData%\ampcode\AGENTS.md`; subtree files included when the agent reads a file in that subtree; per-directory fallback `AGENTS.md` → `AGENT.md` → `CLAUDE.md`; `@`-mentions pull other files (absolute, `@~/…`, globs like `@doc/*.md`; mentions in code blocks ignored); a mentioned file may declare frontmatter `globs: ['**/*.ts']` to scope itself (globs implicitly prefixed `**/` unless starting `./` or `../`); `agents-md list` in the palette shows what loaded. Skills: `.agents/skills/`, `~/.config/agents/skills/`, `~/.agents/skills/`, `~/.config/amp/skills/`, `amp.skills.path` (colon-separated, `;` on Windows), built-in/personal/workspace, plus `.claude/skills/`, `~/.claude/skills/`, `~/.claude/plugins/cache/` unless `amp.skills.disableClaudeCodeSkills: true`; a skill can bundle `mcp.json`. Plugins as above ("Global Plugins & Skills" covers org-wide distribution). Subagents: the `Task` tool plus `oracle`; modes `low|medium|high|ultra` ("The Dial"), `--fast` (= `--features fast`) for one invocation — execute mode always defaults to Standard. Custom tools `amp tools make` / `amp tools use <name>` (args or JSON on stdin). No documented user-defined slash commands; `Ctrl+O` palette only.

**Permissions.** Interactive prompt: `y` allow once / `n` reject / `!` allow and add to allowlist. Auto-approve `--dangerously-allow-all` or `amp.dangerouslyAllowAll: true`. Rules `amp.permissions`, e.g. `{"tool":"Bash","action":"ask","matches":{"cmd":["git push*","git commit*"]}}` with actions `allow`/`ask`/`reject`; managed by `amp permissions list|test|edit|add`. File guard `amp.guardedFiles.allowlist` takes precedence over the built-in sensitive-file denylist. Tool availability `amp.tools.disable`. MCP gating `amp.mcpPermissions` + workspace approval. Sandbox: **none** — no OS-level sandbox flag; isolation is `--executor orb` / `-ox` or `--executor runner:<name>`. No folder-trust dialog; the trust boundary is workspace MCP approval and whether `.amp/settings.json` is read — pre-answer with `amp mcp approve <name>` or declare servers in user settings / `--mcp-config`. Enterprise floor: `managed-settings.json` overrides user and workspace, extra key `amp.admin.compatibilityDate`.

**Environment.** `AMP_API_KEY` (**must start with `sgamp_`**; the short-lived token written by `amp login` is rejected), `AMP_URL` (default `https://ampcode.com/`), `AMP_LOG_LEVEL`, `AMP_LOG_FILE`, `AMP_SETTINGS_FILE`, `AMP_FORCE_BEL`, `AMP_DISABLE_AMP_THREAD_TRAILER=1`, `AMP_DISABLE_AMP_COAUTHOR_TRAILER=1`, `AMP_SKIP_UPDATE_CHECK=1`, `HTTP_PROXY`, `HTTPS_PROXY`, `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`. Settings precedence: enterprise managed-settings (`/Library/Application Support/ampcode/managed-settings.json`, `/etc/ampcode/managed-settings.json`, `%ProgramData%\ampcode\managed-settings.json`) → workspace `.amp/settings.json(c)` (nearest, up to repo root) → user `~/.config/amp/settings.json(c)` or `--settings-file`/`AMP_SETTINGS_FILE`; JSONC; all keys prefixed `amp.`; `amp config edit [--workspace]`. Model: **no `--model` flag** — pick a mode on The Dial (`low|medium|high|ultra`, SDK `mode`) plus `--fast`; Amp selects models per mode. Other flags `--visibility`, `--notifications`/`--no-notifications`, `--log-level error|warn|info|debug|audit`, `--log-file`, `--jetbrains`/`--no-jetbrains`, `--ide`/`--no-ide`, `--executor`, `--plugin-ready-timeout`, `-V`. Config-home isolation: `AMP_SETTINGS_FILE` / `--settings-file` (config dir itself is `~/.config/amp/`).

**Observability.** OTel: none documented. Logs `--log-level`/`--log-file` (`AMP_LOG_LEVEL`, `AMP_LOG_FILE`); default location undocumented; plugin `logger` output attaches to that handler's trace span events; `amp doctor` builds a support bundle; `amp report-bug "…"` (aliases `amp report`, `amp diagnostics`) sends thread state + client logs + settings to Sourcegraph, deleted after a week. Usage/cost: `usage` (input/output/cache tokens, `service_tier`, `max_tokens`) on `assistant` and terminal `result`; `amp.showCosts`; `permission_denials[]` on `result` — Amp is the only one of Cursor/Copilot/Amp reporting per-run tokens in its stream. Exit codes: not documented; failure is in-band via `{"type":"result","is_error":true,"subtype":"error_during_execution"|"error_max_turns"}` — do not trust `$?`.

**Gotchas.**
- `amp login`'s session token will not work in CI; `AMP_API_KEY` must be a long-lived `sgamp_` access token.
- Redirecting stdout silently switches on execute mode (notifications off, Standard mode, last message only).
- `--stream-json` requires `--execute`; `--stream-json-input` requires both. In input mode the process exits only after the agent finishes *and* stdin closes — holding the pipe open hangs forever, closing it early truncates the conversation.
- `--stream-json-thinking` breaks Claude Code schema compatibility.
- Plugin lifecycle events are skipped under `-x` unless `--plugin-ready-timeout` is passed — a silent no-op.
- Workspace MCP servers show `awaiting-approval` in `system/init` `mcp_servers[]`; check that field before assuming tools exist, or pass servers via `--mcp-config`.
- Execute mode resets to Standard every time; pass `--fast` per invocation.
- Threads live on ampcode.com — no offline/local-only store; visibility matters for anything sensitive.
- Git trailers `Co-authored-by: Amp <amp@ampcode.com>` and `Amp-Thread-ID: <url>` are on by default and leak thread URLs into public history.
- Auto-update default `amp.updates.mode: "auto"`; pin with `"disabled"` + `AMP_SKIP_UPDATE_CHECK=1`.
- Node ≥ 22 required; plugin API additionally needs Bun.

**Driver notes.**
- Window mode: PTY-drive `amp`; be careful never to redirect stdout, which silently converts the run to execute mode.
- Session mode: `amp threads continue <T-uuid> -x "…" --stream-json` per turn, or hold one process open with `--execute --stream-json --stream-json-input` and write one JSON line per turn — close stdin exactly when the conversation ends.
- Batch mode: `AMP_API_KEY=sgamp_… amp -x --stream-json --mcp-config <json>` with `--dangerously-allow-all` or an `amp.permissions` ruleset; ignore `$?` and branch on `result.is_error`.
- Any policy/audit hook must be a Bun/TS plugin under `.amp/plugins/`, and the driver must pass `--plugin-ready-timeout` or the hooks never fire.
- Verify `system/init.mcp_servers[].status` before the first turn; `awaiting-approval` means the tools are absent.
- Most stable surface: `--stream-json` (Claude Code-compatible schema) plus the typed Plugin API for control.

**Sources.**
- https://ampcode.com/docs/cli/execute-mode
- https://ampcode.com/docs/cli/streaming-json
- https://ampcode.com/docs/cli/settings
- https://ampcode.com/docs/plugin-api
- https://ampcode.com/docs/customize/mcp
- https://ampcode.com/docs/customize/agents-md

---

### OpenCode (Anomaly / `anomalyco`, MIT, v1.18.29, `curl -fsSL https://opencode.ai/install | bash` or `npm i -g opencode-ai@latest`)

**Launch.** TUI: `opencode [project]` (default with no subcommand); flags `--continue`/`-c`, `--session`/`-s <id>`, `--fork`, `--prompt`, `--model`/`-m`, `--agent`, `--auto`, `--port`, `--hostname`, `--mdns`, `--mdns-domain`, `--cors`. Headless: `opencode run [message..]` with `--command`, `--continue`/`-c`, `--session`/`-s`, `--fork`, `--share`, `--model`/`-m`, `--agent`, `--file`/`-f`, `--format`, `--title`, `--attach`, `--password`/`-p`, `--username`/`-u`, `--dir`, `--port`, `--variant`, `--thinking`, `--auto` (source also accepts undocumented `--yolo` and `--dangerously-skip-permissions`). Stdin: supported — `run.ts` does `process.stdin.isTTY ? undefined : await Bun.stdin.text()` and merges piped text with the argv message. SDK/server/ACP: `opencode serve` (HTTP + SSE, OpenAPI 3.1 at `GET /doc`, defaults `--port 4096 --hostname 127.0.0.1`, plus `--mdns`, `--mdns-domain` default `opencode.local`, `--cors <origin>` repeatable), `opencode web`, `opencode attach <url>`; `opencode acp` speaks ACP over stdio as newline-delimited JSON-RPC (`--cwd`, `--port`, `--hostname`, `--mdns`, `--cors`; used by Zed, JetBrains, Avante.nvim, CodeCompanion.nvim; `/undo` and `/redo` unsupported over ACP); SDK `@opencode-ai/sdk` (`createOpencode()`, `createOpencodeClient({baseUrl})`; `hostname` 127.0.0.1, `port` 4096, `timeout` 5000 ms, inline `config`); Go SDK page exists, no Python SDK. Runtime Bun; TUI is Go/Bubble Tea driven over the local HTTP server.

**Input and follow-up.** Positional variadic argv, stdin pipe, or `--command <name>` to run a slash command with the message as arguments; attachments `--file`/`-f` (repeatable, images become `data:` URLs). One turn per `run`, state lives in the session: follow up with `opencode run -c "…"` or `-s <sessionID> "…"`; `--fork` with `-c`/`-s` branches. Better multi-turn: keep `opencode serve` and `POST /session/:id/message` repeatedly (or `POST /session/:id/prompt_async`, returns `204`); `opencode run --attach http://localhost:4096 "…"` reuses a warm server so MCP servers do not cold-boot per turn.

**Output.** `--format default|json` on `run`; also `opencode session list --format table|json`, `opencode db --format json|tsv`. `--format json` is NDJSON, envelope `{"type":"<type>","timestamp":1757356800000,"sessionID":"ses_…",…}`. Types: `step_start` (`part`), `text` (`part`), `reasoning` (`part`), `tool_use` (`part` — name, state, input, output inside), `step_finish` (`part` — usage/cost inside), `error` (`error`). `part` is the SDK `Part` union carrying `id`, `sessionID`, `messageID`, `type`. Final answer: the last `text` part before the session goes idle; the run loop terminates on `session.status` with `status.type === "idle"` — **there is no separate result envelope**. Server SSE `GET /event`: first `server.connected`, then bus events `command.executed`, `file.edited`, `file.watcher.updated`, `installation.updated`, `lsp.client.diagnostics`, `lsp.updated`, `message.part.removed`, `message.part.updated`, `message.removed`, `message.updated`, `permission.asked`, `permission.replied`, `server.connected`, `session.created`, `session.compacted`, `session.deleted`, `session.diff`, `session.error`, `session.idle`, `session.status`, `session.updated`, `todo.updated`, `shell.env`, `tool.execute.before`, `tool.execute.after`, `tui.prompt.append`, `tui.command.execute`, `tui.toast.show`; each `{type, properties}`. Message schema `{info: Message, parts: Part[]}`; `POST /session/:id/message` body `{messageID?, model?, agent?, noReply?, system?, tools?, parts}`.

**Sessions.** `--session`/`-s <id>`, `--continue`/`-c`, `--fork` (requires `-c`/`-s`, errors otherwise), `--title`, `--share`. `opencode session list [-n <max>] [--format table|json]`, `opencode session delete <id>`. Export/import `opencode export [sessionID] [--sanitize]` / `opencode import <file|share-url>` (e.g. `https://opncd.ai/s/abc123`). Storage: SQLite `~/.local/share/opencode/opencode.db` (non-stable channels `opencode-<channel>.db`; `OPENCODE_DB` overrides filename or absolute path, `:memory:` accepted); `opencode db path`, `opencode db "<sql>"`; auxiliary per-file JSON under `~/.local/share/opencode/storage/` keyed by project slug (`./global/storage/` outside a repo). HTTP: `POST /session/:id/fork` (`{messageID?}`), `/revert` (`{messageID, partID?}`), `/unrevert`, `/abort`.

**Hooks.** No shell-command hook system — no `hooks` key and no `experimental.hook` key in the published schema (`experimental` contains only `disable_paste_summary`, `batch_tool`, `openTelemetry`, `primary_tools`, `continue_loop_on_deny`, `mcp_timeout`, `policies`); third-party write-ups describing `experimental.hook.file_edited`/`session_completed` are stale. The mechanism is the **JS/TS plugin API**: `.opencode/plugins/*.{js,ts}` (project), `~/.config/opencode/plugins/*` (global), or npm packages in the `plugin` array of `opencode.json` (`"plugin": ["opencode-wakatime", ["@my-org/plugin", {…}]]`, installed with Bun into `~/.cache/opencode/node_modules/`). Load order: global config → project config → global plugin dir → project plugin dir; all hooks of all plugins run in sequence. Signature `(input: PluginInput, options?) => Promise<Hooks>` with `PluginInput = {client, project, directory, worktree, experimental_workspace, serverUrl, $}`. Full hook list: `dispose`, `event` (every bus event), `config`, `tool` (register custom tools, overrides same-named built-ins), `auth`, `provider`, `chat.message`, `chat.params`, `chat.headers`, `permission.ask` (`output.status = "ask"|"deny"|"allow"` — the allow/deny gate), `command.execute.before`, `tool.execute.before` (mutate `output.args`; **throw to block**), `tool.execute.after` (rewrite `title`/`output`/`metadata`), `shell.env`, `tool.definition`, `experimental.chat.messages.transform`, `experimental.chat.system.transform` (context injection via `output.system`), `experimental.session.compacting` (`output.context`), `experimental.compaction.autocontinue`, `experimental.text.complete`, `experimental.provider.small_model`. Timeouts: none — in-process async functions. Matchers: none — filter on `input.tool`, `event.type` inside the hook. Disable all external plugins with `--pure` or `OPENCODE_DISABLE_DEFAULT_PLUGINS=1`. Structured logging `client.app.log({body:{service, level, message, extra}})` (`debug|info|warn|error`, also `POST /log`).

**MCP.** The `mcp` object in `opencode.json` / `~/.config/opencode/opencode.json`. Local: `{"type":"local","command":["npx","-y","pkg"],"cwd"?,"environment"?,"enabled"?,"timeout"?}`; remote: `{"type":"remote","url":"https://…","enabled"?,"headers"?,"oauth"?: McpOAuthConfig|false,"timeout"?}`. Default request timeout 5000 ms; `experimental.mcp_timeout` sets a global default. Transports: stdio (`local`) and remote HTTP; no distinct `sse` type in the schema. OAuth automatic with DCR (RFC 7591); `McpOAuthConfig` keys `clientId`, `clientSecret`, `scope`, `callbackPort` (default 19876), `redirectUri` (default `http://127.0.0.1:19876/mcp/oauth/callback`); `"oauth": false` suppresses auto-detection. CLI `opencode mcp add|list|ls|auth [name]|auth list|logout [name]|debug <name>`. Scopes: remote org config (`.well-known/opencode`) → global → project → managed/MDM, all merged; runtime `POST /mcp` (`{name, config}`), status `GET /mcp`. Filtering: the `tools` glob map globally and per-agent (`{"tools":{"mcpname*":false},"agent":{"my-agent":{"tools":{"mcpname*":true}}}}`; `*` many, `?` one). Acts as MCP server: no (`opencode mcp` manages client-side servers only) — it is an ACP agent instead. Unreachable server behavior: not documented beyond the timeout keys.

**Extensibility.** Commands: markdown in `.opencode/commands/<name>.md` or `~/.config/opencode/commands/<name>.md` (filename = command name) or the `command` key; frontmatter `description`, `agent`, `model`, `subtask`; substitutions `$ARGUMENTS`, `$1`/`$2`/`$3`, shell `` !`cmd` ``, file `@path`; invoke `/name`, `opencode run --command <name> <args>`, or `POST /session/:id/command`. Skills: `SKILL.md` from `.opencode/skills/<name>/`, `~/.config/opencode/skills/`, `.claude/skills/`, `~/.claude/skills/`, `.agents/skills/`, `~/.agents/skills/`; frontmatter `name` (required, `^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$`, 1–64 chars, must equal the directory name), `description` (required, 1–1024), `license`, `compatibility`, `metadata`; loaded on demand through the native `skill` tool. Agents/modes: markdown in `.opencode/agents/*.md` or the `agent` key; keys `description` (required), `mode` (`primary`|`subagent`|`all`), `model`, `temperature`, `steps` (legacy `maxSteps` deprecated), `tools`, `permission`, `prompt` (supports `{file:./prompts/x.txt}`), `disable`; built-ins primary `build`, `plan`, subagents `general`, `explore`, `scout`; `opencode agent create --path --description --mode --permissions --model` is non-interactive with all four. Custom tools `.opencode/tools/` plus the plugin `tool` hook. Instructions: `AGENTS.md` first, `CLAUDE.md` fallback, walking up from cwd; then `~/.config/opencode/AGENTS.md`; then `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`; only the first match per category; extra files via the `instructions` array (globs and https URLs, remote fetch 5 s timeout); `@path` references inside AGENTS.md; Claude-compat off via `OPENCODE_DISABLE_CLAUDE_CODE`, `_PROMPT`, `_SKILLS`.

**Permissions.** The `permission` key resolves each action to `allow`, `ask`, or `deny` — a scalar, a per-tool map, or per-tool pattern objects. Keys: `read`, `edit` (edit/write/patch), `glob`, `grep`, `bash`, `task`, `skill`, `lsp`, `question`, `webfetch`, `websearch`, `external_directory`, `doom_loop`, `*`. Patterns `{"bash":{"*":"ask","git *":"allow","rm *":"deny"}}`, `{"edit":{"*":"deny","src/**.js":"allow"}}`; **last matching rule wins**; `~`/`$HOME` expand. Defaults: most `allow`; `doom_loop` and `external_directory` default `ask`; `*.env` and `*.env.*` denied, `*.env.example` allowed. Auto-approve `--auto` (aliases `--yolo`, `--dangerously-skip-permissions`) approves anything not explicitly denied; `OPENCODE_PERMISSION` accepts inline JSON. Per-agent overrides beat global. Interactive replies `once`, `always`, `reject`; over HTTP `POST /session/:id/permissions/:permissionID` with `{response, remember?}`. Sandbox: none built in (no container/seccomp mode); `external_directory` is the only filesystem-scope control; `experimental.policies` holds provider-access policy statements.

**Environment.** Config files `opencode.json`/`opencode.jsonc` (project root, walking up to the git root), `~/.config/opencode/opencode.json`, `tui.json`; interpolation `{env:VAR}` and `{file:path}`. Precedence (later wins, files merged key-by-key): remote `.well-known/opencode` → global → `OPENCODE_CONFIG` path → project `opencode.json` → `.opencode/` dirs → `OPENCODE_CONFIG_CONTENT` → managed files (`/Library/Application Support/opencode/`) → macOS MDM `.mobileconfig`. Top-level keys: `$schema`, `agent`, `attachment`, `autoshare`, `autoupdate`, `command`, `compaction`, `default_agent`, `disabled_providers`, `enabled_providers`, `enterprise`, `experimental`, `formatter`, `instructions`, `layout`, `logLevel`, `lsp`, `mcp`, `mode`, `model`, `permission`, `plugin`, `provider`, `reference`, `references`, `server`, `share`, `shell`, `skills`, `small_model`, `snapshot`, `subagent_depth`, `tool_output`, `tools`, `username`, `watcher`. Model: `--model provider/model` (`-m`), config `model`/`small_model`, `--variant` for reasoning effort, `opencode models [provider] [--refresh] [--verbose]`. Env: `OPENCODE_CONFIG`, `OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR`, `OPENCODE_CONFIG_CONTENT`, `OPENCODE_TUI_CONFIG`, `OPENCODE_PERMISSION`, `OPENCODE_AUTO_SHARE`, `OPENCODE_SERVER_PASSWORD`, `OPENCODE_SERVER_USERNAME`, `OPENCODE_DISABLE_AUTOUPDATE`, `OPENCODE_DISABLE_PRUNE`, `OPENCODE_DISABLE_TERMINAL_TITLE`, `OPENCODE_DISABLE_MOUSE`, `OPENCODE_DISABLE_DEFAULT_PLUGINS`, `OPENCODE_DISABLE_LSP_DOWNLOAD`, `OPENCODE_DISABLE_AUTOCOMPACT`, `OPENCODE_DISABLE_MODELS_FETCH`, `OPENCODE_DISABLE_CLAUDE_CODE[_PROMPT|_SKILLS]`, `OPENCODE_ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_MODELS`, `OPENCODE_ENABLE_EXA`, `OPENCODE_ENABLE_PARALLEL`, `OPENCODE_MODELS_URL`, `OPENCODE_CLIENT`, `OPENCODE_GIT_BASH_PATH`, `OPENCODE_FAKE_VCS`, `OPENCODE_DB`, `OPENCODE_TEST_HOME`, plus the `OPENCODE_EXPERIMENTAL*` family. Provider keys from the environment, a project `.env`, or `~/.local/share/opencode/auth.json`. Config-home isolation: `OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR` (data dir `~/.local/share/opencode/`, cache `~/.cache/opencode/`, state `~/.local/state/opencode/`; `OPENCODE_TEST_HOME` also exists).

**Observability.** Logs `~/.local/share/opencode/log/` with timestamped filenames (`2025-01-09T123456.log`), most recent 10 kept; `--print-logs` mirrors to stderr; `--log-level DEBUG|INFO|WARN|ERROR`; config `logLevel`. OTel: `experimental.openTelemetry: true` enables spans for AI-SDK calls (Vercel AI SDK `experimental_telemetry`); no OTLP endpoint key in the schema — configure via standard `OTEL_*` env vars. Cost/usage: `opencode stats [--days N] [--tools N] [--models[=N]] [--project <p>]`; per-turn usage/cost inside the `step_finish` part and the `message.updated` bus event. Exit codes: `0` success; `1` for usage errors (no message and no command, `--fork` without `-c`/`-s`, session not found, unknown agent, `--mini` with `--format json`) and for agent/session errors (`process.exitCode = 1` when the run loop records an error). No other documented codes.

**Gotchas.**
- Non-interactive runs **auto-reject** permissions by default: on `permission.asked` without `--auto` the CLI prints `permission requested: … ; auto-rejecting` and replies `reject`. Pass `--auto` or a permissive `permission` config or nothing gets edited.
- Non-interactive runs also install a deny ruleset for `question`, `plan_enter`, `plan_exit`, so the agent cannot stop to ask.
- `run` reads stdin whenever stdin is not a TTY — an inherited, never-closed pipe blocks `Bun.stdin.text()` forever. Close the child's stdin or give it `/dev/null`.
- Interactive mode requires a TTY on stdout (`if (interactive && !process.stdout.isTTY)` bails).
- The TUI's HTTP port is random unless `--port`/`--hostname` is passed; to script a running TUI use `GET /tui/control/next` + `POST /tui/append-prompt` / `/tui/submit-prompt` / `/tui/execute-command`.
- Cold start pays MCP boot on every `run`; keep one `opencode serve` and use `--attach`.
- npm plugin install runs `bun install` at startup on first use — slow and network-dependent in a fresh container; `--pure` skips external plugins.
- Autoupdate checks run by default; set `OPENCODE_DISABLE_AUTOUPDATE=1` in CI.
- Prefer the HTTP API over reading `opencode.db` directly.

**Driver notes.**
- Window mode: PTY-drive `opencode` but pass `--port`/`--hostname` so the driver can also reach `/tui/*` control endpoints; the TUI refuses to start without a TTY on stdout.
- Session mode: `opencode run -s <sessionID> --format json --auto "…"`, one process per turn; terminate parsing on `session.status` → `idle` since there is no result envelope, and always close the child's stdin.
- Batch mode: the strongest option is a long-lived `opencode serve` plus `POST /session/:id/message` and the `GET /event` SSE stream — it avoids per-turn MCP cold start entirely.
- `--auto` (or explicit `permission` config) is mandatory headless, otherwise every permission is auto-rejected silently.
- Policy/audit must be a TS plugin (`permission.ask`, `tool.execute.before` throwing to block) — there are no shell hooks.
- Most stable surface: the OpenAPI 3.1 HTTP/SSE server (`GET /doc`) and `@opencode-ai/sdk`; `opencode acp` for editor-style embedding.

**Sources.**
- https://opencode.ai/docs/cli/
- https://opencode.ai/docs/server/
- https://opencode.ai/config.json
- https://opencode.ai/docs/plugins/
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/anomalyco/opencode/dev/packages/opencode/src/cli/cmd/run.ts
- https://opencode.ai/docs/permissions/

---

### Goose (AAIF, formerly Block Inc., Apache-2.0, v1.49.0, `curl -fsSL https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/releases/download/stable/download_cli.sh | bash`)

**Launch.** TUI/interactive: `goose session` (`--session-id`, `-n/--name`, `--path`, `-r/--resume`, `--edit`, `--fork`, `--history`, `--container <id>`, `--debug`, `--max-tool-repetitions <N>`, `--max-turns <N>`, `--with-extension`, `--with-streamable-http-extension`, `--with-builtin`). Headless: `goose run` — headless is the default and it exits when the task completes; input `-i/--instructions <FILE>` (`-` for stdin), `-t/--text <TEXT>`, `--system <TEXT>`, `--recipe <FILE>`, `--params KEY=VALUE`, `--sub-recipe <RECIPE>`; session `-s/--interactive` (drop into a REPL after the initial input), `-n/--name`, `-r/--resume`, `--path`, `--container`, `--no-session`; control `--debug`, `--max-tool-repetitions`, `--max-turns` (default 1000), `--explain`, `--render-recipe`, `-q/--quiet`, `--output-format text|json|stream-json`, `--provider`, `--model`. Stdin: `echo "What is 2+2?" | goose run -i -` or a heredoc. SDK/server/ACP: `goose acp` (ACP over stdio, `--enable-scheduler`), `goose serve` (ACP over HTTP/WebSocket, binds `127.0.0.1:3284`, endpoint `/acp`; `--host`, `--port`, `--with-builtin <names>` default `developer`, `--dangerously-unauthenticated`, `--enable-scheduler`, `--allowed-origin <origin>` repeatable — specifying any replaces the default loopback origins; auth `GOOSE_SERVER__SECRET_KEY` required unless `--dangerously-unauthenticated`, HTTP clients send `X-Secret-Key`, browser WebSocket clients use `?token=`, wrong/missing → `401`; TLS via `GOOSE_TLS`, `GOOSE_TLS_CERT_PATH`, `GOOSE_TLS_KEY_PATH`); `goose mcp <name>` runs one configured MCP server as a subprocess; GDK SDK. Rust; Homebrew `brew install block-goose-cli`; add `CONFIGURE=false` before `bash` in the installer for CI.

**Input and follow-up.** `-t/--text`, `-i/--instructions FILE`, `-i -` (stdin), `--recipe FILE` (a YAML recipe whose `prompt`/`instructions` drive the run), `--system TEXT`. Multi-turn: name the session and resume — `goose run -n my-project -t "step one"` then `goose run -n my-project -r -t "step two"` (or `--session-id <id> --resume`); `--max-turns` bounds turns without user input. In-process follow-up `goose run -i plan.md -s`; over ACP send another `session/prompt`.

**Output.** `--output-format text|json|stream-json` on `goose run` (default `text`); `-q/--quiet` suppresses everything but the model response; also `goose session list -f text|json`, `goose session export --format markdown|json|yaml`, `goose recipe list --format text|json`. `json` = one pretty-printed object at the end: `{"messages":[…],"metadata":{"total_tokens","input_tokens","output_tokens","cache_read_input_tokens","cache_write_input_tokens","cost_usd","status"}}` (optional numeric fields omitted when null; `status` always present). `stream-json` = NDJSON, one serde-tagged `StreamEvent` per line: `message` (`message` — full Message with role, content parts, tool requests/responses); `notification` (`extension_id` plus a flattened `log:{message}` **or** `progress:{progress,total,message}`); `error` (`error` string); `complete` (`total_tokens`, `input_tokens`, `output_tokens`, `cache_read_input_tokens`, `cache_write_input_tokens`, `cost_usd` — all optional except `total_tokens`). Final answer: in `json` the last assistant entry in `messages`; in `stream-json` the last `message` with `role: "assistant"`, terminated by `complete`, an unambiguous end-of-run marker. A recipe's `response.json_schema` forces schema-validated structured output.

**Sessions.** `--session-id <id>` (ids look like `20251108_2`, `YYYYMMDD_<count>`; requires `--resume` or the CLI exits 1), `-n/--name`, `-r/--resume`, `--fork` (with `--resume`), `--edit` (with `--resume`; opens the conversation as YAML in `$VISUAL`/`$EDITOR`/`vi` and continues from the edited transcript), `--history`, `--path <file>` (legacy `.jsonl`), `--no-session` (output to `/dev/null`/`NUL`). `goose session list [-f text|json] [--ascending] [-w <dir>] [-l <n>]`, `goose session remove`, `goose session export [-o FILE] [--format markdown|json|yaml]`, `goose session diagnostics [-o FILE]` (JSON report with system info, full conversation, config files, recent logs). Storage: SQLite `~/.local/share/goose/sessions/sessions.db` (Windows `%APPDATA%\Block\goose\data\sessions\sessions.db`); pre-v1.10.0 per-file `.jsonl` files remain on disk but are unmanaged. Command history `~/.config/goose/history.txt`.

**Hooks.** Implements the **Open Plugins hooks specification** — real shell-command lifecycle hooks. Events and matcher targets: `SessionStart` (none), `SessionEnd` (none), `Stop` (none), `UserPromptSubmit` (prompt text), `PreToolUse` (tool name), `PreToolUseResult` (tool name; observation only but awaited), `PostToolUse` (tool name), `PostToolUseFailure` (tool name), `BeforeReadFile` (file path), `AfterFileEdit` (file path), `BeforeShellExecution` (shell command), `AfterShellExecution` (shell command). `SubagentStart`/`SubagentStop` are explicitly **not** emitted. Location: a plugin directory `~/.agents/plugins/<plugin>/` (user) or `<project>/.agents/plugins/<plugin>/`, plus goose's own install dir (`goose plugin install <git-url>`, `--auto-update`); each plugin has `plugin.json` (`name`, `version`, `description`) and `hooks/hooks.json`. Shape: `{"hooks":{"PreToolUse":[{"matcher":"developer__shell","hooks":[{"type":"command","command":"${PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/block-sudo.sh","timeout":30,"on_failure":"block"}]}]}}`; `matcher` is a **regex, not glob**, unanchored — omit or `".*"` matches everything, a bare `"*"` is an invalid regex and the rule is silently skipped; `type` only `command` (default); `command` runs with `sh -c` and `${PLUGIN_ROOT}` is substituted and exported; `timeout` seconds, **default 30**; `on_failure` `allow` (default) or `block`, interpreted only for `PreToolUse`. Payload — JSON on stdin, snake_case: `event`, `session_id`, `matcher_context`, `tool_name`, `tool_input`, `tool_call_id`, `message` (UserPromptSubmit), `last_assistant_message` (Stop), `working_dir`, and on `PreToolUseResult` only `decision` (`allow`|`deny`), `policy_evaluated`, `blocked_by`, `reason`, `cause` (`policy_denial`|`hook_failure`|absent). Built-in `tool_input` keys: `developer__shell` → `command`, `timeout_secs?`; `developer__write` → `path`, `content`; `developer__edit` → `path`, `before`, `after`; `developer__tree` → `path`, `depth`; `developer__read_image` → `source`, `crop?`. Blocking: only `PreToolUse` and `Stop`. Exit code **2** → deny with the reason from **stderr**; `{"decision":"block","reason":"…"}` on stdout → deny regardless of exit code (stdout must start with `{`, `decision` exactly `"block"`, empty reason becomes `denied by plugin hook`); exit 0 with empty stdout or `{"decision":"allow"}` → allow; anything else (stray stdout log line, malformed JSON, non-UTF-8 stdout, spawn error, timeout, signal death) = "no decision" — **stdout is the decision channel, log to stderr**. Denials return to the model as ``Tool call denied by policy hook `<plugin>`: <reason>. Do not retry; this is a policy denial, not a transient failure.``; `on_failure: block` yields ``Tool call blocked because policy hook `<plugin>` could not complete: <reason>.``. A blocking `Stop` hook forces the turn to continue; consecutive `Stop` blocks are capped, raise with `GOOSE_STOP_HOOK_BLOCK_CAP`. Context injection is **not** a hook feature — use `.goosehints`/`AGENTS.md`, `--system`, recipes, or skills. Disable via `{"disabledPlugins":["session-logger"]}` in `~/.config/goose/settings.json` or `<project>/.config/goose/settings.json`. Validation: unknown event names and unsupported action types are ignored (forward compatible); a malformed config skips that plugin's whole `hooks.json` with a warning; an invalid matcher regex skips only that rule.

**MCP.** MCP servers are called "extensions"; built-ins (`developer`, `computercontroller`, `memory`) are in-process MCP servers. Config `~/.config/goose/config.yaml`: `extensions.<name>` with `type` (`builtin|platform|stdio|streamable_http`), `name`, `enabled`, `bundled`, `cmd`, `args`, `env_keys` (inherited names), `envs` (literal values), `timeout` (seconds, 300), `available_tools` (allowlist, empty = all). Flags on both `session` and `run`: `--with-extension "[name:]ENV1=v cmd args…"`, `--with-streamable-http-extension <url>`, `--with-builtin <name[,name]>` — all repeatable; in-session `/extension <cmd>`, `/builtin <names>`. **`--with-remote-extension` (SSE) is referenced by the Running Tasks guide but is absent from `crates/goose-cli/src/cli.rs`** — treat SSE as removed in favour of `streamable_http`. OAuth for `streamable_http` extensions; `GOOSE_OAUTH_CALLBACK_PORT` pins the callback port; secrets go to the OS keyring (`GOOSE_DISABLE_KEYRING=1` falls back to `~/.config/goose/secrets.yaml`). Scopes: global `config.yaml`, per-invocation flags, per-recipe `extensions:`, org allowlisting via `GOOSE_ALLOWLIST` (a URL of permitted extensions). Filtering: `available_tools:` per extension, recipe `extensions[].available_tools`, per-tool permissions in `~/.config/goose/permissions/tool_permissions.json` (Always Allow / Ask Before / Never Allow), `goose serve --with-builtin`. Acts as MCP server: `goose mcp <name>` runs a *configured* server for another client, but goose does not expose itself as MCP; it is an ACP agent (`goose acp`, `goose serve`) and can consume other agents as providers (e.g. Claude Code, whose permission requests route through goose's confirmation UI). Elicitation, roots, sampling and MCP-UI all have dedicated docs.

**Extensibility.** Custom slash commands map a name to a recipe in `config.yaml`: `slash_commands: [{command: "run-tests", recipe_path: "/path/to/recipe.yaml"}]`, invoked `/run-tests [one parameter]`. Built-ins: `/help`, `/builtin <names>`, `/clear`, `/compact`, `/endplan`, `/exit`, `/quit`, `/extension <cmd>`, `/mode <auto|approve|smart_approve|chat>`, `/plan <text>`, `/prompt <n> [--info] [k=v…]`, `/prompts [--extension <n>]`, `/recipe [path]`, `/r`, `/skills [<name>…]`, `/t [light|dark|ansi]`. Skills: `SKILL.md` with frontmatter (`name`, `description`) in `~/.agents/skills/<name>/` or `.agents/skills/<name>/`; `goose skills list` shows sources and token counts; plugin skills namespaced `<plugin>:<skill>`. Custom agents: markdown + frontmatter (`name` required, `description`, `model`) in `~/.agents/agents/*.md` or `<project>/.agents/agents/*.md`; compatibility discovery `.goose/agents/`, `.claude/agents/`, `~/.goose/agents/`, `~/.claude/agents/`; invoke `@name`. Recipes (the main packaging unit): YAML/JSON keys `version` (default `"1.0.0"`), `title`, `description`, `instructions`, `prompt` (required in headless), `parameters` (`key`, `input_type` `string|number|boolean|date|file|select`, `requirement` `required|optional|user_prompt`, `description`, `default`, `options`), `extensions`, `activities`, `settings` (`goose_provider`, `goose_model`, `temperature`, `max_turns`), `sub_recipes` (`name`, `path`, `values`, `sequential_when_repeated`, `description`), `response` (`json_schema`), `retry` (`max_retries`, `checks[]` `{type:"shell", command}` exit 0 = success, `timeout_seconds` default 300, `on_failure`, `on_failure_timeout_seconds` default 600), `context`, `author`; run `goose run --recipe r.yaml --params k=v`, inspect `--explain`/`--render-recipe`, manage `goose recipe validate|list|open|deeplink`, schedule `goose schedule add --schedule-id … --cron "0 0 9 * * *" --recipe-source …`; discovery `GOOSE_RECIPE_PATH`, `GOOSE_RECIPE_GITHUB_REPO`, `~/.local/share/goose/recipes/`. Plugins: git-backed bundles of skills + hooks under `~/.agents/plugins/<name>/`, `goose plugin install <git-url> [--auto-update]`, `goose plugin update <name>`. Instruction files: `AGENTS.md` **then** `.goosehints` at each directory level, globally (`~/.config/goose/`) and locally, walking nested dirs as files are touched; local hints win over global; `CONTEXT_FILE_NAMES` (JSON array, default `["AGENTS.md",".goosehints"]`) changes the filenames; hints are injected into the system prompt on every request. `goose review` discovers `.agents/checks/*.md` and `.agents/REVIEW.md`.

**Permissions.** Modes via `GOOSE_MODE`: `auto` (full autonomy, **the default**), `approve` (confirm before any tool/extension use), `smart_approve` (risk-based), `chat` (no tools or file modification). Set with the env var, `GOOSE_MODE:` in `config.yaml`, `goose configure → goose settings → goose mode`, or `/mode <name>` — **there is no `--mode` CLI flag**. Per-tool permissions Always Allow / Ask Before / Never Allow in `~/.config/goose/permissions/tool_permissions.json` and `permission.yaml`, effective only in `approve` and `smart_approve`. Load-time allow/deny: `available_tools` per extension, `--with-builtin` on `goose serve`, `GOOSE_ALLOWLIST`. Runaway protection `--max-turns`/`GOOSE_MAX_TURNS` (default 1000), `GOOSE_SUBAGENT_MAX_TURNS` (25), `--max-tool-repetitions`, `GOOSE_MAX_BACKGROUND_TASKS` (5). Sandbox: `--container <container_id>` on `session` and `run` executes extensions inside a Docker container; no seccomp/bubblewrap-style built-in sandbox. Security: prompt-injection detection (`SECURITY_PROMPT_ENABLED`, `SECURITY_PROMPT_THRESHOLD` default 0.8, `SECURITY_PROMPT_CLASSIFIER_ENABLED`), findings logged as `SEC-{uuid}` with the user's decision. Pre-answering trust in automation: `GOOSE_MODE=auto`, install with `CONFIGURE=false`, `GOOSE_DISABLE_KEYRING=1` plus provider keys in the environment, `GOOSE_TELEMETRY_ENABLED` set ahead of the first-use question, `--no-session` for one-shots.

**Environment.** Config `~/.config/goose/config.yaml` (also holds `GOOSE_*` keys as YAML keys), `secrets.yaml`, `permission.yaml`, `permissions/tool_permissions.json`, `settings.json` (`disabledPlugins`), `prompts/`; Windows under `%APPDATA%\Block\goose\config\`. Precedence: environment variables > config file > defaults. Model: `GOOSE_PROVIDER`/`GOOSE_MODEL`, per-run `--provider`/`--model` on `goose run` and `goose review`, recipe `settings.goose_provider`/`goose_model`, `goose configure`; planning uses `GOOSE_PLANNER_PROVIDER`/`GOOSE_PLANNER_MODEL`. Key env vars — model: `GOOSE_TEMPERATURE`, `GOOSE_MAX_TOKENS`, `GOOSE_CONTEXT_LIMIT`, `GOOSE_CACHE_TTL`, `CLAUDE_THINKING_TYPE`, `GEMINI3_THINKING_LEVEL`; session: `GOOSE_MAX_TURNS`, `GOOSE_SUBAGENT_MAX_TURNS`, `GOOSE_GATEWAY_MAX_TURNS`, `GOOSE_MAX_BACKGROUND_TASKS`, `GOOSE_AUTO_COMPACT_THRESHOLD` (0.8), `GOOSE_CONTEXT_STRATEGY`, `GOOSE_DISABLE_SESSION_NAMING`, `CONTEXT_FILE_NAMES`, `GOOSE_STOP_HOOK_BLOCK_CAP`; tools: `GOOSE_MODE`, `GOOSE_TOOLSHIM`, `GOOSE_TOOLSHIM_BACKEND`, `GOOSE_TOOLSHIM_OLLAMA_MODEL`, `GOOSE_CLI_MIN_PRIORITY`, `GOOSE_DEBUG`, `GOOSE_SHELL`; security: `GOOSE_ALLOWLIST`, `GOOSE_DISABLE_KEYRING`, `SECURITY_PROMPT_*`, `GOOSE_TELEMETRY_ENABLED`; recipes: `GOOSE_RECIPE_PATH`, `GOOSE_RECIPE_GITHUB_REPO`, `GOOSE_RECIPE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS`, `GOOSE_RECIPE_ON_FAILURE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS`; server: `GOOSE_SERVER__SECRET_KEY`, `GOOSE_TLS*`, `GOOSE_OAUTH_CALLBACK_PORT`, `GOOSE_PATH_ROOT`; UX: `GOOSE_CLI_THEME`, `GOOSE_CLI_LIGHT_THEME`, `GOOSE_CLI_DARK_THEME`, `GOOSE_CLI_SHOW_THINKING`, `GOOSE_CLI_SHOW_COST`, `GOOSE_CLI_NEWLINE_KEY`, `GOOSE_PROMPT_EDITOR`; network `HTTP_PROXY`, `HTTPS_PROXY`, `NO_PROXY`. Goose sets `GOOSE_TERMINAL=1`, `AGENT=goose`, `AGENT_SESSION_ID` for child processes. Config-home isolation: `GOOSE_PATH_ROOT` overrides the root data directory.

**Observability.** OTel first-class: `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT`, `OTEL_TRACES_EXPORTER`, `OTEL_METRICS_EXPORTER`; the CLI shuts the OTLP pipeline down cleanly on exit when built with the `otel` feature; tutorials for Langfuse (`LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY`, `LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY`, `LANGFUSE_URL`), Laminar, MLflow, clawmetry. Logs `~/.local/state/goose/logs/cli/<YYYY-MM-DD>/` and `server/`, auto-pruned after two weeks; desktop `~/Library/Application Support/Goose/logs/main.log`; `--debug` expands tool output; `goose session diagnostics` bundles logs + transcript + config as JSON. Cost/usage: the `json` `metadata` block and the `stream-json` `complete` event both carry `total_tokens`, `input_tokens`, `output_tokens`, `cache_read_input_tokens`, `cache_write_input_tokens`, `cost_usd`; `GOOSE_CLI_SHOW_COST=true` for per-turn cost. Telemetry opt-in, asked on first use; collects OS/arch, version, install method, provider/model, extension and tool names/counts, session duration, token usage, error types — never conversation content or tool arguments. Exit codes: `fn main() -> Result<()>` maps `Ok(())` → 0 and `Err` → 1; usage errors (`--session-id` without `--resume`, missing instruction file, no `-i`/`-t`/`--recipe`, invalid recipe render) call `std::process::exit(1)`. **No exit-code signal for a failed agent turn** — agent errors are caught in the session loop (`handle_agent_error`), emitted as a `stream-json` `error` event or on stderr, and the process still exits `0`.

**Gotchas.**
- Exit code 0 on agent failure — drive on the `stream-json` `error` event or `metadata.status`, never `$?`.
- Set `GOOSE_MODE=auto` explicitly in CI even though it is the default: a config file or desktop session may have persisted `approve`, blocking on a prompt nobody answers.
- `goose run -i -` is the only stdin path; `goose run` with no `-i`, `-t` or `--recipe` exits 1 with a usage error rather than reading stdin.
- Session naming makes an extra model call per run — `GOOSE_DISABLE_SESSION_NAMING=true`; `--no-session` keeps `sessions.db` from growing.
- `--max-turns` defaults to 1000 — always lower it unattended.
- `--with-extension "npx -y @modelcontextprotocol/server-memory"` names the extension after the launcher (`npx`), so tools become `npx__search` and two npx servers collide; always use `"memory:npx -y …"`.
- `goose serve` refuses to start without `GOOSE_SERVER__SECRET_KEY` unless `--dangerously-unauthenticated`; any `--allowed-origin` **replaces** the default loopback origins.
- The install script's first run starts an interactive configure wizard — `CONFIGURE=false`; keyring access fails in headless containers — `GOOSE_DISABLE_KEYRING=1`.
- Hook stdout is the decision channel: one stray `echo` in a `PreToolUse` hook turns it into "no decision". Log to stderr.
- rustyline + indicatif spinners: use `-q/--quiet` or a JSON format under a pipe; no documented minimum terminal size.
- `Ctrl+C` has three behaviours (clear line / interrupt request / exit on empty line).
- Docs and repo moved: `goose-docs.ai`, `aaif-goose/goose`.

**Driver notes.**
- Window mode: PTY-drive `goose session`; expect spinners and three-way `Ctrl+C` semantics — pass `-q` or a JSON format if anything parses the stream.
- Session mode: `goose run -n <name> -r -t "…" --output-format stream-json --max-turns <small>` per turn; `complete` is a clean end-of-run marker, and `error` is the only failure signal.
- Batch mode: recipes are the native batch unit — `goose run --recipe r.yaml --params k=v --no-session --output-format json`, with `response.json_schema` for validated structured output and `retry.checks[]` for shell-verified success.
- Environment must be pre-seeded: `GOOSE_MODE=auto`, `GOOSE_DISABLE_KEYRING=1`, `GOOSE_DISABLE_SESSION_NAMING=true`, provider keys, `CONFIGURE=false` at install.
- Policy enforcement is real here: ship a plugin with `hooks/hooks.json` `PreToolUse` + `on_failure: block`, and keep every hook's stdout clean.
- Most stable surface: `--output-format stream-json` for one-shot runs; `goose acp`/`goose serve` (ACP over stdio or HTTP/WS) for a supervised long-lived driver.

**Sources.**
- https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/goose-cli-commands/
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/aaif-goose/goose/main/documentation/docs/guides/context-engineering/hooks.md
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/aaif-goose/goose/main/crates/goose-cli/src/session/mod.rs
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/aaif-goose/goose/main/documentation/docs/guides/recipes/recipe-reference.md
- https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/environment-variables
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/aaif-goose/goose/main/documentation/docs/gdk/acp/index.md

---

### Cline CLI (Cline Bot Inc., Apache-2.0, v3.0.61, `npm install -g cline`)

**Launch.** TUI: `cline` (no prompt) or `cline -i`/`--tui`, built on OpenTUI; requires **both** `process.stdin.isTTY` and `process.stdout.isTTY` or it errors `interactive mode requires a TTY (stdin/stdout must both be terminals)`. Headless: `cline "prompt"` — **there is no `-p`/`--print` flag** (`-p` is `--plan`); headless is *detected*: `isHeadless = yolo || zen || outputMode==="json" || (!process.stdin.isTTY && !interactive)`. Stdin: detected by `stdinHasPipedInput()` — `process.stdin.isTTY === false` **and** `fstatSync(0).isFIFO() || isFile()`. SDK/server/ACP: `--acp` (ACP over stdio; auto-approve defaults to `false` in ACP mode, enabled only by explicit `--auto-approve true`/`--yolo`; clients Zed, JetBrains AI Assistant, Neovim CodeCompanion, Emacs agent-shell, Obsidian, marimo); hub daemon `cline hub ensure|start|status|stop|drain [--reason <text>] [--off]|upgrade` with `--cwd`, `--host`, `--port`, `--pathname`, default `127.0.0.1:25463` (`CLINE_HUB_ADDRESS`), backend `CLINE_SESSION_BACKEND_MODE` = `local|hub|remote|auto`; `cline dashboard` (HTTP/WebSocket over the hub); SDK `@cline/sdk` / `@cline/core` (`ClineCore.start()` accepts `extensions` and `pluginPaths`). Background: `-z, --zen` submits to the hub daemon and exits immediately, no streaming, full auto-approval, incompatible with `--data-dir` and `--tui`. `-y, --yolo` (hidden but accepted) skips approvals, enables `submit_and_exit`, disables `spawn`/`team`, and **disables runtime hooks entirely**. Prebuilt platform binaries via npm optionalDependencies; no Node/Bun runtime needed. `bin/cline` harvests OS trust anchors into `~/.cline/cli-node-extra-ca-certs.pem` and sets `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`.

**Input and follow-up.** argv takes a single positional and **the prompt must be one quoted token containing whitespace**: `program.args.length > 1` or an arg with no whitespace prints `Unknown command or unquoted prompt: …  Prompt text must be passed as a single quoted argument` and exits 1 (so `cline hello` is rejected). Piped stdin is read to EOF; with a positional prompt too, they concatenate as `${args.prompt}\n\n${pipedInput}`. No `--prompt-file`/`-f`. **Headless multi-turn is not supported**: `--id <session-id>` forces `startupTarget = "chat"` and rewrites args to `{interactive: true, prompt: undefined}`, so `--json` then fails with `JSON output mode requires a prompt argument or piped stdin (interactive mode is unsupported)` (open: cline/cline#13239, #10856). Resume works only via the interactive TUI.
