A pending-prompt mechanism exists in core (`pending_prompts`/`pending_prompt_submitted`, `delivery: "queue"|"steer"`) driven by the TUI and hub RPC, but no CLI flag or stdin protocol exposes it in one-shot mode. Prompts pass through `rewriteTeamPrompt()`.

**Output.** Text (default, styled ANSI; `-v/--verbose` adds a run-stats line) or NDJSON via `--json` — **there is no `--output-format` flag**. Envelope (`apps/cli/src/utils/output.ts::emitJsonLine`): `JSON.stringify({ ts: nowIso(), ...record })` — **`ts` is an ISO-8601 string, not a number**. stdout records: `run_start` (`providerId`, `modelId`, `catalog` `"live"`/`"bundled"`, `thinking`, `mode`, `sessionId`); `agent_event` (`event`); `hook_event` (`hookEventName`, `agentId`, `taskId`, `parentAgentId`); `team_restored` (`teamName`); `run_abort_requested` (`reason` `"sigint"`/`"sigterm"`); `run_result` (`finishReason`, `iterations`, `usage`, `aggregateUsage?`, `durationMs`, `text`, `model`); `run_aborted` (`reason` `"local_abort"`/`"external_abort"`, `message`). stderr: `{"ts":…,"type":"error","message":"…"}`. `AgentEvent` union (each also `agentId?`, `conversationId?`, `parentAgentId?`): `content_start` (`contentType` `"text"|"reasoning"|"media"|"tool"`, `text?`, `accumulated?`, `reasoning?`, `redacted?`, `toolName?`, `toolCallId?`, `input?`, `execution?` `"client"|"provider"`), `content_update`, `content_end` (+`output?`, `error?`, `durationMs?`, `media?`), `iteration_start` (`iteration`), `iteration_end` (`iteration`, `hadToolCalls`, `toolCallCount`), `usage` (`inputTokens`, `outputTokens`, `cacheReadTokens?`, `cacheWriteTokens?`, `cost?`, `total*`), `notice` (`noticeType` `"recovery"|"stop"|"status"`, `message`, `displayRole?`, `reason?` ∈ `api_error|invalid_tool_call|completion_without_submit|tool_execution_failed|mistake_limit|auto_compaction|manual_compaction|compaction_budget_emergency`, `metadata?`), `done` (`reason` ∈ `completed|max_iterations|aborted|mistake_limit|error`, `text`, `iterations`, `usage?`), `error` (`error {name,message,stack}`, `errorClass?`, `recoverable`, `iteration`). Final answer: the `agent_event` with `event.type === "done"` (`event.text`) and the terminal `run_result` line (`.text`) — key off `run_result`. **Docs discrepancy:** `docs/cli/cli-reference.mdx` and docs.cline.bot document `{"type":"say"|"ask","text","ts":<number ms>,…}` — that is the VS Code webview format, not what `apps/cli` emits. `--json` also suppresses `writeln()` entirely; only `writeDiagnostic()` reaches raw stderr.

**Sessions.** `--id <session-id>` (also sets `CLINE_HOOK_AGENT_RESUME=1` so hooks fire `agent_resume`); broken in headless/JSON mode. No `--continue`/`-c` (`-c` is `--cwd`). Id format `<epoch-ms>_<suffix>` (e.g. `1779126186341_2vo9c`). Storage: sessions root `~/.cline/data/sessions/` (env `CLINE_SESSION_DATA_DIR`) with `<id>/<id>.json` (manifest incl. `metadata.title`), `<id>/<id>.messages.json` (full transcript), `<id>/<id>.compaction.json`, `sessions.index.json` (`{version:1, sessions:{<id>:SessionRow}}`, atomic `.tmp`+rename), `subagent-spawn-queue.json`; `~/.cline/data/db/` holds `cron.db`, `tasks.db`, `connectors.db` (schedules/connectors, **not** transcripts — the docs' "sessions are a SQLite database" is inaccurate for the file backend). Listing `cline history` (alias `h`) with `--json`, `--limit` (default 50), `--page`, `--config`; on a TTY without `--json` it opens the TUI history view instead of printing. Management `cline history delete --session-id <id>`, `cline history update --session-id <id> [--prompt|--title|--metadata <json>]`, `cline history export <sessionId> [-o <path>]` (standalone HTML). Checkpoints: `/undo` in the TUI only.

**Hooks.** Two systems. **(a) File-based hooks** (what the CLI uses). Search dirs in order, all collected and run sorted by path (additive, not first-wins): `~/Documents/Cline/Hooks`, `~/.cline/hooks`, `<workspace>/.clinerules/hooks` (deprecated but read), `<workspace>/.cline/hooks`. Files matched by **basename** (case-insensitive), extensions `""`, `.sh`, `.bash`, `.zsh`, `.js`, `.mjs`, `.cjs`, `.ts`, `.mts`, `.cts`, `.py`, `.ps1`: `TaskStart` → `agent_start` (async), `TaskResume` → `agent_resume` (async), `TaskCancel` → `agent_abort` (async), `TaskComplete` → `agent_end` (async), `TaskError` → `agent_error` (async), `PreToolUse` → `tool_call` (**blocking**), `PostToolUse` → `tool_result` (**blocking**), `UserPromptSubmit` → `prompt_submit` (async), `SessionShutdown` → `session_shutdown` (async), `PreCompact` → mapped to `undefined`, recognized but **not dispatched** by the file-hook runner (the full `HookEventName` enum includes `pre_compact`, reachable via the SDK). Interpreter: shebang first (with `env` unwrapping, `python`→`py -3` on Windows), else by extension (`.sh/.bash/.zsh`→`bash`, `.js/.mjs/.cjs`→`node`, `.ts/.mts/.cts`→`bun run`, `.py`→`python3`/`py -3`, `.ps1`→`powershell`/`pwsh -File`), else `bash <path>`. Delivery: one JSON object on stdin, then stdin closed; cwd = workspace, env inherited. Payload casing is **deliberately mixed**: base fields `clineVersion`, `hookName`, `timestamp` (ISO), `taskId`, `sessionContext {rootSessionId, hookLogPath?}`, `workspaceRoots[]`, `workspaceInfo? {rootPath, hint, associatedRemoteUrls, latestGitCommitHash, latestGitBranchName}`, `userId` (`CLINE_USER_ID` → `USER` → `"unknown"`), plus snake_case **`agent_id`** and **`parent_agent_id`**. Per-event: `tool_call` adds `iteration`, `tool_call {id,name,input}`, `preToolUse {toolName, parameters}` (parameters `Record<string,string>`, non-strings JSON-stringified); `tool_result` adds `iteration`, `tool_result` (`ToolCallRecord {id,name,input,output,error?,durationMs,startedAt,endedAt}`), `postToolUse {toolName,parameters,result,success,executionTimeMs}`; `prompt_submit` adds `userPromptSubmit {prompt, attachments[]}`; `agent_start` `taskStart {taskMetadata}`; `agent_resume` `taskResume {taskMetadata, previousState}`; `agent_abort` `reason?`, `taskCancel`; `agent_end` `iteration`, `turn {outputText,status}`, `taskComplete`; `agent_error` `iteration`, `error {name,message,stack?}`; `session_shutdown` `reason?`; `pre_compact` `preCompact {taskId, ulid, contextSize, compactionStrategy, previousApiReqIndex, tokensIn, tokensOut, tokensInCache, tokensOutCache, deletedRangeStart, deletedRangeEnd, contextJsonPath, contextRawPath}`. Control protocol (blocking hooks only), JSON on stdout, lines prefixed `HOOK_CONTROL\t` preferred (**last one wins**), else the whole trimmed stdout parsed: `cancel: true` blocks the call (reason = `errorMessage` ?? `context`, surfaced as `stop: true, reason: <text>`); `review: true` forces a review; `context` **or** `contextModification` injected as `appendContext` — **only when `cancel` is not true**; `errorMessage` beats `context` for the cancel reason; `overrideInput` replaces PreToolUse tool input. Multiple files for one event run sequentially; controls merge — `cancel` OR'd, `context` joined with `\n`, last `overrideInput` wins. Timeouts: blocking hooks get `toolCallTimeoutMs ?? 120000` (120 s); async hooks have no timeout and are spawned `detached: true` with stdio `ignore`. On timeout, non-zero JSON parse failure, or spawn error the hook is logged as a warning and **ignored — fail-open**; there is no `fail_closed` for file hooks. **Matchers: none** — a `PreToolUse` script fires for every tool and must inspect `payload.preToolUse.toolName`. Audit log: every invocation appended as JSONL to `~/.cline/data/logs/hooks.jsonl` (`CLINE_HOOKS_LOG_PATH`). `--hooks-dir <path>`/`CLINE_HOOKS_DIR` is set by `main.ts` but **no reader for that variable exists** in `apps/cli` or `sdk/packages/*` — treat as non-functional. `--yolo` disables all runtime hooks. **(b) `cline hook`** reads one JSON `HookEventPayload` from stdin, appends it to the audit log, dispatches it to the session, prints `{}`, exits 0 (empty/invalid → stderr, exit 1) — an *ingest* endpoint, not a registrar. **(c) SDK plugin hooks** (richer, plugin-only): stages `input, runtime_event, session_start, run_start, iteration_start, turn_start, before_agent_start, tool_call_before, tool_call_after, turn_end, stop_error, iteration_end, run_end, session_shutdown, error`; handlers `beforeRun, afterRun, beforeModel, afterModel, beforeTool, afterTool, onEvent`; policy `{mode:"blocking"|"async", timeoutMs, retries, retryDelayMs, failureMode:"fail_open"|"fail_closed", maxConcurrency, queueLimit}`.

**MCP.** Config `~/.cline/data/settings/cline_mcp_settings.json` (`resolveMcpSettingsPath()`, override `CLINE_MCP_SETTINGS_PATH`). **There is no per-project MCP file** — the `.cline/mcp.json` in `docs/cli/cli-reference.mdx` does not exist in `paths.ts` or the loader. Shape `{"mcpServers":{"<name>": <registration>}}`, bootstrapped to `{"mcpServers":{}}`. Nested registration `{"transport":{"type":"stdio","command","args","cwd","env"},"disabled":false,"timeout":60,"metadata":{},"oauthClient":{"clientId","clientSecret"},"oauth":{}}`; remote `{"transport":{"type":"sse"|"streamableHttp","url","headers"}}`; legacy flat form accepted (`"type"|"transportType"` ∈ `stdio|sse|http|streamableHttp`, `"http"` maps to `streamableHttp`). Transports stdio, sse, streamableHttp (streamable HTTP recommended, SSE legacy). OAuth for non-stdio only (`oauthSupported = transport.type !== "stdio"`); state persists in the settings file under `oauth` (`clientInformation`, `tokens`, `codeVerifier`, `discoveryState`, `redirectUrl`, `lastError`, `lastAuthenticatedAt`, `authorizationRequired`) and static creds under `oauthClient`. Scopes: **one global file, no tiering**; Cline plugins can contribute servers, and portable Agent Plugins are discovered only from `~/.agents/plugins` explicitly so "opening a repository cannot activate repository-controlled MCP servers." CLI: `cline mcp` (wizard, **requires a TTY**, otherwise prints `MCP wizard requires a TTY. Use cline config mcp to list servers.`), `cline mcp install <name> [targetArgs…]` (alias `add`) with `--transport <stdio|sse|http|streamable-http|streamableHttp>` (default stdio), `--header` (repeatable), `--yes`, `--json`; stdio args after `--`; `cline mcp uninstall <name> [--json]`; non-interactive listing `cline config mcp [--json]`. No `--mcp-config` file flag. Tool naming `<serverName>__<toolName>`, sanitized to `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`, capped at 64 chars (truncated + `_<8-hex-sha1>`); disable a server with `"disabled": true`, individual tools via `createDisabledMcpToolPolicy` → `ToolPolicy {enabled:false}` driven by `disabledTools` in `global-settings.json`; **no `autoApprove` array** in the CLI/core zod schema (it exists only in IDE-oriented docs). Acts as MCP server: **none** — no `cline mcp serve`, no `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server` import anywhere; ACP is the embedding surface. Unreachable server: **non-fatal** — `McpManager.connectState` catches, disconnects, sets `status = "disconnected"`, records `lastError`, and the run continues without those tools. Known defect (cline/cline#12344, CLI 3.0.40): the `initialize` handshake has a hardcoded ~1500 ms budget (other requests ~5000 ms) and the per-server `timeout` does **not** apply to it, so slow servers are dropped with `[mcp] Failed to load tools from MCP server "<name>", skipping: MCP request timed out for "<name>" (initialize).` while still appearing in the list with zero tools.

**Extensibility.** Rules search order (`resolveRulesConfigSearchPaths()`, deduped): `<workspace>/AGENTS.md`, `<workspace>/.clinerules` (dir or file), `<workspace>/.cline/rules/`, `~/.agents/AGENTS.md`, `~/.cline/rules/`, `~/Cline/Rules/` (WSL/headless fallback, cline/cline#13542), `~/Documents/Cline/Rules/`; docs add auto-detection of `.cursorrules` and `.windsurfrules` and state workspace rules beat global on conflict; all `.md`/`.txt` in `.clinerules/` merge (numeric prefixes are convention only). Skills (`resolveSkillsConfigSearchPaths()`): `<ws>/.clinerules/skills`, `<ws>/.cline/skills`, `<ws>/.agents/skills`, `~/.cline/skills`, `~/.agents/skills`; each a directory with `SKILL.md` (frontmatter `name` matching the dir, `description` ≤1024 chars) plus optional `docs/`, `templates/`, `scripts/`; invoked by description match or `/<skill-name>`; docs also list `.claude/skills/` and claim global skills win collisions, but `.claude/skills` is not in the source path list. Workflows: `<ws>/.clinerules/workflows`, `~/Documents/Cline/Workflows`, `~/.cline/workflows`, `<ws>/.cline/workflows` (docs say `~/.cline/data/workflows/`; the source resolves `~/.cline/workflows`). Slash commands (TUI only): `/settings`, `/config`, `/model`, `/theme`, `/account`, `/mcp`, `/plugins`, `/compact`, `/skills`, `/fork`, `/undo`, `/clear`, `/history`, `/help`, `/quit` (docs' `/newtask`, `/smol`, `/newrule`, `/deep-planning`, `/reportbug` are the IDE set; `/smol` aliases `/compact`); connector chat surfaces have their own set. Plugins, two deliberately separate lanes: *Cline plugins* (JS/TS at `<ws>/.cline/plugins`, `~/.cline/plugins`, `~/Documents/Cline/Plugins`; managed installs under `~/.cline/plugins/_installed/{npm,git,remote,local}/`; manifest via a `cline` key in `package.json`: `{"cline":{"plugins":[{"paths":["./index.ts"],"capabilities":["tools","hooks"]}]}}`; `node_modules` never descended; `cline plugin install <source> [--npm] [--git] [--force] [--json] [--cwd]`, `cline plugin uninstall <name> [--json]`; contributions `{capabilities, tools, skills, rules, hooks, commands, mcpServers, providers}`) and *Agent Plugins* (agent-plugins.org format, require `plugin.json`, discovered **only** from `~/.agents/plugins`). Subagents/teams: definitions at `<ws>/.cline/agents/` and `~/.cline/agents/`; runtime `spawn` and `team` tools; `--team-name <name>` persists under `~/.cline/data/teams/<team-name>/` (`CLINE_TEAM_DATA_DIR`) with a task board, inter-agent messaging and activity history; `/team` in-session; both tools **disabled by default under `--yolo` and `--zen`**; teammate output is filtered out of stdout (only `usage` events pass). Scheduling `cline schedule create|list|get|trigger|history|export|import` (`--cron`, `--prompt`, `--workspace`, `--timeout`, `--tags`, `--limit`, `--delivery-adapter`, `--delivery-bot`, `--delivery-thread`); specs at `~/.cline/cron/` or `<ws>/.cline/cron/`, state in `cron.db`. Connectors `cline connect <telegram|slack|gchat|whatsapp|linear|discord>`.

**Permissions.** Modes act (default) / plan (`-p, --plan`; legacy `-a, --act` hidden); persisted `planActMode` in `global-settings.json`; in plan mode a `command-guard` blocks file-editing commands and output redirection to non-`/dev`, non-temp targets. `--auto-approve <boolean>` defaults **`true`** in the CLI and **`false`** in ACP mode, applied as `{"*": {autoApprove: <bool>}}`. `-y, --yolo` skips approvals, enables `submit_and_exit`, disables `spawn`/`team`, disables hooks. Safe-tool allowlist when auto-approve is off (`tool-policies.ts`): `ask_followup_question`, `ask_question`, `fetch_web_content`, `read_files`, `search_codebase`, `skills`, `submit_and_exit`; full built-in set `read_files`, `search_codebase`, `run_commands`, `fetch_web_content`, `apply_patch`, `editor`, `skills`, `ask_question`, `submit_and_exit`. Approval prompt (TTY): `Approve tool "<tool_name>" with input <preview>? [y/N]` — anything but `y`/`yes` rejects; **if stdin/stdout is not a TTY, approval-required calls are auto-denied rather than hanging**. Desktop approval over file IPC: `CLINE_TOOL_APPROVAL_MODE=desktop` + `CLINE_TOOL_APPROVAL_DIR=<path>` (request JSON written, decision JSON polled; exact schema undocumented). Disallowed tools via `disabledTools: string[]` and `tools: {<name>:{enabled}}` in `global-settings.json`; no `--allowedTools`/`--disallowedTools` flags. Sandbox: `--data-dir <path>` or `CLINE_SANDBOX=1` is **state isolation, not OS-level sandboxing** — no seccomp/seatbelt/landlock/container anywhere in the repo; `configureSandboxEnvironment()` sets `CLINE_SANDBOX=1`, `CLINE_SANDBOX_DATA_DIR`, `CLINE_DATA_DIR`, `CLINE_DB_DATA_DIR`, `CLINE_SESSION_DATA_DIR`, `CLINE_TEAM_DATA_DIR`, `CLINE_PROVIDER_SETTINGS_PATH`, `CLINE_HOOKS_LOG_PATH` inside the dir (default `<tmpdir>/cline-sandbox`), but **MCP settings are not redirected**. `--worktree` auto-creates a detached git worktree under `~/.cline/worktrees/`. `CLINE_COMMAND_PERMISSIONS` is documented as `{"allow":["npm *","git *"],"deny":["rm -rf *","sudo *"],"allowRedirects":false}` but **no implementation exists** in `apps/cli` or `sdk/packages/*` — do not rely on it as a security control. Trust prompts: none — no workspace-trust dialog; the only interactive gates are tool approval and the auth/MCP/connect/schedule wizards. OAuth providers (`cline`, `openai-codex`, `oca`) never auto-launch a browser; headless with no saved credentials fails fast.

**Environment.** Provider keys `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `CLINE_API_KEY`, `OPENAI_API_KEY`, `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`, `AI_GATEWAY_API_KEY`, `V0_API_KEY` (`--key` beats all). Paths/state `CLINE_DIR` (default `~/.cline`), `CLINE_DATA_DIR` (default `<CLINE_DIR>/data`), `CLINE_SESSION_DATA_DIR`, `CLINE_TEAM_DATA_DIR`, `CLINE_DB_DATA_DIR`, `CLINE_PROVIDER_SETTINGS_PATH`, `CLINE_GLOBAL_SETTINGS_PATH`, `CLINE_MCP_SETTINGS_PATH`, `CLINE_SANDBOX`, `CLINE_SANDBOX_DATA_DIR`, `CLINE_HOOKS_DIR` (no reader), `CLINE_HOOKS_LOG_PATH`. Runtime `CLINE_HUB_ADDRESS`, `CLINE_SESSION_BACKEND_MODE`, `CLINE_TOOL_APPROVAL_MODE`, `CLINE_TOOL_APPROVAL_DIR`, `CLINE_USER_ID`, `CLINE_VERSION`, `CLINE_HOOK_AGENT_RESUME`, `CLINE_WRAPPER_PATH`, `CLINE_NO_AUTO_UPDATE`, `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS` (merged, not replaced). Logging `CLINE_LOG_ENABLED`, `CLINE_LOG_LEVEL` (`trace|debug|info|warn|error|fatal|silent`, default `info`), `CLINE_LOG_PATH`, `CLINE_LOG_NAME`, `CLINE_DEBUG`, `CLINE_BUILD_ENV`, `CLINE_DEBUG_HOST`, `CLINE_DEBUG_PORT_BASE`. Settings files `~/.cline/data/settings/providers.json`, `global-settings.json` (zod-validated `telemetryOptOut` default false, `autoUpdateEnabled` default true, `compactionStrategy` `basic|agentic`, `compactionEnabled`, `planActMode`, `toolAutoApprove`, `tuiTheme`, `disabledTools[]`, `tools{name:{enabled}}`, `disabledPlugins[]`, `disabledAgentPlugins[]`; unknown keys stripped), `cline_mcp_settings.json`. Precedence: explicit CLI flags > `global-settings.json` > defaults; `--key` > env vars > persisted provider settings. Model/provider: `-P, --provider <id>` (default `cline`), `-m, --model` (default `anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.6`), `-k, --key`, `-s, --system`, `--thinking [none|low|medium|high|xhigh]` (bare = `medium`), `--compaction <agentic|basic|off>`, `--retries` (README says default 3, the Commander description says 6 — unresolved), `-t, --timeout <seconds>` (default 0 = none), `-c, --cwd`; setup `cline auth [provider] [-p|-k|-m|-b|--azure-api-version]`. Telemetry opt-out is `telemetryOptOut: true` in `global-settings.json`; `DO_NOT_TRACK`/`CLINE_TELEMETRY` are not honored. Config-home isolation: `CLINE_DIR` (or `--config <path>`), plus `CLINE_DATA_DIR`; `--data-dir` sandboxes everything **except** MCP settings; there is no dedicated HOME override (`setHomeDir(homedir())` at startup).

**Observability.** Runtime log `~/.cline/data/logs/cline.log` (`cline doctor log` opens it). Hook audit log `~/.cline/data/logs/hooks.jsonl`. Hub log `~/.cline/data/logs/hub-daemon.log`; connector logs `~/.cline/data/connectors/`. Diagnostics `cline doctor` (scans stale CLI/hub/sidecar processes via `pgrep -fal` and listening ports via `lsof -nP -tiTCP:<port> -sTCP:LISTEN`, skipping PIDs from other containers), `cline doctor fix`, `cline doctor log`, `cline config [--json]`. Usage/cost: text mode `-v` prints `[<elapsed>s | <in> in, <out> out[, N cache read][, N cache write][, $X est. cost][, N iterations]]`; JSON mode `run_result.usage`/`aggregateUsage` (`inputTokens`, `outputTokens`, `cacheReadTokens`, `cacheWriteTokens`, `cost`, `total*`) plus streaming `usage` events; cost is zeroed where not meaningful. No OTel. Exit codes: `0` = `finishReason === "completed"` or clean SIGINT shutdown; `1` = argument/usage error, JSON-mode misuse, missing session, worktree failure, run timeout, thrown error, or any `finishReason !== "completed"` (`max_iterations`, `mistake_limit`, `error`). **An aborted run (`finishReason === "aborted"`) exits `0`** unless it was a timeout. On `EPIPE` the process exits immediately with the current `process.exitCode`, so a driver that closes the pipe early may see a misleading `0`.

**Gotchas.**
- The prompt must be a single quoted argv token with whitespace; `cline fix the tests` and `cline hello` both fail.
- `--id` + `--json` is broken — **there is currently no working headless multi-turn** (#13239, #10856). Re-send full context per run or allocate a PTY.
- TUI requires `stdin.isTTY && stdout.isTTY` and emits no readiness handshake; a PTY driver must match rendered output.
- Wizards need a TTY (`cline mcp`, `cline connect`, bare `cline schedule`, bare `cline auth`); `cline history` without `--json` on a TTY silently opens the TUI.
- Signals: first `SIGINT` requests an abort (`run_abort_requested`), a second exits 0; **`SIGTERM` only requests an abort and never exits** — if `abortAll()` returns false it is a no-op. A supervisor must escalate to `SIGKILL`.
- Async hooks are spawned `detached: true` with stdio `ignore` and outlive the CLI unreaped; the hub daemon is separately long-lived (`cline hub stop`, `cline doctor fix`).
- MCP `initialize` is ~1500 ms and the per-server `timeout` does not apply — slow servers are silently dropped with zero tools.
- The TUI becomes unresponsive in a VS Code terminal after prolonged use (#13306); related #4356, #12417, #7906, #7938.
- Auto-update at launch unless `CLINE_NO_AUTO_UPDATE` or `autoUpdateEnabled: false`.
- Do not build a parser from the docs' `{"type":"say","ts":<number>}` table; parse `{"ts":"<ISO>","type":"agent_event","event":{…}}` and treat unknown top-level `type` as a no-op.
- `--json` swallows text output; errors go to stderr as JSON.
- `--data-dir` does not isolate MCP — a "sandboxed" run still launches the user's global MCP servers.
- `--zen` rejects `--data-dir` and `--tui` and, without the menubar app, gives no completion signal — poll `cline history`.
- Hooks silently fail open (timeout, non-JSON stdout, spawn error) and there is no `fail_closed`; `--yolo` disables hooks entirely — use `--auto-approve true` instead if hooks matter.

**Driver notes.**
- Window mode: PTY-drive `cline` with both stdin and stdout as terminals; this is the **only** way to resume a session, and there is no readiness marker so the driver must match rendered output.
- Session mode: not available — `--id` + `--json` is broken, so a headless driver must re-send full context each run or fall back to the PTY.
- Batch mode: `cline "<single quoted prompt>" --json --auto-approve true` (never `--yolo` if hooks are load-bearing), one process per task; key off the terminal `run_result` line, not `$?`, since aborts exit 0.
- Process supervision is mandatory: SIGTERM does not exit, async hooks are detached and unreaped, and the hub daemon is separate — kill the process group and run `cline doctor fix`.
- Isolate with `CLINE_DIR` + `CLINE_DATA_DIR` and set `CLINE_MCP_SETTINGS_PATH` explicitly, because `--data-dir` leaves MCP config global.
- Most stable surface: `--acp` (ACP over stdio) for a supervised driver; the `--json` NDJSON schema is source-accurate but contradicted by the published docs.

**Sources.**
- https://docs.cline.bot/cli/cli-reference
- https://docs.cline.bot/usage/acp.md
- https://docs.cline.bot/sdk/plugins
- https://github.com/cline/cline/blob/main/apps/cli/README.md
- https://github.com/cline/cline/issues/13239
- https://github.com/cline/cline/issues/12344

---

### Grok CLI / "Grok Build" (xAI, Apache-2.0 first-party code, v1.0.13 stable (alpha 1.0.24, enterprise 1.0.22), `curl -fsSL https://x.ai/cli/install.sh | bash`)

**Launch.** TUI: `grok` with no args (fullscreen, mouse-interactive, alt-screen). Headless: `-p, --single <PROMPT>`, `--prompt-json <JSON>`, or `--prompt-file <PATH>`. Stdin: **not supported as prompt input** — "Headless mode does not read piped stdin into the prompt"; stdin is the transport for hook scripts and ACP stdio only. SDK/server/ACP: `grok agent stdio` (ACP, JSON-RPC 2.0 over stdin/stdout; agent-level flags go between `agent` and the mode, e.g. `grok agent --always-approve --model grok-4.6 stdio`); WebSocket server `grok agent --always-approve serve --bind 127.0.0.1:2419 --secret <token>` (omitting `--secret` prints a generated one, or `GROK_AGENT_SECRET`; state survives client reconnects); WebSocket relay `grok agent --always-approve headless --grok-ws-url wss://…`; `--leader`/`--no-leader` (leader mode is **refused** when a non-`off` sandbox profile is requested). An official "grok-agent-sdk" is referenced by the hooks docs with no package name; third-party ACP SDKs are listed instead (TS `@agentclientprotocol/sdk`, Rust `agent-client-protocol`, Python, Go, Kotlin). Rust; binary `grok` (cargo name `xai-grok-pager`); Windows `irm https://x.ai/cli/install.ps1 | iex`; npm `@xai-official/grok`. Other subcommands: `login|logout|inspect|models|mcp|plugin|sessions|export|import|memory|worktree|dashboard|usage|du|wrap|update|version|completions|setup`.

**Input and follow-up.** argv `-p`/`--single` (plus `--verbatim` to send the prompt exactly as given), `--prompt-json <JSON>` content blocks, `--prompt-file <PATH>`. Each `grok -p` is a **fresh session by default**; follow-up pattern: `ID=$(grok -p "…" --output-format json | jq -r '.sessionId')` then `grok -p "…" --resume "$ID"`, or `grok -p "continue" -c` for the most recent session in the cwd. `-s, --session-id <UUID>` **creates a new** session with a caller-supplied UUID — it does not resume, errors on an invalid or in-use UUID, and with `-r`/`-c` requires `--fork-session`. `-r, --resume <ID_OR_TITLE>` accepts a title for the current directory (case-insensitive) — scripts should pass IDs. ACP multi-turn: `session/new` then repeated `session/prompt` with `prompt: [{type:"text", text:"…"}]`; assistant text arrives as `session/update` notifications, not in the `session/prompt` result (which returns `stopReason` and completion metadata).

**Output.** `--output-format <plain|json|streaming-json|streaming-messages-json>` plus `--include-partial-messages` (affects `streaming-messages-json` only); the public docs list three formats, the in-repo guide four. `json`: `{"text","stopReason","sessionId","requestId","num_turns","usage":{"input_tokens","cache_read_input_tokens","cache_creation_input_tokens","output_tokens","reasoning_tokens","total_tokens"},"modelUsage":{"grok-4.6":{"inputTokens","outputTokens","cacheReadInputTokens","modelCalls","costUSD"}},"total_cost_usd","total_cost_usd_ticks"}`; also possible `thought`, `cost_is_partial`, `usage_is_incomplete`; error `{"type":"error","message":"…"}` with non-zero exit. `usage.input_tokens` is **uncached only**; `total_tokens = input_tokens + cache_read_input_tokens + cache_creation_input_tokens + output_tokens`; when `cost_is_partial` is true **all** cost floats are omitted; `total_cost_usd_ticks` is exact integer (1 USD = 10^10 ticks). `streaming-json` NDJSON types: `text` (`data`), `thought` (`data`), `tool_call` (`toolCallId`, `title`, `kind`, `status`, `toolName`, `rawInput`, `content`, `locations`), `tool_call_update` (`toolCallId`, `status`, `rawOutput`, `content`, `locations`), `usage` (`messageId`, `stopReason`, `usage`, `signature`), `plan` (`entries`), `available_commands` (`tools`, `commands`), `end` (`stopReason`, `sessionId`, `requestId`, `usage`, `num_turns`, `modelUsage` — **always last**), `error` (`message`); `end.stopReason` ∈ `end_turn`, `max_tokens`, `max_turn_requests`, `refusal`, `cancelled`, plus `max_turns_reached`, `auto_compact_*`; **the list is explicitly non-exhaustive**; the final answer is the concatenation of `text` events (`end` carries no text). `streaming-messages-json` is NDJSON in Anthropic Messages API `stream-json` wire format: `system` (`subtype:"init"`/`"compact_boundary"`), `assistant`, `user`, `result`; final answer `result.result`; `init` carries `session_id`, `apiKeySource`, `model`, `cwd`, `permissionMode`, `tools[]`, `slash_commands[]`, `mcp_servers[]`, `skills[]`, `uuid`; `result` carries `subtype` (`success`, `error_max_turns`, `error_during_execution`, `error_max_structured_output_retries`), `is_error`, `duration_ms`, `duration_api_ms`, `num_turns`, `stop_reason`, `total_cost_usd`, `usage`, `modelUsage`, `errors[]`. Both streaming formats are **read-only** — approvals and bidirectional flows require ACP.

**Sessions.** `-s, --session-id <UUID>` (create only), `-r, --resume [<ID>]`, `-c, --continue`, `--fork-session`. Storage `~/.grok/sessions/<encoded-cwd>/<session-id>/` (cwd URL-encoded; over 255 bytes a slug+hash is used and the real path recorded in a `.cwd` file). Per-session files: `summary.json`, `updates.jsonl` (**authoritative** ACP session-update stream, drives `/resume`), `chat_history.jsonl`, `plan.json`, `rewind_points.jsonl`, `signals.json`, `feedback.jsonl`, `compaction_checkpoints/`, `subagents/`. JSONL is the source of truth; `grok sessions search` maintains a local SQLite FTS5 index over titles and prompts. Listing `grok sessions list [--limit N]` (default 20), `grok sessions search <query>`, `grok sessions delete <id>`, `grok export <session-id> [output]` (Markdown, `--clipboard`), `grok import [targets…]` (from Claude Code), `grok du`/`grok disk-usage --json`. Per-session usage `grok usage <session-id> [turn]` → `sessionId`, `updatedAt`, `session`, `turns`, `costUsdTicks`. TUI: `/resume`, `/sessions`, `/rename` (`/title`), `/fork [directive]`, `/rewind` (or `Esc Esc`), `/compact [context]`, `/share`, `/session-info`, `/context`. Auto-compaction `[session] auto_compact_threshold_percent = 85`.

**Hooks.** Events: `SessionStart`, `SessionEnd`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse`, `PostToolUseFailure`, `PermissionDenied`, `Stop`, `StopFailure`, `StopCancelled`, `Notification`, `SubagentStart`, `SubagentStop` (alias `SubagentEnd`), `PreCompact`, `PostCompact`; unrecognized names are skipped, not errors. Locations, all merged: global `~/.grok/hooks/*.json` (extra roots via `~/.grok/hooks-paths`), `~/.claude/settings.json` + `settings.local.json`, `~/.cursor/hooks.json` (always trusted); project `<project>/.grok/hooks/*.json`, `.claude/settings*.json`, `.cursor/hooks.json` (**require folder trust**); config `~/.grok/config.toml`, `managed_config.toml` (`$GROK_HOME` and `/etc/grok`), `requirements.toml` (user + system); plugin-bundled (per-plugin trust). Shape JSON `{"hooks":{"PreToolUse":[{"matcher":"Bash","hooks":[{"type":"command","command":"bin/safety-check.sh","timeout":10,"env":{"MY_SECRET":"value"}}]}]}}`, or structurally identical TOML `[[hooks.PreToolUse]]` with `matcher` and a `hooks = [ { type = "command", command = "…", timeout = 10 } ]` array. Config-layer hooks are additive across layers (deduplicated, highest-authority copy kept) and tagged by origin (`managed:`, `requirements/user:`, `user:`) in `/hooks`. Types: `"command"` (executable path relative to the JSON file, or inline shell) and `"http"` (POST); **no prompt type**. Payload (JSON on stdin) has a deliberate casing split: **`hookEventName` (camelCase key) carries grok's snake_case value (`pre_tool_use`); `hook_event_name` (snake_case key) carries Claude's PascalCase value (`PreToolUse`)**. Common fields: `hookEventName`, `hook_event_name`, `sessionId`, `cwd`, `workspaceRoot`, `timestamp`, `permissionMode` (`default`/`auto`/`plan`/`bypassPermissions`), `promptId` (absent for session-scoped events); tool events add `toolName`, `toolInput`, `toolUseId`, `toolInputTruncated`; `PostToolUse` adds `toolResult`/`toolResultTruncated`; `Stop`/`SubagentStop` add `stopHookActive` and `lastAssistantMessage` (clipped at 32,768 chars); `StopFailure` adds `error`, `errorDetails` (clipped 1000), `lastAssistantMessage`, `subagentType`; `StopCancelled` adds `reason`, `cancelledBy`, `cancelTrigger`, `reasonDetails`, `lastAssistantMessage`, `subagentType`. Hook process env: `GROK_HOOK_EVENT`, `GROK_HOOK_NAME`, `GROK_SESSION_ID`, `GROK_WORKSPACE_ROOT`, `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`; plugin hooks also get `GROK_PLUGIN_ROOT`, `GROK_PLUGIN_DATA`; `command` and `url` support `${VAR}`/`$VAR`. Decisions (stdout JSON): `PreToolUse` → `{"decision":"allow"|"deny"|"ask"|"defer","reason"}` or canonical `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"PreToolUse","permissionDecision":"deny","permissionDecisionReason":"…"}}` (`permissionDecision` wins; legacy `approve`/`block` accepted; **`allow` means "not blocked", it does not auto-approve**; `ask` forces the prompt; `defer` = no opinion); context injection `{"hookSpecificOutput":{…,"additionalContext":"…"}}` delivered **after** the call runs, wrapped in a system-reminder tag naming the hook, clipped at 10,000 chars; input rewrite `{"hookSpecificOutput":{…,"updatedInput":{…}}}` applied silently after all handlers (last writer wins; a rewrite failing the tool schema **blocks** the call); `PostToolUse` → `{"decision":"block","reason","hookSpecificOutput":{…,"additionalContext","updatedToolOutput"}}` (`updatedMCPToolOutput` is the MCP alias; replacement cap 64K chars); `Stop`/`SubagentStop` → `{"decision":"block","reason"}` keeps working, `{"continue":false,"stopReason":"…"}` force-stops, hard cap **8 continuations per turn**; `UserPromptSubmit` → `{"decision":"block","reason"}` or exit 2. Exit codes: `0` allow/success; `2` explicit deny (`PreToolUse`, first stderr line becomes the reason if JSON carries none) / block-stop with stderr as feedback (`Stop`, `SubagentStop`) / feedback to model (`PostToolUse`); anything else = **fail-open**, recorded as `exit code N: <first stderr line>`. A `deny` in stdout JSON is honored regardless of exit code. Timeouts: default **5 s**; **600 s** for `Stop`, `SubagentStop`, `PostToolUse`; **30 s** for `UserPromptSubmit`; `timeout` field in seconds. Matchers: a **regex** per event — tool name on tool events and `PermissionDenied`; notification type on `Notification`; subagent type on `SubagentStart`/`SubagentStop`; start source on `SessionStart` (`startup`, `resume`, …); end reason on `SessionEnd`; `manual`/`auto` on `PreCompact`/`PostCompact`; error class on `StopFailure` (`rate_limit`, `authentication_failed`, `invalid_request`, `server_error`, `max_output_tokens`, `unknown`); cancel reason on `StopCancelled` (`user_interrupt`, `permission_rejected`, `permission_cancelled`, `max_turns`, `no_progress`, `unknown`). A matcher on `Stop` or `UserPromptSubmit` is **ignored with a warning**; omitted/empty matches everything; MCP calls appear as `server__tool`; Claude aliases `Bash`→`run_terminal_command`, `Read`→`read_file`, `Edit`/`Write`/`MultiEdit`→`search_replace`, `Grep`→`grep`, `Glob`/`ListDir`→`list_dir`, `WebSearch`→`web_search`, `Task`→`spawn_subagent`. Trust: project hooks are **silently skipped** until the folder is trusted — grant with `/hooks-trust` or `--trust`, stored in `~/.grok/trusted_folders.toml`; one grant covers MCP, LSP, hooks, project instructions and project skills; disable the gate with `GROK_FOLDER_TRUST=0` or `[folder_trust] enabled = false`; also `/hooks-list`, `/hooks-add <path>`, `/hooks-remove <path>`, `/hooks-untrust`. HTTP hooks are **https-only**, enforced in `crates/codegen/xai-grok-hooks/src/runner/http.rs::validate_hook_url` — the recorded failure is `blocked by SSRF protection: only https:// URLs are allowed for HTTP hooks, got http://`, logged as `SSRF protection: blocked HTTP hook URL`; it is a hook *failure*, so it fails open. The same validator rejects private/internal IPs (RFC1918 `10.*`, `172.16-31.*`, `192.168.*`, link-local `169.254.*`, CGNAT `100.64-127.*`, unspecified, IPv6 `fe80::/10` and `fc00::/7`, IPv4-mapped equivalents) literally and after DNS resolution, but **loopback (`127.*`, `::1`) is explicitly allowed**; redirects are not followed (`Policy::none()`); body is a `Content-Type: application/json` POST of the full envelope; response body capped; URL validation bounded by the hook's `timeout`.

**MCP.** CLI: `grok mcp add filesystem -- npx -y @modelcontextprotocol/server-filesystem /path`, `grok mcp add --transport http linear https://mcp.linear.app/mcp`, `--header "Authorization: Bearer ${API_TOKEN}"`, `grok mcp add --scope project <name> -- <cmd>` (writes `./.grok/config.toml`), `grok mcp list [--json]`, `grok mcp remove <name>`, `grok mcp doctor [name] [--json]`. TOML config `[mcp_servers.<name>]` with `command`, `args`, `env`, `cwd`, `enabled`, `startup_timeout_sec` (default 30), `tool_timeout_sec` (default 6000), `tool_timeouts = {some_tool = 120}`; remote `url`, `headers`, `bearer_token_env_var`. `${VAR}` and `${VAR:-default}` expand at load in `url`, `command`, `args`, `env`, `headers`; headers also support `{{session_id}}`. Transports stdio and HTTP/SSE (`--transport http` documented; no separate `--transport sse`). OAuth native for `url` servers — first use triggers a browser flow, tokens in `~/.grok/mcp_credentials.json` (plaintext, `0600` on Unix); authenticate from the TUI with `i` in `/mcps`. Precedence `~/.grok/config.toml` < `<repo-root>/.grok/config.toml` < `<cwd>/.grok/config.toml` (walked cwd→git root); a project server with the same name **replaces** the user one entirely. Cross-vendor merge order `config.toml` > `~/.claude.json` > `.cursor/mcp.json` > project `.mcp.json`; disable with `[compat.claude] mcps = false` / `[compat.cursor] mcps = false` or `GROK_CLAUDE_MCPS_ENABLED`/`GROK_CURSOR_MCPS_ENABLED`. Tools namespaced `<server>__<tool>`; meta-tools `search_tool` and `use_tool` (toggled by `GROK_TOOL_SEARCH` / `[features] tool_search`, default on); filtering by permission rule `MCPTool(server__*)`; org-level `allowedMcpServers`, `deniedMcpServers`, `allowManagedMcpServersOnly`, `enableAllProjectMcpServers` in `requirements.toml`/`managed_config.toml` — blocked servers are dropped **at merge time, before spawn**, with log line `MCP server blocked by managed settings policy`. Subagents inherit the parent's servers; restrict with agent frontmatter `mcpInheritance` (`all`/`none`/`named`/`except`). Acts as MCP server: not documented anywhere — the embedding surface is ACP. Unreachable server: no documented hard failure; handshake bounded by `startup_timeout_sec` (global override `GROK_MCP_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_SECS` in **seconds** or Claude-compatible `MCP_TIMEOUT` in **milliseconds**, checked first; a per-server value still wins); stdio stderr captured to `~/.grok/logs/mcp/<server>.stderr.log` (truncated each launch); in `streaming-messages-json`, `init.mcp_servers[].status` is **always `"connected"`**.

**Extensibility.** Slash commands: `/help`, `/new`, `/resume`, `/fork`, `/rewind`, `/compact`, `/model`, `/effort`, `/plan`, `/view-plan`, `/auto`, `/always-approve`, `/tasks`, `/queue`, `/loop`, `/dashboard`, `/settings`, `/mcps`, `/hooks`, `/plugins`, `/skills`, `/marketplace`, `/agents`, `/personas`, `/export`, `/transcript`, `/terminal-setup`, `/usage`, `/privacy`, `/btw`, `/imagine`, `/deep-research`, `/create-workflow`, `/workflow`, `/workflows`; custom commands come from skills and `~/.agents/commands/`. Skills: `SKILL.md` folders discovered from `./.grok/skills/` (walked to repo root), `~/.grok/skills/`, enabled plugins' `skills/`, `[skills] paths`, `~/.agents/skills/`, plus Claude/Cursor skill dirs; bundled skills cached in `~/.grok/bundled/skills/`; frontmatter `name`, `description`, `when-to-use` (alias `when_to_use`), `paths` (gitignore globs), `allowed-tools` (advisory only), `argument-hint`, `user-invocable` (default true; **only the literal `true` counts**), `disable-model-invocation`, `metadata`; `model`, `effort`, `license`, `compatibility` are accepted and **not applied**; user-invocable skills surface as `/<skill-name>`, collisions as `/local:commit` / `/plugin:name`. Subagents: built-ins `general-purpose`, `explore` (read/list/search + shell, no edits), `plan` (no shell, no edits); definitions `.md` + frontmatter in `.grok/agents/` or `~/.grok/agents/`; the model calls `spawn_subagent` with `subagent_type`; master switch `[subagents] enabled` / `GROK_SUBAGENTS`, per-type `[subagents.toggle]`, per-type model routing `[subagents.models]`; **personas** are subagent-only behavioral overlays in `[subagents.personas]` or `.grok/personas/*.toml` / `~/.grok/personas/*.toml`; headless flags `--agent <NAME>`, `--agents <JSON>`, `--no-subagents`, `--disallowed-tools "Agent"` / `"Agent(explore)"`. Plugins from `./.grok/plugins/`, `~/.grok/plugins/`, `~/.grok/plugins/marketplaces/`, `[plugins] paths`, `--plugin-dir <PATH>`; they bundle skills, agents, hooks, MCP servers, LSP servers; `grok plugin list|install|uninstall|update|enable|disable|details|validate`, `grok plugin marketplace list|add|remove|update`; `~/.grok/plugins/` is auto-trusted, project plugins require `--trust`. Instruction files load global `~/.grok/` rules first, then every directory from repo root down to cwd, **deeper files win**; within each directory: `AGENTS.md`, `Agents.md`, `AGENT.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `Claude.md`, `CLAUDE.local.md`, plus every `*.md` in `.grok/rules/` (and `.claude/rules/`, `.cursor/rules/`); `.gitignore`-matched files are **skipped**; loaded in full with no size cap; **there is no `GROK.md`**; startup loading of project instructions requires folder trust; per-run overrides `--rules <TEXT>` (appended) and `--system-prompt-override <TEXT>` (replaces), with `--append-system-prompt`/`--system-prompt` as Claude aliases. Workflows: Rhai scripts at `.grok/workflows/<name>.rhai` or `~/.grok/workflows/`, authored with `/create-workflow`, launched `/workflow <name> [JSON args]` or `/<name>`; disable via `[workflows] enabled = false` or `GROK_WORKFLOWS=0`. `grok inspect [--json]` is the driver's pre-flight check: every contributing config file (including `managed_config.toml` and `requirements.toml` layers and which values won), each rules file with path and approximate token count, every skill with `name`/`description`/`source` (`project`/`user`/`bundled`/`config`/`server`/`plugin: <name>`)/`userInvocable` (disabled ones tagged `[disabled]`, collisions carrying `collidesWith` and `invocableAs`), every plugin, every loaded MCP server with vendor origin (`[cursor]`, `[claude]`), `allowedMcpServers`/`deniedMcpServers`, `allowManagedMcpServersOnly` state (`off`/`advisory`/`enforced`), marketplace pins, loaded hooks, and which login policy loaded.

**Permissions.** Modes: `default`/ask (read-only tools + built-in read-only shell commands); `acceptEdits` (`--permission-mode acceptEdits`); `plan`; `auto` (`/auto`, `Shift+Tab`, `--permission-mode auto` — classifier-driven, dangerous calls still prompt); `dontAsk` (only pre-approved tools + read-only handling, **silently denies everything else**); `bypassPermissions`/always-approve (`--always-approve`, `--yolo`, `--dangerously-skip-permissions`, `--permission-mode bypassPermissions`, `Ctrl+O`, `/always-approve`, `Shift+Tab`). `Shift+Tab` cycles Normal → Plan → Auto → Always-approve; always-approve and auto are mutually exclusive (always-approve wins); ACP uses `session/new` `_meta: {"yoloMode": true}` or `{"autoMode": true}`. Config default is **user config only** (`[ui] permission_mode = "auto"` ∈ `default|ask|auto|always-approve`); legacy `approval_mode` and `yolo = true` still work, `permission_mode` wins. Plan mode `/plan [description]`, `Shift+Tab`, `--no-plan`; only the session plan file may be edited until approval and **auto and always-approve do not skip plan review**; it gates edit tools, not the shell. Rules `[permission] allow/deny/ask` lists with prefixes `Bash(…)`, `Edit(…)`, `Write(…)`, `Read(…)`, `Grep(…)`, `WebFetch(…)` (glob or `domain:host`), `MCPTool(…)`, or `rules = [{action, tool, pattern}]` with `action` ∈ `allow|deny|ask` and `tool` ∈ `any|bash|edit|read|grep|mcp|webfetch`; evaluation order **deny > ask > allow**; CLI `--allow <RULE>`/`--deny <RULE>` (Claude aliases `--allowedTools`/`--disallowedTools`). Two traps: allow rules are **conjunctive per shell segment** (`Bash(git *)` approves `git status && git diff` but not `git status && rm -rf /`), and allow rules are **not a closed allowlist** — an unmatched command falls through to the mode, not to denial; for deny-by-default use `dontAsk`. Headless-only tool removal `--tools <LIST>` / `--disallowed-tools <LIST>` over internal IDs (`run_terminal_cmd`/`run_terminal_command`, `read_file`, `search_replace`, `grep`, `list_dir`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`, `spawn_subagent`); `--disallowed-tools` wins; in the TUI these warn and are ignored. Sandbox off by default, Landlock on Linux / Seatbelt on macOS: `off` (unrestricted), `workspace` (write cwd, `~/.grok/`, temp; network allowed), `devbox` (write top-level dirs except `/data`), `read-only` (write `~/.grok/` + temp, network blocked), `strict` (read cwd + system paths, write cwd/`~/.grok/`/temp, network blocked); enable via `--sandbox <PROFILE>`, `[sandbox] profile`, `GROK_SANDBOX=workspace`, or a `requirements.toml` pin (which can override the CLI); `[sandbox] auto_allow_bash` / `GROK_SANDBOX_AUTO_ALLOW_BASH=1` skips bash prompts inside a sandbox; custom profiles in `~/.grok/sandbox.toml` or `.grok/sandbox.toml` (`extends`, `restrict_network`, `read_only`, `read_write`, `deny`); kernel-level read-deny on Linux needs **bubblewrap**; child-network restriction is Linux-only; `~/.grok/` stays writable; built-ins do **not** protect `~/.ssh`; a non-`off` profile refuses leader mode. Trust: `--trust` grants folder trust non-interactively, `/hooks-trust`/`/hooks-untrust` in-session, store `~/.grok/trusted_folders.toml` (pre-seed it), gate off via `GROK_FOLDER_TRUST=0` or `[folder_trust] enabled = false`; plugin trust `grok plugin install <source> --trust`; first approval-row preselection `GROK_DEFAULT_SELECTED_PERMISSION` / `[ui] default_selected_permission` (`always_allow_all_sessions` default, `allow_command_always`, `allow_once`, `reject`). Enterprise lock `[ui] disable_bypass_permissions_mode = true` in `requirements.toml` blocks `--yolo`, `bypassPermissions`, `Ctrl+O`, `/always-approve`, the `Shift+Tab` cycle, client-supplied yolo settings and catch-all `*`/`**` allow rules — honored only from root-owned `/etc/grok/requirements.toml`.

**Environment.** `GROK_HOME` (default `~/.grok`) is the HOME override for config, auth, sessions, skills, plugins and logs. `XAI_API_KEY`; `GROK_DEFAULT_MODEL`, `GROK_WEB_SEARCH_MODEL`, `GROK_MODELS_BASE_URL`, `GROK_MODELS_LIST_URL`, `GROK_XAI_API_BASE_URL` (`https://api.x.ai/v1`); `GROK_DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER`; `GROK_SANDBOX`, `GROK_SANDBOX_AUTO_ALLOW_BASH`; `GROK_FOLDER_TRUST`; `GROK_AGENT` (default `grok-build`), `GROK_AGENT_SECRET`, `GROK_AGENT_DASHBOARD`; feature toggles `GROK_SUBAGENTS`, `GROK_MEMORY`, `GROK_WEB_FETCH`, `GROK_LSP_TOOLS` (default 0), `GROK_WRITE_FILE`, `GROK_TOOL_SEARCH` (default 1), `GROK_WORKFLOWS`, `GROK_RESPECT_GITIGNORE`; `GROK_WEB_FETCH_PROXY`; `GROK_MCP_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_SECS` / `MCP_TIMEOUT`; `GROK_LOG_FILE`, `RUST_LOG`; `GROK_CRASH_HANDLER`; `GROK_OIDC_ISSUER`, `GROK_OIDC_CLIENT_ID`, `GROK_AUTH_PROVIDER_COMMAND`, `GROK_DISABLE_API_KEY_AUTH`; installer-only `GROK_CHANNEL`, `GROK_BIN_DIR`; `HTTPS_PROXY`/`HTTP_PROXY`/`NO_PROXY`, `GROK_POOL_IDLE_TIMEOUT_SECS` (90 s); vendor scanners `GROK_{CURSOR,CLAUDE}_{SKILLS,RULES,AGENTS,MCPS,HOOKS}_ENABLED`; `GROK_EXTERNAL_OTEL`, `OTEL_*`; UI `GROK_THEME`, `GROK_SHOW_THINKING_BLOCKS`, `GROK_SCROLL_*`, `GROK_DEFAULT_SELECTED_PERMISSION`, `GROK_REMEMBER_TOOL_APPROVALS`, `GROK_MOUSE_REPORTING_TOGGLE`. Settings precedence lowest→highest: `/etc/grok/managed_config.toml`, `~/.grok/managed_config.toml`, `~/.grok/config.toml`, `~/.grok/requirements.toml`, `/etc/grok/requirements.toml` (highest; fail-closed pins env and CLI cannot override); project `.grok/config.toml` contributes **only** `[mcp_servers]`, `[plugins]`, `[permission]`, `[mcp] max_output_bytes`; env vars sit between as session/CI overrides; CLI overrides config except where a `requirements.toml` pin applies; Claude `managed-settings.json` read for a subset; all layers support `[[version_overrides]]` and `$VAR` expansion; `fail_closed = true` refuses to start when `requirements.toml` is missing or its signature does not verify. Other files `~/.grok/sandbox.toml`, `~/.grok/pager.toml` (`[terminal] alt_screen = "always"`), `~/.grok/trusted_folders.toml`, `~/.grok/hooks-paths`. Model `-m, --model`, `/model` (`/m`), `GROK_DEFAULT_MODEL`, `[models] default`; reasoning `--effort`/`--reasoning-effort` ∈ `none|minimal|low|medium|high|xhigh|max` or `/effort`; BYOK `[model.<id>]` with `model`, `base_url`, `name`, `env_key`/`api_key`, `api_backend` (`chat_completions`/`responses`/`messages`), `context_window`, `extra_headers`, `stream_tool_calls`, `supports_backend_search`; restrict with `[models] allowed_models`/`hidden_models`/`disabled_models`. Auth per model: `model.api_key` > `model.env_key` > active session token > `XAI_API_KEY`; methods browser OIDC (`grok login`), device code (`grok login --device-auth`, RFC 8628), `[auth] auth_provider_command`, API key; required hosts `cli-chat-proxy.grok.com`, `auth.x.ai`.

**Observability.** Logs: `GROK_LOG_FILE=<path>` (verbatim) + `RUST_LOG=debug`; in headless, logs go to **stderr** by default; `~/.grok/logs/` (`unified.jsonl`); `~/.grok/logs/mcp/<server>.stderr.log`; `~/.grok/crash/` with `GROK_CRASH_HANDLER=1`; `~/.grok/trace-exports/`. **Update-check messages go to stderr; stdout stays clean for `--output-format json`.** Usage/cost: `grok usage <session-id> [turn]` (`costUsdTicks`, 10^10 ticks/USD); headless `json`/`end`/`result` carry `usage`, `num_turns`, `modelUsage` (`inputTokens`, `outputTokens`, `cacheReadInputTokens`, `modelCalls`, `costUSD`, `webSearchRequests`, `contextWindow`), `total_cost_usd`, `total_cost_usd_ticks`, plus `cost_is_partial`/`usage_is_incomplete` — **absence of cost means unreported, never free**; TUI `/usage`, `/context`, `/session-info`, `grok du --json`. External OTel: `[telemetry] otel_enabled` / `GROK_EXTERNAL_OTEL` with a full OTLP surface (`otel_endpoint`, `otel_protocol` `http/protobuf`/`grpc`, per-signal endpoints/protocols/certs, mTLS, `otel_timeout`, `otel_metric_export_interval`, `otel_metrics_include_session_id`, content gates `otel_log_user_prompts`/`otel_log_tool_details`); meter scope `ai.xai.grok_code`; events are OTLP log records (e.g. `grok_code.user_prompt`); product telemetry `[features] telemetry` = `boolean`/`session_metrics`/`off` (enterprise default off); a documented startup suppression window exists. Exit codes (headless): `0` success; `1` error (auth failure, network error, runtime error); `130` SIGINT; `143` SIGTERM.

**Gotchas.**
- **stdin is not the prompt channel** — piping into `grok -p` does nothing; use `--prompt-file` or command substitution.
- Session flags are easy to get wrong: `-s` creates, `-r`/`-c` resume; `-r` matches a *title* when the value is not UUID-shaped and errors on ambiguous duplicates.
- Project hooks are silently skipped until the folder is trusted; pre-answer with `--trust`, a pre-seeded `trusted_folders.toml`, or `GROK_FOLDER_TRUST=0`.
- First launch with no cached credential opens a browser; set `XAI_API_KEY` or pre-run `grok login --device-auth`. ACP clients must call `authenticate` with `methodId` `xai.api_key` or `cached_token` (pass `_meta: {headless: true}`) before `session/new`.
- The TUI takes the alternate screen by default (`--no-alt-screen`, `[ui] screen_mode`, `[terminal] alt_screen = "always"`); grok intentionally runs inline under Zellij and `tmux -CC`; no readiness marker — prefer ACP or `-p --output-format streaming-json` over screen-scraping.
- Auto-updater: `--no-auto-update`, `GROK_DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1`, or `[cli] auto_update = false`; non-TTY stderr auto-disables it.
- Signals SIGINT→130, SIGTERM→143; session state is saved to the last completed tool call but **file modifications are not rolled back**; an interrupt while a `Stop` hook runs kills it mid-flight and the turn reports `StopCancelled`.
- At session exit grok waits half a second for queued turn-end hooks, then drops the rest and aborts any still running; general child-reaping is undocumented (background commands, monitors, `/loop`, MCP stdio servers, subagent sessions, `grok wrap` PTYs).
- Project root discovery walks up from `--cwd` to the nearest `.git` — pointing `--cwd` inside a monorepo makes startup slow.
- Read-only `~/.grok` is supported but session persistence fails **silently** (ephemeral) and update checks warn and skip.
- SSE per-chunk idle timeout is 600 s and the HTTP pool holds idle connections 90 s — proxies need idle timeouts of at least 10 minutes.
- Headless-only flags warn and no-op in the TUI: `--tools`, `--disallowed-tools`, `--max-turns`, `--agents`.
- Treat the `streaming-json` `type` list as non-exhaustive; in `streaming-messages-json` `uuid` is not a correlation key, `init.mcp_servers[].status` is always `"connected"`, and an all-zero `result.usage` means unknown.
- HTTP hook endpoints must be https and must not resolve to a private IP (loopback exempt); a violation is a fail-open hook failure, not a visible startup error.
- Worktrees leak — ending or deleting a session leaves `~/.grok/worktrees/<repo>/<name>`; `grok worktree gc --max-age 7d` runs only when invoked.
- `--always-approve` is not total: deny rules, `PreToolUse` hooks, admin locks and plan-mode review still apply.

**Driver notes.**
- Window mode: PTY-drive `grok`, or run inline with `--no-alt-screen`; there is no readiness marker, so treat the TUI as a last resort.
- Session mode: `grok -p "…" --resume <UUID> --output-format streaming-json`, one process per turn; capture the id from the first run's `json` `sessionId` or `end.sessionId`, and always pass IDs (never titles).
- Batch mode: `grok -p --prompt-file <path> --trust --permission-mode dontAsk --allow <rules> --tools <ids> --output-format json`, with `--sandbox workspace` where isolation matters (but not with leader mode); exit codes are trustworthy (0/1/130/143).
- Pre-flight every workspace with `grok inspect --json` — it is the only way to see which config layers, hooks, skills and MCP servers actually loaded, and whether trust gated any of them.
- Deny-by-default requires `dontAsk`; allow rules alone fall through to the mode, and are conjunctive per shell segment.
- Most stable surface: `grok agent stdio` (ACP) for bidirectional control and approvals; the streaming formats are read-only and their `type` lists are explicitly non-exhaustive.

**Sources.**
- https://docs.x.ai/build/cli/headless-scripting.md
- https://docs.x.ai/build/cli/reference.md
- https://docs.x.ai/build/features/hooks.md
- https://docs.x.ai/build/features/permissions.md
- https://docs.x.ai/build/features/mcp-servers.md
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/xai-org/grok-build/main/crates/codegen/xai-grok-hooks/src/runner/http.rs

### Antigravity CLI / `agy` (Google, **no license file** — closed-source prebuilt binary, repo has no source code, v1.1.27, `curl -fsSL https://antigravity.google/cli/install.sh | bash`)

**Launch.** TUI: `agy` with no arguments — Bubble Tea, `altScreenMode` `default` (adaptive) / `always` / `never`; adaptive auto-degrades to inline over SSH and in non-interactive sessions. Headless: `agy -p "<prompt>"` (aliases `--print`, `--prompt`); response → stdout, errors/auth prompts/progress/permission notices → stderr; combine with `--output-format {text|json|stream-json}`. Interactive-with-seed `-i`/`--prompt-interactive` (mutually exclusive with `--print`). Stdin: plain text **not supported** for the prompt (`agy -p` deliberately no longer reads stdin when a prompt flag is given); NDJSON stdin via `--input-format stream-json` (requires `--output-format stream-json`) gives real multi-turn in one process. SDK/server/ACP: SDK is a separate Python package (`pip install google-antigravity`, `from google.antigravity import Agent, LocalAgentConfig`, repo `google-antigravity/antigravity-sdk-python`) plus a managed cloud REST/gRPC API; `agy remote-control start|status|stop` installs a **headless daemon as an OS service** (systemd user service / LaunchAgent / Scheduled Task) exposing the machine to the browser dashboard over a reverse tunnel — **not** a local API (1.1.22 removed a banner advertising `http://localhost:<port>` because "it was never a supported way to connect"). **ACP: none** — no `--acp` flag, open FR #31; a third-party `agy-acp` adapter runs the official binary in a PTY. Language Go; installs to `~/.local/bin/agy` (Windows `%LOCALAPPDATA%\agy\bin`); installer flags `--skip-aliases`, `--skip-path`; background self-updater with state in `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/updater/`, disabled by `AGY_CLI_DISABLE_AUTO_UPDATE=true`; no `agy upgrade` subcommand (FR #973). Subcommands `agy models`, `agy agents`, `agy plugin|plugins list|install <path|github>|enable|disable|uninstall|import gemini`, `agy mcp add|remove|list|enable|disable` (`--type`, `--env`, `--header`), `agy remote-control`, `agy mic-serve`. **`--output-format` is a global flag and must precede the subcommand** (`agy --output-format json models` works; `agy models --output-format json` is rejected — #777, #937). `--permission-mode` does not exist (use `--mode`). Other launch flags: `--json-schema`, `--model <slug>`, `--effort low|medium|high`, `--agent <name>`, `-c/--continue`, `--conversation <id>`, `--mode default|accept-edits|plan`, `--project <id|name>`, `--new-project`, `--sandbox`, `--dangerously-skip-permissions`, `--print-timeout <duration>` (default 5m), `--log-file <path>`, `--remote-control`.

**Input and follow-up.** argv `-p`/`--print`/`--prompt`; `@path` mentions inside a prompt pull file contents (respects `.antigravityignore`); no `--prompt-file` (FR #525); `Ctrl+V` pastes images/audio/video into the TUI. NDJSON stdin lines: `{"event":"user","message":{"content":"…"}}` or `{"event":"user","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"…"}]}}` — `content` takes a string **or** an array of blocks, and `text` is the **only** supported block type (any other ends the session with exit `1`). Three multi-turn options: `agy -p "…" -c` (new process, most recent conversation for the cwd); `agy -p "…" --conversation <uuid>`; `--input-format stream-json` — one process, one `user` line per turn, read events until that turn's `result`, then write the next line; close stdin to end gracefully (the process finishes the current turn and still emits the final `result`; clean sessions exit `0`). Slash commands handled by the CLI itself (`/model`, `/usage`) **cannot** be sent into a stream-json session — they produce `status: "ERROR"` and exit `2`.

**Output.** `--output-format text|json|stream-json`. `json` is one single-line envelope: `conversation_id` (UUID, empty string on early failures), `status` (`SUCCESS`/`ERROR`/`CANCELED`/`INTERRUPTED`/`INVALID`/`WAITING`/`RUNNING`), `response`, `error` (failure only), `duration_seconds`, `num_turns`, `structured_output` and `json_schema` (with `--json-schema`), `denied_actions` (added 1.1.27, shape undocumented), `usage` (`input_tokens`, `output_tokens`, `thinking_tokens`, `cache_read_tokens`, `total_tokens`). `stream-json` NDJSON: exactly one `init`, then any number of `step_update`, then exactly one `result` **per turn**; every line has an `event` field and the payload sits under a key of the same name — `{"event":"init","conversation_id":"…","init":{"cwd":"…","tools":["ask_permission","run_command","write_to_file",…],"permission_mode":"request-review"}}`, `{"event":"step_update","step_update":{"conversation_id","step_index":0,"state":"DONE","step_type":"user_input"}}`, `{"event":"result","result":{"conversation_id","status":"SUCCESS","response":"…","duration_seconds":6.88,"num_turns":1,"usage":{…}}}`. `init` payload: `cwd`, `tools[]`, `permission_mode` (`request-review` default, `always-proceed` under `--dangerously-skip-permissions`), plus `model`/`agent`/`json_schema` only when the matching flag is set. `step_update` payload: `conversation_id`, `step_index` (0-based), `state` (`ACTIVE`|`DONE`), `step_type` (`user_input`, `agent_response`, `tool`, `checkpoint`), `tool_name`, `text_delta`, `duration_seconds`, `usage`, `tool_info` (`{name, parameters, output}` plus `error:{type,message}`), `subagent_info` (`subagents[]` with `type_name`, `role`, `conversation_id`, `log_uri`, `workspace_uris`). Final answer: `result.response` (stream-json) or top-level `response` (json); text mode = all of stdout; streaming text arrives as `agent_response` `step_update`s with `text_delta` in `ACTIVE`, ending `DONE` (`jq -j 'select(.event=="step_update") | .step_update.text_delta // empty'`). In a stream-json session `response` is that turn only, while `num_turns`, `usage`, `duration_seconds` are cumulative. Structured output `--json-schema '<schema>'`, a path to a `.json` file, or a primitive type name — parsed value in `structured_output`, `response` holds the same payload serialized. Read-only slash commands also work in print mode without starting a turn or spending quota: `-p "/permissions"`, `/hooks`, `/help`, `/changelog`, `/config`, `/usage`, `/quota`, `/credits`, `/model`, `/effort`, `/skills`.

**Sessions.** Identifier `conversation_id`, a UUID, in every `json`/`stream-json` payload and in the status-line JSON (where `session_id` is a back-compat alias). Flags `--continue`/`-c`, `--conversation <id>`, `--project <project_id|name>`/`--new-project` (default project `default-cli-project`; resuming uses the conversation's own project). In-TUI `/resume` (`/switch`, `/conversation`), `/fork` (`/branch`), `/rename <name>`, `/rewind` (`/undo`), `/clear` (`/new`); picker keys: type to filter, up/down, left/right paging, `Enter` load, `F2` rename, `Ctrl+Delete` delete, `Ctrl+F` flat vs workspace-grouped, `Tab` to the **Antigravity** tab to import a desktop conversation, `Esc` close; default view `pickerGrouping`. History is scoped to the launch directory. Storage: `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/conversations/*.db` (SQLite with `.db-wal`/`.db-shm`; schema undocumented and explicitly unsupported for direct reads, #602; WAL checkpointed on exit since 1.1.26, clean print-mode exits still retain ~2 MB WAL/SHM per conversation, #683); transcript `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/brain/<conversationId>/.system_generated/logs/transcript.jsonl` (handed to hooks as `transcriptPath`, to status-line scripts as `transcript_path`); artifacts `artifactDirectoryPath` = `<app_data_dir>/brain/<conversationId>`; subagent worktrees `<conversation dir>/.system_generated/worktrees`; `-c` resolution cache `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/cache/last_conversations.json`, a map of absolute workspace path → conversation UUID. **Listing sessions non-interactively: none** — no `agy conversations list` (FR #602).

**Hooks.** Events (complete): `PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse`, `PreInvocation`, `PostInvocation`, `Stop`. There is **no** `SessionStart`, `SessionEnd`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `Notification` or `SubagentStop`; unrecognized event keys are **silently dropped** with only an info-level log line (#463; `SessionStart` requested in #809). Config: `hooks.json` at `.agents/hooks.json` (workspace), `~/.gemini/config/hooks.json` (global), or `<plugin>/hooks.json`; `/docs/cli/plugins` also claims hooks can live in the primary `settings.json` but names no key. Shape is a map of hook *name* → event configuration, e.g. `{"my-linter-hook":{"PostToolUse":[{"matcher":"run_command","hooks":[{"type":"command","command":"./scripts/lint.sh","timeout":10}]}]},"safety-gate":{"enabled":false,"PreToolUse":[…]},"reminder":{"PreInvocation":[{"type":"command","command":"./scripts/reminder.sh"}]}}`. **Two entry shapes**: tool events (`PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse`) use the nested `{matcher, hooks:[…]}` form; non-tool events (`PreInvocation`, `PostInvocation`, `Stop`) put the handler **flat on the entry** — using the nested form there fails with `command hook must specify 'command'` and the hook silently never runs (#925, confirmed on 1.1.24). Hook types: `type` optional, `"command"` the only supported value — no HTTP, no prompt. Timeouts: `timeout` in **seconds**, integer, default `30`. Matchers: regex on `PreToolUse`/`PostToolUse` only, against the tool name (`""` or `"*"` = all, `"run_command"`, `"run_command|view_file"`, `"browser_.*"`); ignored for `PreInvocation`, `PostInvocation`, `Stop`. Tool names available to matchers: `view_file`, `write_to_file`, `replace_file_content`, `multi_replace_file_content`, `list_dir`, `find_by_name`, `grep_search`, `search_web`, `read_url_content`, `run_command`, `manage_task`, `schedule`, `list_permissions`, `ask_permission`, `invoke_subagent`, `define_subagent`, `send_message`, `ask_question`, `generate_image`. `"enabled": false` disables a hook (default true). I/O: JSON on stdin, JSON on stdout, field names **camelCase** — differing from the snake_case of the headless event stream and status-line payload. Common inputs on every event: `conversationId`, `workspacePaths[]`, `transcriptPath`, `artifactDirectoryPath`, `modelName`. Per event — `PreToolUse` in `toolCall.name`, `toolCall.args`, `stepIdx`, out `decision` (**required**), `reason`, `permissionOverrides[]`; `PostToolUse` in `toolCall`, `stepIdx`, `error` (empty on success), out `{}`; `PreInvocation` in `invocationNum` (0-indexed), `initialNumSteps`, out `injectSteps`; `PostInvocation` same in, out `injectSteps` + `terminationBehavior`; `Stop` in `executionNum`, `terminationReason` (`model_stop`, `max_steps_exceeded`, `error`), `error`, `fullyIdle` (**required**; false while background tasks run), out `decision` (**required**), `reason`. `PreToolUse` `decision` values: `"allow"` (auto-allow), `"deny"` (hard block), `"ask"` (prompt, respecting "Always Allow"), `"force_ask"` (always prompt, ignoring cached permissions), `"deny_unless_prior_grant"`. `permissionOverrides` uses the permissions engine's `action(target)` syntax, e.g. `["read_file(/path)","command(npm test)"]`. Context injection: `PreInvocation`/`PostInvocation` return `injectSteps`, an array whose objects each carry exactly one of `toolCall`, `userMessage` (string), or `ephemeralMessage` (string) — e.g. `{"injectSteps":[{"ephemeralMessage":"Remember to lint"}]}`; `PostInvocation` also returns `terminationBehavior` ∈ `"force_continue"`, `"terminate"`, `""`. Preventing a stop: `Stop` returns `{"decision":"continue","reason":"Not done yet"}` to re-enter the loop (any other decision allows the stop); `reason` is injected as a system message. Known defects, all open: hooks in `.agents/hooks.json` load but never execute when authenticated via `GEMINI_API_KEY` in headless `-p` (#893); `/hooks` does not display workspace `.agents/hooks.json` entries (#562); `PreToolUse` deny is ignored for `invoke_subagent` (#640); `injectSteps` with a `toolCall` fails `unknown injected step type: <nil>` (#728); hooks do not fire at all on Windows and reportedly macOS for some users (#222, #257, #49); `injectSteps` unavailable on tool events (#808).

**MCP.** Config files carry a top-level `mcpServers` object (**not** nested inside `settings.json`, which is a Gemini CLI legacy layout): global `~/.gemini/config/mcp_config.json`, workspace `<workspace>/.agents/mcp_config.json`, plugin `<plugin>/mcp_config.json`. JSONC tolerated (`//`, `/* */`, trailing commas since 1.1.24; UTF-8 BOM on Windows since 1.1.23). Per-server, exactly one transport key required: `command` (stdio) or **`serverUrl`** (remote Streamable HTTP / SSE / websocket) — the Gemini CLI keys `url` and `httpUrl` are documented as unsupported, though changelog 1.0.x and 1.1.16 mention `url`; treat `serverUrl` as supported. Optional `args`, `env`, `cwd`, `headers`, `authProviderType` (`"google_credentials"`), `oauth` (`{clientId, clientSecret}`), `disabled`, `disabledTools`. CLI `agy mcp add|remove|list|enable|disable` with `--type`, `--env`, `--header` (1.1.16); the exact `agy mcp add` grammar is undocumented; in-TUI `/mcp` opens the MCP Manager Overlay with live status rings, manual reload and connection logs. OAuth automatic for DCR servers; otherwise supply `oauth: {clientId, clientSecret}` and register redirect URI **`https://antigravity.google/oauth-callback`**; tokens at `~/.gemini/antigravity/mcp_oauth_tokens.json`, refreshed automatically; Google ADC via `authProviderType: "google_credentials"`. Scopes: global / workspace / plugin — **no `--mcp-config` or `--no-mcp` per-invocation flag** (FR #342). Filtering: `disabledTools` (string[]); `enabledTools`, `timeoutSeconds` and `tools.eager` are recognized fields named in the 1.1.16 changelog but undescribed; no `includeTools`/`excludeTools`; otherwise use the permissions engine (`mcp(server/tool)`, `mcp(server/*)`, `mcp(*)`, defaulting to Ask). Acts as MCP server: none. Unreachable server: a per-server launch timeout is configurable and `-1` disables it, and a single malformed entry no longer takes down the rest (1.1.14) — **but if a stdio server's subprocess exits or crashes during initialization the connection manager misses the closed pipe and blocks the whole prompt loop for a hardcoded 5 minutes** (#657, log `MCP: 1 server(s) still connecting after 5m0s`); PowerShell stdio servers also fail on reload with exit status 1 (#644).

**Extensibility.** Skills: folder + `SKILL.md` with YAML frontmatter (`name` optional, defaults to folder name; `description` **required**; `metadata.icon`), locations `<workspace>/.agents/skills/<skill>/SKILL.md` and global, optional `scripts/`, `examples/`, `resources/`, progressive disclosure; **registered skills become slash commands** (`/format-tests`); browse with `/skills`. Documented path conflict: `/docs/skills` says global skills live at `~/.gemini/config/skills/<skill-folder>/`, while `/docs/cli/plugins` and `/docs/cli/gcli-migration` say `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/skills/` (#144; #103 reports `~/.agents/skills` not picked up). There is **no TOML command format** — Gemini CLI's TOML commands are converted to skills by `agy plugin import gemini`. Subagents/custom agents: markdown + YAML frontmatter at `.agents/agents/<name>.md` or `.agents/agents/<name>/agent.md` (workspace) and `~/.gemini/config/agents/<name>/agent.md` (global); frontmatter `subagent: true`, `skills:`, `rules:`, `agents:` (dependencies, 1.1.27), `model:` (`inherit` supported), `inheritCustomizations`; select `--agent <name>` or `/agents`, list `agy agents`; runtime tools `invoke_subagent`, `define_subagent`, `manage_subagents`, `send_message`. Plugins (Gemini CLI "extensions" renamed) staged at `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/plugins/<plugin_name>/` containing required `plugin.json` plus optional `mcp_config.json`, `hooks.json`, `rules.json` (1.1.15), `skills/`, `agents/`, `rules/`, `sidecars/`; `plugin.json` schema `https://antigravity.google/schemas/v1/plugin.json` (`name` required `^[a-zA-Z0-9-_]+$`, `description`, `additionalProperties: false`); tracking manifest `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/import_manifest.json`. Sidecars: long-running background processes from `sidecar.json` in `~/.gemini/config/sidecars/<id>/` or `<plugin>/sidecars/<name>/` with `command` xor `builtin` (`"schedule"`), `args`, `restart_policy` (`always`|`on-failure`|`never`), `description`, `env`, `display_name`; enabled in `~/.gemini/config/config.json` under `sidecars.<id>.enabled`; runtime data `~/.gemini/antigravity/sidecar_data/<sidecarId>/{data,logs,events}` with `data/` exposed as `ANTIGRAVITY_EXECUTABLE_DATA_DIR`; sidecars get an `agentapi` binary on PATH (`agentapi new-conversation <prompt>`, `agentapi send-message <conversation_id> <prompt>`) — **the closest thing to a programmatic "create a conversation" API, and only available to sidecar processes**. Instruction files: workspace `GEMINI.md` and `AGENTS.md` (both parsed), global `~/.gemini/GEMINI.md` (always applied), `.agents/rules/` (fallback `.agent/rules/`) markdown rules max 12,000 chars each with activation modes Manual (@-mention), Always On, Model Decision, Glob; `@filename` inside a rule resolves relative to the rule file, then absolute, then repo root; `.antigravityignore` excludes paths from `@` completion. **Precedence is not documented as an ordered list**; two partial data points — an explicitly configured skill/plugin path wins a name collision against auto-discovered customizations (1.1.21), and a rule named via an agent's `rules:` frontmatter always applies (1.1.15); open defects #396 (file-based rules from in-plugin `rules/`, `plugin.json` `"rules"`, and `~/.gemini/rules` never injected), #714 (memory-import processing ignored), #591 (`AGENTS.md` file references not followed). Slash commands: `/add-dir`, `/agents`, `/artifact`, `/boost`, `/btw`, `/clear` (`/new`), `/config` (`/settings`), `/context`, `/copy`, `/credits`, `/diff`, `/exit` (`/quit`), `/feedback`, `/fork` (`/branch`), `/help`, `/hooks`, `/keybindings`, `/logout`, `/mcp`, `/model`, `/open`, `/permissions`, `/plan`, `/rename`, `/resume` (`/switch`, `/conversation`), `/rewind` (`/undo`), `/skills`, `/statusline`, `/tasks`, `/teamwork-preview` (`/teamwork`), `/title`, `/usage` (`/quota`), `/voice` (`/record`); `/planning` and `/fast` were removed in 1.1.0 though the reference table still lists them.

**Permissions.** Execution modes `--mode` (`Shift+Tab` cycles `default → accept-edits → plan → default`, persisted as `agentMode`): `default` pauses for an interactive line-level diff review before creating/modifying files; `accept-edits` auto-approves file edits and creations and subagents inherit it; `plan` prepends the `/plan` prefix for read-only investigation. Diff-review keys `y` accept, `n` reject, `f` full-screen diff, `Ctrl+G` open in `$EDITOR`, or type instructions to reject with feedback. Tool permission presets (`toolPermission`, default `"request-review"`, also `/permissions`): `request-review` (prompts before write/bash/web tools), `proceed-in-sandbox` (auto-runs sandboxed commands, prompts to escape), `strict` (prompts for all non-read tools), `always-proceed` (never prompts; since 1.1.21 also auto-approves MCP tool calls and page reads). `artifactReviewPolicy`: `asks-for-review` (default) / `agent-decides` / `always-proceed`. Fine-grained `permissions.allow`/`deny`/`ask` arrays in `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/settings.json`, each entry an `action(target)` resource, **precedence Deny > Ask > Allow**. Actions and targets: `read_file(/path|dir|*)` recursive, default Ask but auto-allowed inside the workspace; `write_file(...)` same, implicitly granting `read_file` on the same target; `read_url(domain)` matching subdomains and ignoring paths; `execute_url(domain)` browser actuation; `command(prefix)` word-by-word literal or `command(regex:…)`; `unsandboxed(prefix)`/`unsandboxed(regex:…)`; `mcp(server/tool)`, `mcp(server/*)`, `mcp(*)`. Implicit rules: write implies read, deny-read implies deny-write; Windows paths normalized (drive letters stripped, `\` → `/`) and commands that cannot be word-split require `command(regex:…)`. `/permissions` edits three scopes — Project, Shared (across all Antigravity products), Global — whose on-disk backing files are undocumented. **Headless permission behavior: with no interactive prompt available a tool needing approval is soft-denied — the run continues, exits `0`, and a notice naming the tool goes to stderr**; since 1.1.27 refused actions also appear as `denied_actions` in the JSON output; reads/writes inside the active workspace are auto-allowed, shell commands default to Ask and are therefore soft-denied unless granted; bypass with `--dangerously-skip-permissions` (sets `permission_mode: "always-proceed"` in `init`); #548 reports `--print` ignoring `permissions.allow` entirely on some versions, #627 asks for a scoped per-invocation allow (there is none). Sandbox: `enableTerminalSandbox` (default false) or `--sandbox`, native OS primitives with no VM/Docker — Linux kernel namespaces (`/docs/cli/features` says `nsjail`), macOS `sandbox-exec` (Seatbelt SBPL), Windows `AppContainer` (per `/docs/cli/features`; the sandbox page lists only Linux and macOS). Boundaries derive from the permissions config: workspace folders and `write_file`-allowed paths mount read-write, `read_file`-allowed paths read-only, denied paths blocked; `~/.ssh` and `.env` blocked; **network off by default**, `read_url`-allowed domains join the outbound allowlist; `unsandboxed(...)` is the escape hatch. Bugs: `--sandbox` with `--dangerously-skip-permissions` lets the agent bypass the sandbox (#36); `--sandbox` diverts headless structured-output runs from the task (#813). Folder trust: the first launch in a directory shows a **Workspace Trust** confirmation as step 3 of onboarding (color scheme → rendering mode → workspace trust) with **no documented env var or settings key to pre-answer it**; the related `allowNonWorkspaceAccess` (default false) is a different control (whether file tools may leave recognized Git/workspace roots; since 1.1.14 grants read access only).

**Environment.** `GEMINI_API_KEY` is the only credential env var the CLI reads and **has no effect on its own** — `"modelProvider": "gemini"` must also be set in `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/settings.json`; with both, sign-in is skipped, and if `modelProvider` is `gemini` with the key unset the CLI refuses to start. `GOOGLE_GEMINI_BASE_URL` for a custom endpoint; **`GOOGLE_API_KEY` has no effect** (explicitly ignored, and the CLI does not load `.env` files); `AGY_CLI_DISABLE_AUTO_UPDATE`, `AGY_CLI_HIDE_LOGO`, `AGY_CLI_HIDE_ACCOUNT_INFO`, `AGY_CLI_DISABLE_LATEX`, `AGY_CLI_DISABLE_ESCAPE_SEQUENCE_OPTIMIZATIONS`, `AGY_CLI_CMD_OUTPUT_PERCENTAGE`, `EDITOR`; `ANTIGRAVITY_EXECUTABLE_DATA_DIR` is set *by* Antigravity for sidecars. SDK-only: `GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI`, `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT`, `GOOGLE_CLOUD_LOCATION`. Settings: only **one** CLI settings file is documented, `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/settings.json`, with sparse persistence and no documented system-wide or workspace-level file; the three-scope Project/Shared/Global model exists in `/permissions` without disclosed paths; what is documented is that **CLI flags override the persisted settings for the session** (`/config` shows `! Tool Permission: strict (overridden by command flag)`). Since 1.1.16/1.1.20 a refused save leaves an unparseable settings file byte-identical. Full key list: `colorScheme`, `altScreenMode`, `toolPermission`, `artifactReviewPolicy`, `agentMode`, `notifications`, `showTips`, `showFeedbackSurvey`, `editor`, `editorMode`, `vimInsertFirst`, `allowNonWorkspaceAccess`, `enableTerminalSandbox`, `useG1Credits`, `enableTelemetry`, `verbosity`, `runningLightSpeed`, `pickerGrouping`, `modelProvider`, `permissions` (`allow`/`deny`/`ask`), `statusLine` (`type`, `command`, `padding`, `enabled`, `stack_with_default`). Model: `--model <slug>` (no `-m` alias) + `--effort low|medium|high`; slugs from `agy models` / `agy --output-format json models`, e.g. `gemini-3.8-flash-high`, `gemini-3.8-flash-medium`, `gemini-3.7-flash-high`, `gemini-3.6-flash-medium`, `gemini-3.1-pro-high`, `claude-sonnet-4-6` (catalog also has Claude Opus 4.6 thinking and GPT-OSS-120b); **in headless mode an unknown `--model` is a hard error (exit `1`, `status: "ERROR"`)** deliberately, so pinned pipelines fail loudly (#710 reports a silent fallback to Flash anyway). Telemetry opt-out is the settings key `enableTelemetry` (default true), not an env var. Config-home isolation: **no documented HOME override variable** — #947's repro sets `HOME` directly to point at a different agy profile, implying the whole `~/.gemini/` tree follows `HOME`.
