**Observability.** Logs `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/log/cli-*.log` (newest wins), override `--log-file <path>`, path shown in `/help`, glog-style Go format (`I0721 22:36:42.357881 33780 input_loop.go:34] …`); remote-control daemon logs via `journalctl --user -u antigravity-cli-daemon -n 50` (Linux), `~/Library/Logs/antigravity-cli-daemon.log` (macOS); sidecar logs `~/.gemini/antigravity/sidecar_data/<sidecarId>/logs/`. **OTel/OTLP: none** — Gemini CLI's OpenTelemetry token-usage export was not carried over (FR #366). Usage: `usage` in `json`/`stream-json` and per-step `step_update.usage` — `input_tokens`, `output_tokens`, `thinking_tokens`, `cache_read_tokens`, `total_tokens`; interactively `/usage` (`/quota`), `/context`, `/credits`; headless `agy -p "/usage"`/`"/quota"`/`"/credits"` spend no quota and create no conversation. **Best real-time telemetry channel for a driver: the status-line script** — `{"statusLine":{"type":"command","command":"~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/statusline.sh"}}`; on every agent state change the TUI runs it, pipes JSON to stdin and renders stdout. Payload (**snake_case**, unlike hooks): `cwd`, `session_id` (alias of `conversation_id`), `conversation_id`, `conversation_title` (1.1.27), `transcript_path`, `model.{id,display_name}`, `workspace.{current_dir,project_dir}`, `version`, `context_window.{total_input_tokens,total_output_tokens,context_window_size,used_percentage,remaining_percentage,current_usage.{input_tokens,output_tokens,cache_creation_input_tokens,cache_read_input_tokens}}`, `exceeds_200k_tokens`, `product`, `quota.<bucket>.{remaining_fraction,reset_time,reset_in_seconds}`, `cost` (1.1.21), `agent_state` (`idle`|`thinking`|`working`|`tool_use`|`initializing`), `vcs.{type,branch,client,dirty}`, `sandbox.{enabled,allow_network}`, `artifact_count`, `plan_tier`, `email`, `pending_input_count`, `tool_confirmation_pending`, `task_count`, `terminal_width`, `execution_mode`, `vim.mode`; reference script `examples/statusline/statusline.sh`, same mechanism for terminal titles (`examples/title/title.sh`); the refresh runner leaks orphaned subprocesses on kill timeout on Windows, accumulating to OOM (#709). Exit codes: `0` success — **including a run where tools were soft-denied and nothing happened**, and including clean stream-json shutdown; `1` run failed to produce a response (invalid model, agent-state stream error, invalid JSON input line, missing `event` field, non-`text` content block); `2` stream-input protocol violation (`control_request`/`control_response` events, or a CLI-handled slash command sent into the stream). Since 1.1.20 benign tool errors and permission denials no longer produce non-zero exits.

**Gotchas.**
- **No headless authentication** — the biggest blocker. `GEMINI_API_KEY` grants model access but is not an account session, and hooks demonstrably do not fire under it (#893). Run interactive `agy` once and complete sign-in; the run then uses cached keyring credentials. With no terminal an unauthenticated run exits `authentication required` rather than hanging. Print mode can accept a pasted OAuth code via the controlling terminal (`/dev/tty`, `CONIN$`). FRs #223, #632, #381.
- Keyring dependency: on Linux headless/SSH the CLI needs an unlocked keyring and live D-Bus or it hangs / errors `failed to retrieve token: secret keyring is locked` (wait is 5 s); workaround `export $(dbus-launch)` or macOS `security unlock-keychain`; SSH sessions fall back to file-based token storage.
- First-run onboarding is a three-step interactive wizard (color scheme, rendering mode, workspace trust) with no documented way to pre-answer; #284: the first launch creates an empty unauthenticated profile needing a manual `/logout` to trigger OAuth. Pre-seed `settings.json` before first launch or complete onboarding once per profile.
- A Gemini CLI migration checklist appears interactively on first `agy` where legacy configs exist.
- **Windows headless is unreliable**: `agy` hangs forever with no attached console (detached subprocess, `DETACHED_PROCESS`, `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`, a service, CI, or redirected stdio — #508); `agy.exe` produces no stdout at all when spawned with `stdio: ['ignore','pipe','pipe']` (#187); `--print` hangs after the stream-json `init` on longer prompts (#841). On POSIX prefer a real PTY or at least leave stdout/stderr as pipes rather than `ignore`.
- Held-open stdin deadlock: `agy models` and other non-interactive subcommands never exit when stdin is a held-open non-TTY pipe (#882). Close the child's stdin or point it at `/dev/null` unless using `--input-format stream-json`.
- The process does not always exit: print mode can produce its answer, log `CLI store manager shutting down`, and never exit (#947); `--print-timeout` does not always bound this (#594). Enforce your own wall-clock kill.
- Orphaned children: the agy/backend process can remain orphaned at ~100% CPU on macOS (#183); background tasks and subagents remain orphaned in the UI (#977); the statusLine/title runner leaks subprocesses on Windows (#709). Kill the whole process group.
- Signals: `Ctrl+C` cancels, double-press exits; `Ctrl+D` exits only on an empty prompt box; a SIGINT-terminated run reports `status: "INTERRUPTED"`; no documented `--cancel` for a running headless job (#779).
- No TUI readiness signal and no `--wait-for-ready`; slash-command autocomplete intercepts automated terminal input in non-interactive streams (#243). Force inline with `"altScreenMode": "never"`.
- CPU burn: the interactive TUI holds ~0.8 core for a turn where the identical headless `-p` turn costs 0.03 core (#945), 33-74% of a core while idle on Linux (#949), and a 100% busy-loop after streaming render that does not exit on terminal disconnect (#134). **Strongly prefer `--input-format stream-json` over PTY-driving the TUI.**
- A crashing stdio MCP server blocks the prompt loop for a hardcoded 5 minutes (#657). Start with no MCP servers when timing matters.
- Use a lenient JSON parser: `response` can contain a raw unescaped newline making the `json` envelope invalid (#702 — `json.loads(raw, strict=False)` works, `jq` and Go `encoding/json` fail), and `stream-json` can emit `U+FFFD` for multibyte UTF-8 (#800). Unrecognized input `event` names are safely skipped with a stderr warning.
- No PTY delegation for shell tools: `run_command` cannot run interactive commands (#165), crashes/hangs on Windows (#513), and hangs indefinitely on NixOS non-FHS (#677).
- Flag parsing: always write `-p "…"` with the value attached, and put `--output-format` before any subcommand.

**Driver notes.**
- Window mode: PTY-drive `agy` only when you must — the TUI burns ~0.8 core per turn and idles at 33-74% of a core; set `"altScreenMode": "never"`, `AGY_CLI_HIDE_LOGO` and `AGY_CLI_DISABLE_ESCAPE_SEQUENCE_OPTIMIZATIONS` for a cleaner capture.
- Session mode: `agy -p "…" --conversation <uuid> --output-format json`, one process per turn; there is no way to list conversations non-interactively, so the driver must persist the UUID itself.
- Batch mode: the intended surface is one long-lived `agy --input-format stream-json --output-format stream-json` process — write one `user` line per turn, read until that turn's `result`, close stdin to finish; never send CLI-handled slash commands into it (exit 2).
- Authentication must be established interactively once per profile (`HOME`-scoped) before any automated run; `GEMINI_API_KEY` alone is not an account session and silently disables hooks.
- Enforce an external wall-clock kill and kill the process group — `--print-timeout` does not reliably bound the run, and orphans are a documented pattern.
- Exit code `0` does **not** mean work happened: headless permission denials are soft and silent, so check `status`, `denied_actions` (1.1.27+) and the stderr notices.
- Most stable surface: `--input-format stream-json` + `--output-format stream-json`; the status-line script is the best real-time telemetry channel; there is no ACP.

**Sources.**
- https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/headless
- https://antigravity.google/docs/hooks
- https://antigravity.google/docs/mcp
- https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/permissions
- https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/statusline
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/google-antigravity/antigravity-cli/main/CHANGELOG.md

---

### Kiro CLI (AWS / kiro.dev, proprietary closed source, v2.21.1 binary — "CLI 3.0" is the agent engine generation, not the binary version, `curl -fsSL https://cli.kiro.dev/install | bash`)

**Launch.** TUI: `kiro-cli` (bare command defaults to `kiro-cli chat`) or `kiro-cli chat`; default UI engine `tui`; `--classic`/`--tui` force it for one launch (precedence CLI flag → `KIRO_CHAT_UI` → setting `chat.ui` → `tui`); `--v3` opts into the v3 agent engine, `--engine v2`/`--engine v3` select explicitly (saved as `chat.agentEngine` ∈ `v1|v2|v3`); `kiro-cli chat --sessions` opens the V3 session dashboard; `kiro-cli --cloud [--repo owner/repository]` runs in a managed cloud sandbox. Headless: `kiro-cli chat --no-interactive "<prompt>"` ("Print first response to STDOUT without interactive mode"; requires a prompt argument), with `--trust-all-tools`, `--trust-tools=<categories>` (e.g. `read,grep,write`), `--output-format stream-json` (V2/V3 only), `--require-mcp-startup`, `--wrap always|never|auto`, `--effort low|medium|high|xhigh|max`, `--agent <name>`, `--resume`, `--resume-id <ID>`; requires `KIRO_API_KEY` (Pro / Pro+ / Pro Max / Power subscribers) unless an interactive browser login already exists. Stdin: supported as *context* (`cat build-error.log | kiro-cli chat --no-interactive "Explain this build failure"`, `git diff | kiro-cli chat --no-interactive "Review these changes"`); docs say the prompt must still be an argv argument, though a filed bug (kirodotdev/Kiro#8352) shows stdin-only invocation working in practice — treat the argv prompt as required. SDK/server/ACP: **no SDK**; `kiro-cli acp` runs as an ACP agent over JSON-RPC 2.0 on stdin/stdout (`--agent <name>` selects a config) — the real programmatic driver surface. Rust core binary plus a **Bun/JavaScript TUI**: documented process tree `kiro-cli → kiro-cli-chat chat → bun tui.js chat → kiro-cli-chat acp`. Other subcommands: `agent {list,create,edit,validate,migrate,set-default}`, `mcp {add,remove,list,import,status}`, `login`, `logout`, `whoami`, `settings {open,list,list --all}`, `doctor`, `diagnostic`, `update`, `version`, `theme`, `integrations`, `inline`, `translate`, `issue`, `crew`, `launch`. Successor to Amazon Q Developer CLI — the installer detects `$HOME/.local/bin/q` and prints "Kiro CLI is the new version of Amazon Q CLI."

**Input and follow-up.** Positional `INPUT` on `kiro-cli chat [OPTIONS] [INPUT]`; piped stdin fed in as context alongside it; no `--prompt-file`/`-f` (in-chat `@path` references like `@src/api/routes.ts`, `@"my file.txt"`, and agent configs accept `"prompt": "file://./my-agent-prompt.md"`; for a file as the headless prompt use shell substitution). **Headless multi-turn works** via session resume: `kiro-cli chat --no-interactive --trust-all-tools --resume "<next prompt>"` continues the most recent session for the cwd, `--resume-id <SESSION_ID>` a specific one; each invocation is one turn and "No mid-session user input is possible." For true multi-turn in one process use `kiro-cli acp` with repeated `session/prompt` requests against the same `sessionId`.

**Output.** Text (default): human-formatted; in `--no-interactive` the first/final response goes to stdout. **Known bug: ANSI SGR escapes are emitted even when stdout is not a TTY (kirodotdev/Kiro#8352, open); `NO_COLOR=1` and `KIRO_LOG_NO_COLOR=1` have no effect** — workaround `sed 's/\x1b\[[0-9;]*m//g'`. `--output-format stream-json` "emits run events as JSON Lines on stdout for programmatic use", requires engine V2 or V3, shipped in 2.19.2. **The stream-json event schema is unpublished** — no event type names or field keys anywhere; a community FR (#5423) *proposes* `{"response","tool_calls","stop_reason"}` and NDJSON `{"type":"tool_use"|"tool_result"|"message_delta",…}` but that is a proposal, not the implementation. Capture one run's JSONL empirically before writing a parser. Where the final answer is in stream-json: undocumented. The documented, schema-stable alternative is **ACP**: `session/notification` update types `AgentMessageChunk` (streaming text/content), `ToolCall` (name, parameters, status), `ToolCallUpdate` (progress), `TurnEnd`; core methods `initialize`, `session/new`, `session/load`, `session/prompt`, `session/cancel`, `session/set_mode`, `session/set_model`; Kiro extension methods (prefix `_kiro.dev/`, experimental) `_kiro.dev/commands/execute`, `/commands/options`, `/commands/available`, `/mcp/oauth_request`, `/mcp/server_initialized`, `/compaction/status`, `/clear/status`, `_session/terminate` — clients may ignore unsupported extensions. Other JSON: `--format plain|json|json-pretty` on `doctor`, `whoami`, `settings list`, `diagnostic`, `inline show-customizations`; `kiro-cli chat --list-models --format json`.

**Sessions.** `--resume`/`-r` (most recent for this directory), `--resume-picker`, `--resume-id <SESSION_ID>`, `--list-sessions`, `--delete-session <SESSION_ID>`, `--sessions` (V3 dashboard). Session id is a UUID (e.g. `f2946a26-3735-4b08-8d05-c928010302d5`); on exit the CLI prints the id plus the resume command; `/session-id` prints it mid-session. Storage: chat sessions auto-save **every conversation turn** to a **SQLite database in `~/.kiro/`** keyed by directory path; **ACP sessions are persisted as files at `~/.kiro/sessions/cli/`** — `<session-id>.json` (metadata/state) and `<session-id>.jsonl` (event log / conversation history), the format a driver can tail. Manual export `/chat save <path>` / `/chat load <path>`; script hooks `/chat save-via-script <script>` (session JSON on stdin, exit 0 = success) and `/chat load-via-script <script>` (script prints session JSON to stdout). v3 uses a new **not backward-compatible** session format — back up `~/.kiro/sessions/` before upgrading; V3 sessions cannot be opened by V2. Listing `kiro-cli chat --list-sessions` (cwd-scoped, most recently updated first), `/chat resume`, `/sessions` (cross-workspace + cloud); cleanup `/sessions clean [--yes]`. **Hard constraint: a session can be active in only one process at a time** — resuming one open elsewhere fails with `Failed to start session: Session is active in another process (PID XXXXX)`; branch via `/chat save` + `/chat load` into a copy.

**Hooks.** The docs carry **two trigger-name vocabularies and are internally inconsistent** — verify empirically. Config (v3 / IDE 1.0 format): standalone JSON files at `.kiro/hooks/<id>.json` (any `.json` filename, multiple hooks per file), activated automatically at session start: `{"version":"v1","hooks":[{"name":"lint-on-save","description":"…","trigger":"PostFileSave","matcher":"\\.(ts|tsx)$","action":{"type":"command","command":"npx eslint --fix"},"timeout":60,"enabled":true}]}`. Fields: `version` (required, `"v1"`), `hooks[]` (required), `name` (required), `description` (documentation only), `trigger` (required, PascalCase), `matcher` (regex, defaults to always-match), `action.type` (required, `"command"` or `"agent"`), `action.command` (required for command), `action.prompt` (required for agent), `timeout` (**seconds**, default 60, `0` disables, ignored for agent actions), `enabled` (default true), `confirm` (`Stop`-trigger command hooks only). **Trigger name set A** (hooks.md / hooks/types.md availability tables): `PromptSubmit`, `AgentStop`, `SessionStart` (IDE only), `AgentSpawn` (CLI only), `PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse`, `FileCreate`, `FileSave`, `FileDelete`, `PreTaskExecution`, `PostTaskExecution`, `Legacy Manual Hook`. **Trigger name set B** (v3 hooks-migration table, matching the actual code examples which use `PostFileSave` and `Stop`) with matcher target and blocking: `SessionStart` (—, no), `Stop` (—, no), `PreToolUse` (tool name, **yes**), `PostToolUse` (tool name, no), `PreTaskExec` (—, **yes**), `PostTaskExec` (—, no), `UserPromptSubmit` (prompt text, **yes**), `PostFileCreate` (file path, no), `PostFileSave` (file path, no), `PostFileDelete` (file path, no), `Manual` (—, no). CLI-only note: only `UserPromptSubmit`/`PromptSubmit`, `Stop`/`AgentStop`, `AgentSpawn`, `PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse` are supported in the CLI; file and task triggers are IDE-only, and file triggers fire only for agent-made changes. 2.x → 3.0 mapping: `agentSpawn`→`SessionStart`, `userPromptSubmit`→`UserPromptSubmit`, `preToolUse`→`PreToolUse`, `postToolUse`→`PostToolUse`, `fileEdited`→`PostFileSave`, `fileCreated`→`PostFileCreate`, `agentStop`/`stop`→`Stop`; auto-convert with `kiro-cli agent migrate`. Legacy 2.x format (embedded in agent config, camelCase keys, still accepted): `{"hooks":{"agentSpawn":[{"command":"git status"}],"userPromptSubmit":[…],"preToolUse":[{"matcher":"execute_bash","command":"…"}],"postToolUse":[{"matcher":"fs_write","command":"cargo fmt --all"}]}}` with triggers `agentSpawn`, `userPromptSubmit`, `preToolUse`, `postToolUse`, `stop` and per-hook fields `command` (required), `matcher`. Hook types: `command` (shell, run in the project root, event JSON on **stdin**) and `agent` (injects a prompt into the conversation and **consumes credits** since it starts a new agent loop); no HTTP type. Payload casing: `hook_event_name` values are **camelCase even when the trigger key is PascalCase** — `{"hook_event_name":"agentSpawn","cwd":"…","session_id":"…"}`; `userPromptSubmit` adds `prompt`; `stop` adds `assistant_response`; `preToolUse` adds `tool_name`, `tool_input` (e.g. `{"operations":[{"mode":"Line","path":"…"}]}`); `postToolUse` adds `tool_response` (`{"success":true,"result":[…]}`); MCP tools appear as `"tool_name": "@postgres/query"` with raw args. Decisions via exit codes: `0` = success, **stdout is added to the agent's context for `SessionStart`/`AgentSpawn` and `UserPromptSubmit`** (the context-injection mechanism) and ignored for others; `2` = **block**, valid for `PreToolUse`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `PreTaskExec`, with stderr returned to the LLM; any other = hook failed, stderr shown as a warning, execution proceeds (fail-open). Stop-hook block via stdout JSON at exit 0: `{"decision":"block","reason":"You haven't run the tests yet."}` makes the agent continue instead of stopping, with `reason` delivered as a new user message. **Timeouts are inconsistent across pages**: hooks.md / hooks-migration say `timeout` in seconds, default 60, `0` disables, ignored for agent actions; hooks/actions.md (CLI tab) says default 30 seconds (30,000 ms) configured with **`timeout_ms`**, plus `cache_ttl_seconds` (`0` = no caching, default; `>0` caches successful results; **AgentSpawn hooks are never cached**). Matchers: regex — for `PreToolUse`/`PostToolUse` against the tool name, accepting canonical names (`fs_read`, `fs_write`, `execute_bash`, `use_aws`) or aliases (`read`, `write`, `shell`, `aws`), with `"@git"` = all tools from the `git` MCP server, `"@git/status"` = one tool, `"*"` = all, `"@builtin"` = built-ins, `"@mcp"` = all MCP tools, `"@powers"` = all Powers tools, and `@`-prefixed patterns regex-matched (`@mcp.*sql.*`); file triggers match the path; `UserPromptSubmit` matches prompt text; `SessionStart`/`Stop`/`PreTaskExec`/`PostTaskExec`/`Manual` do not evaluate the matcher. Confirmation prompts (`Stop` command hooks only): `"confirm": {"question":"…","confirmCommand":"./confirm-options.sh","options":[{"id":"submit","label":"Yes, submit","run":true},{"id":"dismiss","label":"Not this time","run":false}]}` — `confirmCommand` runs first and its stdout JSON can suppress the prompt or replace question/options; non-zero exit, timeout or invalid JSON falls back to the static values. Template variable `{{filePath}}` in v3 hook commands for file triggers. Env var `USER_PROMPT` exposes the prompt to IDE Prompt Submit shell hooks.

**MCP.** Config `~/.kiro/settings/mcp.json` (global/user) and `<workspace>/.kiro/settings/mcp.json` (workspace) — docs also reference `~/.kiro/mcp.json` and `.kiro/mcp.json`, but the canonical repeated paths are the `settings/` ones. Shape `{"mcpServers":{"<name>":{…}}}` with local `command`, `args`, `env` (`${EXPANDED_VARIABLE}` supported), `disabled`, `autoApprove: ["tool1","tool2"]` or `["*"]`, `disabledTools`; remote `url`, `headers`, `oauth` (`clientId`, `clientSecret`, `redirectUri`, `clientMetadataUrl`, `oauthScopes`), `oauthScopes`. Agent-config `mcpServers` entries additionally accept `timeout` (stdio handshake, ms, default **60000**), `requestTimeout` (per-call, ms, default **120000**), and `"type": "http"`. CLI: `kiro-cli mcp add --name <n> --command <cmd> [--args …] [--scope workspace|global] [--env k1=v1,k2=v2] [--timeout <ms>] [--force] [--agent <name>]`, `mcp remove`, `mcp list [workspace|global]`, `mcp import --file <f> [workspace|global] [--force]`, `mcp status --name <n>`; in-session `/mcp`, `/mcp add`, `/mcp auth` (`^A`), `/mcp cancel-auth` (`^X`), `/mcp logout` (`^R`). Transports: stdio and HTTP (`url`, optional `"type": "http"`, `headers`; remote must be HTTPS except on localhost). **SSE is not mentioned anywhere.** OAuth: browser flow, automatic, DCR (RFC 7591) by default; `oauth.clientId` skips DCR (public client); `clientId`+`clientSecret` = confidential client (**CLI only**, IDE is PKCE-public-only); `oauth.clientMetadataUrl` = CIMD identity; `redirectUri` accepts a full URL / `127.0.0.1:7778` / `:7778` / omitted (random port), host must be `127.0.0.1` or `localhost`, scheme `http`; default scopes `openid`, `email`, `profile`, `offline_access`; `oauth.oauthScopes` beats top-level `oauthScopes`; mid-session token expiry triggers transparent re-auth. Precedence (highest first): agent config `mcpServers` → workspace `.kiro/settings/mcp.json` → global `~/.kiro/settings/mcp.json`; same-name servers fully override (not merged), different names are additive, `"disabled": true` at a higher scope suppresses a lower-scope server. Filtering: `autoApprove`, `disabledTools`, `disabled`, plus agent-level `tools`/`excludedTools`/`allowedTools` and permission rules with `capability: mcp`, `match: ["server/*"]`. Acts as MCP server: **none** — no `mcp serve` mode; the programmatic-server role is filled by `kiro-cli acp`. Unreachable server: MCP failures are **logged as warnings and do not affect the exit code** by default — the run proceeds without those tools; `--require-mcp-startup` exits immediately with **code 3**. Init timeouts `mcp.initTimeout` (ms, default 5000) interactive, `mcp.noInteractiveTimeout` (ms, default 30000) non-interactive. Hot-reload: a file watcher on `.kiro/agents` and `mcp.json` reconciles servers between turns without restarting the session, restarting only changed servers; key/env reordering is not a change; servers added mid-session via `/mcp add` survive reconciliation — "No command is required to trigger a reload." Tool Search: `toolSearch.enabled` (default false), `toolSearch.minPct` (5), `toolSearch.minTokens` (50000), `KIRO_CLI_TOOL_SEARCH_MATCHING_THRESHOLD` (1.5).

**Extensibility.** Custom agents `.kiro/agents/<name>.{json,md}` (workspace, wins on collision, loads **only if the workspace is trusted**) and `~/.kiro/agents/<name>.{json,md}` (global); nested dirs give slashed names (`~/.kiro/agents/team/planner.md` → `team/planner`); markdown form uses YAML front matter plus body as system prompt. Fields `name`, `description`, `prompt` (inline or `file://./x.md` relative to the config file), `mcpServers`, `tools`, `toolAliases`, `allowedTools`, `permissions` (v3), `toolsSettings` (deprecated, **removed in V3**), `resources`, `hooks`, `includeMcpJson`, `includePowers`, `model`, `keyboardShortcut`, `welcomeMessage`, `excludedTools`; `tools` tags `read`, `write`, `shell`, `web`, `subagent`, `knowledge`, `todo_list`, `@mcp`, `@builtin`, `@server`, `@server/tool`, `*`; `allowedTools` supports globs `*`/`?`, `@server/read_*`, `@*-mcp/read_*`, `@git-*`, exact matches win, case-sensitive, and **does not** accept a bare `"*"`. Select `--agent <name>`, switch `/agent swap <name>`, default `kiro-cli agent set-default <name>` or `chat.defaultAgent`. Prompts/commands: `/prompts list|create --name <n> [--content …]|edit <n>|details <n>`, saved to `.kiro/prompts/` or globally, invoked `@prompt-name`. Skills: folder with required `SKILL.md` (front matter `name`, `description`, markdown body) plus optional `references/`, at `.kiro/skills/<name>/` and `~/.kiro/skills/<name>/`; auto-loaded by the **default** agent only — **custom agents do not load skills by default**, add `"resources": ["skill://.kiro/skills/*/SKILL.md","skill://~/.kiro/skills/*/SKILL.md"]`; skills also surface as slash commands. Subagents: the `subagent` built-in tool; the default sub-agent inherits `read`, `write`, `shell`, `web_search`, `web_fetch` and configured MCP tools; a custom orchestrator must list `subagent` in `tools` (or use `@builtin`); spawnable/auto-approved agents via `toolsSettings.subagent` (`trustedAgents`); restrict a sub-agent's tools in **its own** config; `Ctrl+G` opens the crew monitor; `api.subagentTimeout` default 3600 s. Steering: `.kiro/steering/*.md` (workspace, overrides global) and `~/.kiro/steering/*.md`; foundation files `product.md`, `tech.md`, `structure.md` included in every interaction by default; front matter `inclusion:` modes `always` (default), `fileMatch` (+`fileMatchPattern`), `manual` (`#steering-file-name`), `auto` (description-matched, also exposed as `/<filename>`). **Critical caveat stated in the docs: "On Kiro CLI, inclusion modes are not currently supported. All steering files in the `.kiro/steering/` directory are loaded automatically"** — and a 2.20.x changelog entry says `fileMatch`/`manual` files are no longer auto-injected by glob match, so behavior is in flux. Custom agents do not auto-include steering — add `"resources": ["file://.kiro/steering/**/*.md"]`. `AGENTS.md` supported (agents.md standard) on IDE, CLI, Web and Mobile; discovered at the workspace root, in `~/.kiro/steering/`, **and in subdirectories throughout the workspace**; it does not support inclusion modes and is always included. Precedence summary: workspace agents > global agents; workspace steering > global steering; agent-config MCP > workspace mcp.json > global mcp.json; UI engine `--tui`/`--classic` > `KIRO_CHAT_UI` > `chat.ui` > `tui`; **permissions have no scope precedence** — deny > ask > allow across all scopes. Powers: `github.com/kirodotdev/powers`, auto-picked-up from the IDE in v3, agent field `includePowers`.

**Permissions.** Two generations coexist. 2.x (still functional): `--trust-all-tools` auto-approves every tool call (in the terminal UI it shows a confirmation warning you must acknowledge; docs confirm it "still works as a session-scope override for CI use cases without configuration changes"); `--trust-tools=<comma-separated>` for categories (`read`, `grep`, `write`, `shell`, …); in-session `/tools`, `/tools help|trust <tool>|untrust <tool>|trust-all|reset` with states **Trusted** or **Per-request** (sub-agents inherit `/tools trust-all` since 2.19.2); agent config `allowedTools` globs and `toolsSettings` (deprecated) e.g. `toolsSettings.shell.{allowedCommands, deniedCommands, autoAllowReadonly, denyByDefault}` — regex anchored `\A`/`\z`, **no look-around**; interactively chosen trust patterns persist as regex in `allowedCommands`. 3.0 capability model: YAML rule files `~/.kiro/settings/permissions.yaml` (user) and `~/.kiro/workspace-roots/<hash(workspaceRoot)>/permissions.yaml` (workspace, per-user, **outside the repo** so a cloned repo cannot inject trust); agents can embed a `permissions` block; sessions accumulate in-memory rules. Shape `rules: [{capability: shell, match: ["git *"], exclude: ["git push *"], effect: allow}]`. Capabilities `fs_read`, `fs_write`, `shell`, `web_fetch`, `web_search`, `mcp`, `subagent`, `skill`, `power`, `context`, `diagnostics`, `sandbox_network`; meta `all`, `builtin`, `filesystem`; effects `deny`, `ask`, `allow`. **Evaluation is deny-overrides with no scope precedence** — most restrictive wins across scopes Kiro (hardcoded, deny/ask only), administration (`managed-settings.json`, deny/ask only), user, workspace, agent, session. Glob semantics: for `fs_*`, `*` is single-component, `**` crosses separators, brace and `[abc]` classes and `?` supported, and a wildcard-free pattern implicitly matches children; for shell/web/MCP only `*` — no `**`, no `?`, no classes. Shell compound commands (`;`, `&&`, `||`, `|`) are split and each sub-command evaluated independently. Hardcoded invariants: always denied — writes to `~/.kiro/settings/`, `.kiro/settings/`, `~/.kiro/workspace-roots/`; always asks — writes to `.git/**`, `.kiro/agents/**`, `.kiro/hooks/**`, `.kiroignore`. Default with no permissions.yaml: `fs_read` on `./**` allowed silently, read-only git commands (`git status/log/diff/branch`) and system info (`pwd`, `whoami`, `uname`) allowed, utility tools allowed, everything else prompts; `read`, `grep`, `glob` trusted within cwd, `shell`, `write`, `aws` prompt. Pre-answering trust for automation: `--trust-all-tools`, or write `rules: [{capability: all, effect: allow}]` into the CI `~/.kiro/settings/permissions.yaml` — **there is no env var to pre-answer trust prompts**. Trusted workspaces (v3): the CLI prompts trust verification on first launch and restricts capabilities until approval. Sandbox: **the local CLI has none** — `sandbox_network` exists as a capability name but the sandbox is a Kiro Web / cloud-session feature; `kiro-cli chat --cloud [--repo owner/repo]` runs in that managed cloud sandbox, where `permissions.yaml` and interactive approvals do **not** apply. Related setting `chat.disableGranularTrust`.

**Environment.** `KIRO_API_KEY` (API-key auth for headless/CI, `ksk_…`, Pro/Pro+/Pro Max/Power only), **`KIRO_HOME`** (override `~/.kiro` — the mechanism for isolating profiles per driver instance), `KIRO_LOG_LEVEL` (`error` default, `warn`, `info`, `debug`, `trace`), `KIRO_LOG_NO_COLOR` (log output only, not chat), `KIRO_CHAT_LOG_FILE`, `KIRO_CHAT_UI`, `KIRO_CHAT_CLI_BIN` (purpose undescribed), `KIRO_ACP_RECORD_PATH` (JSONL file recording **TUI ACP wire traffic** — the practical way to reverse-engineer the event stream), `KIRO_CLI_TOOL_SEARCH_MATCHING_THRESHOLD`, `KIRO_ASCII_MODE`, `KIRO_NO_HYPERLINKS` / `KIRO_NO_PROGRESS` / `KIRO_NO_SYNCHRONIZED`, `KIRO_VOICE_SERVER_URL`, `NO_COLOR` (reported ineffective for `--no-interactive` chat output, #8352), `HTTP_PROXY`/`HTTPS_PROXY`/`NO_PROXY` (incl. `http://user:pass@host:port`), `AGENT_DISPLAY_OUT`/`AGENT_CONTEXT_OUT` (FIFOs **exported by Kiro into agent-run shell commands**; `AGENT_CONTEXT_OUT` lines surface in the tool result's `agent_notes` field), `USER_PROMPT`, legacy installer `Q_INSTALL_GLOBAL`/`Q_SKIP_SETUP`. AWS credentials are **not** used to authenticate — sign in with `kiro-cli login` (Builder ID / IAM Identity Center / Google / GitHub / external IdP) or `KIRO_API_KEY`; precedence **active browser session > `KIRO_API_KEY` > prompt to sign in**. Settings file `~/.kiro/settings/cli.json`, managed with `kiro-cli settings [list|list --all|open|--delete] [KEY] [VALUE] --format plain|json|json-pretty`. Config dirs: `~/.kiro/settings/{cli.json,mcp.json,permissions.yaml}`, `~/.kiro/workspace-roots/<hash>/permissions.yaml`, `~/.kiro/agents/`, `~/.kiro/steering/`, `~/.kiro/skills/`, `~/.kiro/sessions/`, `~/.kiro/sessions/cli/`, `~/.kiro/sessions-v2-backup`; project `.kiro/{agents,hooks,settings,steering,skills,prompts,specs}/`, `.kiroignore`. Legacy `~/.aws/amazonq` paths are not documented anywhere in current Kiro docs. Model: **no `--model` launch flag** — use the agent-config `model` field (falls back to default with a warning), `chat.defaultModel`, `chat.modelDefaults`, in-session `/model`, or ACP `session/set_model`; discover with `kiro-cli chat --list-models [--format json]`; reasoning effort at launch `--effort low|medium|high|xhigh|max`. Telemetry opt-out `kiro-cli settings telemetry.enabled false` (also CLI Preferences toggles; enterprise users opted out automatically); no telemetry env var. Driver-relevant settings: `chat.ui`, `chat.agentEngine`, `chat.greeting.enabled`, `chat.enablePromptHints`, `chat.disableWrap`, `chat.disableMarkdownRendering` (classic only), `chat.allowAnimations`, `chat.allowAsciiArt`, `chat.allowIcons`, `chat.showThinking`, `chat.autoExpandToolOutput`, `chat.disableAutoCompaction`, `chat.keybindings.{cancelStream,closeMenu,quit}`, `api.timeout` (3600 s), `api.streamIdleSoftTimeout` (60 s), `api.streamIdleHardTimeout` (300 s), `api.subagentTimeout` (3600 s), `mcp.initTimeout` (5000 ms), `mcp.noInteractiveTimeout` (30000 ms), `app.disableAutoupdates`. Config-home isolation: `KIRO_HOME` (a plain `HOME` override is not documented).

**Observability.** Logs: macOS `$TMPDIR/kiro-log/kiro-chat.log`, Linux `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/kiro-log/kiro-chat.log` (docs elsewhere say `/tmp/kiro-log/` when unset), Windows `%TEMP%\kiro-log\logs\kiro-chat.log`; override `KIRO_CHAT_LOG_FILE`; verbosity `KIRO_LOG_LEVEL` or repeated `-v`/`-vv`/`-vvv`; docs warn logs may contain file paths, code and command output. ACP wire traffic recorded separately to JSONL with `KIRO_ACP_RECORD_PATH`. Diagnostics `kiro-cli doctor [--all|--strict|--format plain|json|json-pretty]`, `kiro-cli diagnostic [--force] [--format …]` (`[q-details]` version/hash/date/variant, `[system-info]`, `[environment]`, `[env-vars]`; without `--force` it needs the app running via `kiro-cli launch`). Usage: in-session `/usage` shows billing/credits; a hidden `/stats` command ("intentionally hidden from tab-completion, but works when typed directly") shows request IDs, total duration, time-to-first-chunk, input/output token counts and errors for the last 100 requests, with `/stats save <file>.json`; **no CLI-level usage flag**. No OTel. Exit codes: `0` success; `1` general failure (auth error, invalid args, operation failed); `3` MCP server failed to start — **only** when `--require-mcp-startup` is passed. Hook exit codes: `0` success (stdout may be injected as context), `2` block (PreToolUse / UserPromptSubmit / PreTaskExec, stderr to the LLM), other = warning and execution proceeds.

**Gotchas.**
- No TUI readiness signal, no `--wait`, no ready event — prefer `kiro-cli acp` (whose `initialize` returns capabilities, a real handshake) or `--no-interactive` one-shots over screen-scraping.
- **The TUI is a JS process, not the Rust binary** (`kiro-cli → kiro-cli-chat chat → bun tui.js chat → kiro-cli-chat acp`): your subprocess handle is three levels above the thing holding the PTY.
- Orphaned children (kirodotdev/Kiro#6810, now closed): closing the terminal without `/quit` left `bun tui.js chat` reparented to PID 1 at 130-180% CPU for 10+ hours. Do not rely on SIGHUP propagation — kill the process group and verify.
- First-run login starts a sign-in flow; locally it opens a browser and login-selection flags are generally ignored; on remote/SSH it falls back to device flow; external IdP login does not support device flow. **Browser-session credentials take precedence over `KIRO_API_KEY`, so a stale login can silently override your key.** `kiro-cli logout` is user-wide (preserves agents, conversations, settings, MCP config).
- With `--no-interactive` and no `--trust-all-tools`/`--trust-tools`, tool calls have nobody to approve them; in the TUI `--trust-all-tools` and `/tools trust-all` additionally show a confirmation warning.
- **ANSI SGR escapes leak into piped `--no-interactive` output** and `NO_COLOR`/`KIRO_LOG_NO_COLOR` do not suppress them (#8352, open) — strip them or use `--output-format stream-json`.
- The `stream-json` schema is undocumented: capture a real run first, and gate it behind `--engine v2`/`--engine v3` (a no-op on V1).
- Session single-process lock: resuming a session open elsewhere fails with `Session is active in another process (PID XXXXX)`; fork via `/chat save` + `/chat load`.
- Sessions are cwd-scoped — `--resume`/`--list-sessions` return nothing from a different directory. Always pin cwd.
- No terminal-size flag or documented minimum; force determinism with `KIRO_ASCII_MODE=1`, `KIRO_NO_HYPERLINKS=1`, `KIRO_NO_PROGRESS=1`, `KIRO_NO_SYNCHRONIZED=1`, `chat.allowAnimations=false`.
- Signals: `Ctrl+C` exits the session (rebindable via `chat.keybindings.quit`), `Ctrl+D` exits from the chat input, `Ctrl+Z` **twice** suspends (a single press is ignored during streaming), `Esc` cancels; **no documented SIGTERM contract**.
- Known PTY/subprocess issues: #7554 (TUI-mode ACP subprocess exits status 0 right after the ~30 KB `#V2` context payload, then `session/prompt` writes hit EBADF — "Agent connection closed unexpectedly", 100% reproducible on AlmaLinux 8 behind an HTTPS proxy, classic mode unaffected), #4497 ("Bad file descriptor (os error 9)" on Linux under SSH `RemoteCommand`), #7172 (`/editor` hangs in TUI mode because stdin/stdout is not released to the external editor), #7169 (shell integration unconditionally exec-wraps zsh with no opt-out; the `kiro-cli-term` PTY wrapper drops typeahead and creates tty mismatches in tmux panes), #7438 (the shell tool does not support interactive commands).
- Engine gaps: CLI 3.0 does not run on Amazon Linux 2, and **classic (non-TUI) mode does not support the v3 engine**, so "headless + v3 + classic" is invalid; `--output-format stream-json` needs v2 or v3.
- `aws_tool` was removed in 3.0 — AWS access must go through MCP servers.
- Auto-update runs in the background by default; pin with `kiro-cli settings app.disableAutoupdates true`. CloudShell resets to its pre-installed version on environment restart (`~/.kiro` persists).

**Driver notes.**
- Window mode: PTY-drive `kiro-cli chat`, but remember the PTY is held by a `bun tui.js` grandchild — signal and kill the whole process group, and set `KIRO_ASCII_MODE=1 KIRO_NO_HYPERLINKS=1 KIRO_NO_PROGRESS=1 KIRO_NO_SYNCHRONIZED=1` for a deterministic capture.
- Session mode: `kiro-cli chat --no-interactive --trust-all-tools --resume-id <UUID> "<prompt>"` per turn, always from the same cwd (sessions are cwd-scoped), and never resume a session another process holds.
- Batch mode: pin cwd, set `KIRO_HOME` per instance, write `rules: [{capability: all, effect: allow}]` into that home's `settings/permissions.yaml`, and add `--require-mcp-startup` when missing MCP tools should fail the run (exit 3) rather than pass silently.
- Text output is unusable raw — ANSI escapes leak into pipes and cannot be disabled; either strip them or move to `--output-format stream-json` after capturing its schema empirically.
- Use `KIRO_ACP_RECORD_PATH` to record real wire traffic before writing any parser; `~/.kiro/sessions/cli/<session-id>.jsonl` is the tailable ACP event log.
- Most stable surface: `kiro-cli acp` — the only documented, versioned schema (`AgentMessageChunk`, `ToolCall`, `ToolCallUpdate`, `TurnEnd` plus `_kiro.dev/` extensions); `stream-json` is undocumented and the TUI is fragile.

**Sources.**
- https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/headless.md
- https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/acp.md
- https://kiro.dev/docs/hooks.md
- https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/v3/permissions.md
- https://kiro.dev/docs/mcp/configuration.md
- https://kiro.dev/docs/reference/exit-codes.md

---

### pi (Mario Zechner / badlogic, earendil-works, MIT, v0.85.1, `npm install -g --ignore-scripts @earendil-works/pi-coding-agent` or `curl -fsSL https://pi.dev/install.sh | sh`)

> Renamed scope: `@mariozechner/pi-coding-agent` is deprecated at 0.73.1 ("please use @earendil-works/pi-coding-agent instead going forward"); `github.com/badlogic/pi-mono` redirects to `github.com/earendil-works/pi`. Binary name is `pi` (`bin: {"pi": "dist/bundle/cli.js"}`, `engines.node >=22.19.0`).

**Launch.** Four modes, resolved by `resolveAppMode()` in `src/main.ts`: `--mode rpc` → rpc; `--mode json` → json; `parsed.print || !stdinIsTTY || !stdoutIsTTY` → print; else interactive. **TUI:** `pi [messages...]`, optional `--tui-mode regular|fullscreen` (`fullscreen` experimental). **Headless:** `pi -p "prompt"` / `pi --print "prompt"` (text: final assistant text only) or `pi --mode json "prompt"` (JSONL event stream). Note there is no `-p` needed when piping — pi silently drops to print mode whenever stdin or stdout is not a TTY. **Stdin:** piped stdin is read in every non-RPC mode (`readPipedStdin()` returns `undefined` when `process.stdin.isTTY`) and is prepended into the initial prompt (`cat README.md | pi -p "Summarize this text"`); RPC mode skips this because stdin carries the protocol. **SDK/RPC:** `pi --mode rpc` speaks LF-delimited JSONL commands on stdin / responses+events on stdout; the SDK is the same npm package — `import { createAgentSession, ModelRuntime, SessionManager } from "@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent"`, then `await session.prompt(text)`, `session.subscribe(listener)`, plus `createAgentSessionRuntime()`/`AgentSessionRuntime` for multi-session hosting and exported `InteractiveMode`, `runPrintMode`, `runRpcMode`. No ACP (unknown/absent, checked all of `packages/coding-agent/docs/` — no `acp`/`Agent Client Protocol` match).

**Input and follow-up.** Positional args are prompts: `pi -p "first" "second"` runs them as *sequential prompts in one process* (`runPrintMode` loops `for (const message of messages) await session.prompt(message)`). `@file` prefixes inline file/image content (`pi -p @screenshot.png "What's in this image?"`); `--` stops flag parsing. Interactive queueing: **Enter** = *steering* message (delivered after the current assistant turn's tool calls, before the next LLM call), **Alt+Enter** = *follow-up* (delivered only when the agent stops), Escape aborts and restores the queue, Alt+Up pulls queued text back. RPC equivalents: `{"type":"prompt","message":...,"streamingBehavior":"steer"|"followUp"}` (omitting `streamingBehavior` while streaming is an error), plus dedicated `steer`, `follow_up`, `abort`, `clear_queue` (returns `{steering:[],followUp:[]}` so a client can restore editor text). Delivery policy: `steeringMode` / `followUpMode` = `"one-at-a-time"` | `"all"`.

**Output.** Flags: `-p`/`--print`, `--mode json`, `--mode rpc`, `--export <in> [out]` (HTML). JSON mode emits `JsonAgentSessionEvent` JSONL. Line 1 is the session header: `{"type":"session","version":3,"id":"uuid","timestamp":"...","cwd":"/path"}`. Then: `agent_start`, `turn_start`, `message_start{message}`, `message_update{usage, assistantMessageEvent}`, `message_end{message}`, `turn_end{message,toolResults}`, `agent_end{messages}`, `agent_settled`, `tool_execution_start{toolCallId,toolName,args}`, `tool_execution_update{...,partialResult}`, `tool_execution_end{toolCallId,toolName,result,isError}`, `queue_update`, `compaction_start`/`compaction_end`, `auto_retry_start`/`auto_retry_end`, `summarization_retry_scheduled`/`_attempt_start`/`_finished`, `bash_execution_update` (RPC only), `extension_error{extensionPath,event,error}`. **`message_update` is delta-only** — it omits the cumulative `message` field and `assistantMessageEvent.partial`; you assemble text from `contentIndex` + `delta`, and `toolcall_start` carries extra `id` and `toolName`. **Final answer:** `message_end` holds the authoritative message; in `-p` text mode only the last assistant message's `text` blocks are written to stdout; over RPC use `{"type":"get_last_assistant_text"}` → `data.text` (or `null`). `agent_end` may be followed by retries — wait for **`agent_settled`** for true completion (RPC `agent_end` carries `willRetry`).

**Sessions.** `-c`/`--continue`, `-r`/`--resume`, `--session <path|id>` (accepts partial UUID), `--fork <path|id>`, `--session-id <id>` (undocumented in the README CLI table but parsed in `src/cli/args.ts`; supplies a custom session ID instead of a generated UUID), `--session-dir <dir>`, `--no-session` (ephemeral), `--name`/`-n <name>`. Path: `~/.pi/agent/sessions/--<cwd-with-/-\-:-replaced-by-->--/<timestamp>_<session-id>.jsonl`. Format is a **JSONL tree**: every entry after the header has `id` (usually 8-char hex, may fall back to a full UUID), `parentId` (`null` at a root) and ISO `timestamp`. Entry types: `session` (header, `version` 1|2|3, current 3, optional `parentSession`), `message` (wraps an `AgentMessage`: `user`|`assistant`|`toolResult`|`bashExecution`|`custom`|`branchSummary`|`compactionSummary`), `model_change` (`provider`,`modelId`), `thinking_level_change` (`thinkingLevel`), `compaction` (`summary`,`firstKeptEntryId`,`tokensBefore`, optional `usage`/`details`/`fromHook`), `branch_summary` (`summary`,`fromId`), `custom` (`customType`,`data` — extension state, NOT in LLM context), `custom_message` (`customType`,`content`,`display` — IS in LLM context), `label` (`targetId`,`label`), `session_info` (`name`). Branching: `/tree` (in place, same file), `/fork` (new file from an earlier user message), `/clone` (new file from the active branch); RPC `fork`, `clone`, `switch_session`, `get_tree`, `get_entries`, `get_fork_messages`, `new_session`.

**Hooks.** There is no hook system in the Claude-Code sense — **no shell-command hooks, no hook JSON config, no hook timeouts**. Extensibility is in-process TypeScript **extensions**: `export default function (pi: ExtensionAPI) {...}` (may be `async`; pi awaits the factory before startup continues). 36 documented events: `project_trust`, `resources_discover`, `session_start`, `session_info_changed`, `session_before_switch`, `session_before_fork`, `session_before_compact`, `session_compact`, `session_compact_failed`, `session_before_tree`, `session_tree`, `session_shutdown`, `before_agent_start`, `agent_start`, `agent_end`, `agent_settled`, `ui_prompt_start`, `ui_prompt_end`, `turn_start`, `turn_end`, `message_start`, `message_update`, `message_end`, `tool_execution_start`, `tool_execution_update`, `tool_execution_end`, `context`, `before_provider_headers`, `before_provider_request`, `after_provider_response`, `model_select`, `thinking_level_select`, `tool_call`, `tool_result`, `user_bash`, `input`. **Block:** `pi.on("tool_call", …)` returns `{ block: true, reason?: string, terminate?: boolean }`; `event.input` is mutable in place (mutations affect the real execution, later handlers see earlier mutations, **no re-validation after mutation**); `terminate` only applies to a blocked call and stops the agent early only when *every* finalized result in the batch terminates. An extension error inside `tool_call` also blocks the tool (fail-safe); errors in other handlers are logged and the agent continues. **Allow/modify:** `tool_result` handlers chain middleware-style and return partial patches (`content`, `details`, `isError`, `usage`). **Inject context:** `before_agent_start` returns `{ message: {customType, content, display}, systemPrompt }` (system prompt chains across handlers; `event.systemPromptOptions` exposes `customPrompt`, `selectedTools`, `toolSnippets`, `promptGuidelines`, `appendSystemPrompt`, `cwd`, `contextFiles`, `skills`); the `context` event can rewrite the message array; `pi.sendMessage()` / `pi.sendUserMessage()` / `pi.appendEntry()` inject at any time. **Add tools:** `pi.registerTool({name,label,description,promptSnippet,promptGuidelines,parameters,prepareArguments,execute,renderCall,renderResult})` — works at load time *and* dynamically at runtime without `/reload`; registering a built-in name overrides it. **Config locations:** `~/.pi/agent/extensions/*.ts` or `*/index.ts` (global), `.pi/extensions/*.ts` or `*/index.ts` (project, loaded only after trust), plus `extensions` and `packages` arrays in `settings.json`, plus CLI `-e`/`--extension <path|npm|git>` (repeatable). Disable with `--no-extensions`/`-ne`. **Timeouts:** none for handlers (they are awaited); only `ctx.ui` dialogs take a `timeout` option (`pi.exec` also takes `timeout`).

**MCP.** **Not supported natively — deliberately.** README: "**No MCP.** Build CLI tools with READMEs, or build an extension that adds MCP support." No MCP transport, no tool filtering, no config file. Available only through third-party community pi packages (e.g. `pi-mcp-extension` on npm, `nicobailon/pi-mcp-adapter`, `tickernelz/pi-mcp-tools`) — unofficial, unversioned against pi core. **pi does not act as an MCP server** (unknown/absent, checked the full docs tree and README).

**Extensibility.** Five layers, all shareable as **pi packages** (npm or git, `pi install npm:@foo/pi-tools[@ver]` / `git:github.com/user/repo[@ref]` / `https://` / `ssh://`, `-l` for project-local; `pi remove`, `pi list`, `pi update [--all|--extensions|--models|--self]`, `pi config`; packages land in `~/.pi/agent/git/` and `~/.pi/agent/npm/`, or `.pi/git/` and `.pi/npm/` locally; declared via a `pi` key in `package.json` with `extensions`/`skills`/`prompts`/`themes`, else auto-discovered from those directory names). **Extensions** (above). **Skills** — Agent Skills standard (agentskills.io), `SKILL.md` with `name` + `description` frontmatter, invoked as `/skill:name [args]` (args appended as `User: <args>`); loaded from `~/.pi/agent/skills/`, `~/.agents/skills/`, `.pi/skills/`, `.agents/skills/` (cwd up to git root), packages, the `skills` settings array, and `--skill <path>` (repeatable, additive even with `--no-skills`). Cross-harness reuse is explicit: put `"~/.claude/skills"` or `"~/.codex/skills"` in the `skills` setting. **Prompt templates** — Markdown in `~/.pi/agent/prompts/` or `.pi/prompts/`, expanded by typing `/name`, `{{var}}` placeholders; `--prompt-template <path>`, `--no-prompt-templates`/`-np`. **Themes** — `~/.pi/agent/themes/`, `.pi/themes/`, `--theme`, `--use-theme <name>`, `--no-themes`; hot-reload. **Instruction files:** `AGENTS.md` or `CLAUDE.md`, loaded from `~/.pi/agent/AGENTS.md`, every parent directory walking up from cwd, and cwd — all concatenated. `AGENTS.override.md` in a directory replaces `AGENTS.md`/`CLAUDE.md` **for that directory only** (other directories still concatenate). Context files load regardless of project trust; disable with `--no-context-files`/`-nc`. System prompt precedence: `--system-prompt <text>` / `.pi/SYSTEM.md` (project) / `~/.pi/agent/SYSTEM.md` (global) replace the default; `--append-system-prompt` / `APPEND_SYSTEM.md` append. Context files and skills are still appended after a replaced prompt.

**Permissions.** **No permission system and no sandbox at all** — by design. README: "No permission popups. Run in a container, or build your own confirmation flow with extensions." docs/security.md: "Pi does not include a built-in sandbox… Real isolation needs to come from the operating system or a virtualization/container boundary." There are no auto-approve flags for tools; the only gating is **project trust**, which controls *loading of project-local config*, not tool execution: pi prompts on interactive startup when it finds `.pi/settings.json`, `.pi/extensions|skills|prompts|themes`, `.pi/SYSTEM.md`/`APPEND_SYSTEM.md`, or project `.agents/skills`. Decisions are stored per canonical directory in `~/.pi/agent/trust.json`; `/trust` saves one (restart required). **Non-interactive modes (`-p`, `--mode json`, `--mode rpc`) never prompt** — they fall back to `defaultProjectTrust` (`"ask"` default and `"never"` both ignore project resources; `"always"` trusts). Per-run overrides: `-a`/`--approve`, `-na`/`--no-approve`. Tool surface control is the real lever: `-t`/`--tools <list>` allowlist, `-xt`/`--exclude-tools <list>`, `-nbt`/`--no-builtin-tools`, `-nt`/`--no-tools`. Built-ins: `read`, `bash`, `powershell` (Windows), `edit`, `write`, `grep`, `find`, `ls`. Sandboxing is documented as external only (docs/containerization.md: Gondolin micro-VM extension, plain Docker, OpenShell).

**Environment.** `PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR` overrides the config dir (default `~/.pi/agent`) — **this is the per-run home isolation var**; `PI_CODING_AGENT_SESSION_DIR` overrides session storage (itself overridden by `--session-dir`); `PI_PACKAGE_DIR` overrides the package dir. `PI_OFFLINE=1` / `--offline` kills all startup network ops (version check, package update check, install telemetry); `PI_SKIP_VERSION_CHECK=1` kills only the `pi.dev/api/latest-version` fetch; `PI_TELEMETRY=0` disables the `pi.dev/api/report-install` ping and provider attribution headers (equivalently `enableInstallTelemetry:false`). `PI_CACHE_RETENTION=long`, `PI_SHARE_VIEWER_URL`, `PI_HARDWARE_CURSOR`, `PI_HYPERLINKS`, `PI_IMAGE_PROTOCOL`, `PI_TRUE_COLOR`, `PI_TUI_ESC_TIMEOUT` (ms; default 100 over SSH, 10 otherwise), `HTTP_PROXY`/`HTTPS_PROXY`, `VISUAL`/`EDITOR`. Markers pi *sets* on every child process from the CLI and RPC entry points: `AI_AGENT=pi`, `PI_CODING_AGENT=true` (not set under the SDK). The LLM-callable `bash`/`powershell` tools additionally receive `PI_SESSION_ID`, `PI_SESSION_FILE` (unset when ephemeral), `PI_PROVIDER`, `PI_MODEL`, `PI_REASONING_LEVEL`, resolved per command — but **not** user `!`/`!!` commands. Settings precedence: `.pi/settings.json` (project, only when trusted) overrides `~/.pi/agent/settings.json` (global); CLI flags override both, and `--no-*` + explicit flags bypass settings discovery entirely (`--no-extensions -e ./my-ext.ts`). Model flags: `--model <pattern>` (accepts `provider/id` and a `:<thinking>` suffix, e.g. `--model sonnet:high`), `--provider <name>`, `--api-key <key>`, `--thinking off|minimal|low|medium|high|xhigh|max`, `--models <patterns>` (Ctrl+P cycling), `--list-models [search]`. Custom providers via `~/.pi/agent/models.json`.

**Observability.** No log file and no `PI_LOG`-style debug var (unknown, checked `packages/coding-agent/docs/environment-variables.md` and the whole docs tree — no logging section exists). Usage/cost live in the `Usage` object on every assistant and tool-result message: `{input, output, cacheRead, cacheWrite, cacheWrite1h?, reasoning?, totalTokens, cost:{input,output,cacheRead,cacheWrite,total}}`. RPC `get_session_stats` returns `sessionFile`, `sessionId`, `userMessages`, `assistantMessages`, `toolCalls`, `toolResults`, `totalMessages`, `tokens{input,output,cacheRead,cacheWrite,total}`, `cost` (number), and `contextUsage{tokens,contextWindow,percent}` (omitted when no model/context window; `tokens`/`percent` are `null` right after compaction). Tools that make nested LLM calls report their own `usage`, which rolls into session totals. Interactive footer shows `↑`/`↓`/`R`/`W`/`CH` plus cost and context %. **Exit codes** (`src/modes/print-mode.ts`): `0` success; `1` when the last assistant message has `stopReason === "error"` or `"aborted"` (message written to stderr) or when an exception escapes; `129` on SIGHUP; `143` on SIGTERM. **The `stopReason`→`1` mapping is guarded by `if (mode === "text")` — `--mode json` returns `0` even when the turn ended in `stopReason:"error"`.**

**Gotchas.**
- `pi` with no `-p` still runs headless whenever stdin *or* stdout is not a TTY (`resolveAppMode`), so a naive `spawn("pi")` never gets the TUI and silently produces one-shot text.
- `--mode json` swallows failures in the exit code: only text print mode maps `stopReason:"error"|"aborted"` to `1`. Parse `message_end`/`agent_end` for `stopReason` instead.
- RPC framing is **LF-only**. The docs explicitly call out that Node `readline` is non-compliant because it also splits on `U+2028`/`U+2029`, which are legal inside JSON strings. Split on `\n`, strip a trailing `\r`.
- `message_update` has no cumulative `message` and no `assistantMessageEvent.partial` — reconstruct text from `contentIndex` + `delta` or wait for `message_end`.
- `agent_end` is not the end. Auto-retry, auto-compaction-then-retry, and queued follow-ups can continue afterwards (`agent_end.willRetry` in RPC). `agent_settled` is the completion signal.
- Non-interactive modes never show the trust prompt, so project `.pi/settings.json`, project extensions and project `.agents/skills` are **silently ignored** unless `defaultProjectTrust:"always"` or `-a`/`--approve` is passed. A driver that works interactively can behave differently headless.
- Nothing restricts tool execution. `bash` runs with full user permissions from the first turn; the only mitigations are `--tools`/`--exclude-tools`/`--no-tools` and OS-level containment.
- Sending a `prompt` over RPC while streaming without `streamingBehavior` is an error response, not an implicit queue.
- Extension commands (`/mycommand`) bypass the streaming guard and execute immediately even mid-stream; `steer`/`follow_up` reject them (use `prompt`).
- `pi -p "a" "b"` is multi-turn in a *single* process; each positional arg is a separate `session.prompt()` call, not concatenated text.
- `tool_call` mutations to `event.input` are applied with **no re-validation** against the tool schema.
- The npm scope moved: `@mariozechner/pi-coding-agent` is frozen/deprecated at 0.73.1. Pin `@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent`.
- `--session-id` exists in `src/cli/args.ts` but is absent from the README CLI reference; it is only mentioned in docs/session-format.md.
- Sibling tool calls run concurrently by default; `tool_call` is preflighted sequentially but is not guaranteed to see sibling results from the same assistant message in `ctx.sessionManager`.

**Driver notes.**
- **Window mode (PTY):** must allocate a real PTY on *both* stdin and stdout, otherwise `resolveAppMode` silently downgrades to print. Launch `pi --tui-mode regular` (avoid experimental `fullscreen`), set `PI_HARDWARE_CURSOR`/`PI_TRUE_COLOR`/`PI_IMAGE_PROTOCOL` explicitly rather than relying on terminal probing, and bump `PI_TUI_ESC_TIMEOUT` if injecting Alt-key sequences. Interactive is the only mode where the trust prompt appears, so a first run in a new repo blocks on it.
- **Session mode (one resumed turn per process):** `pi --session-id <bee-id> --mode json -a --tools ... "<turn>"` — supplying your own session ID up front removes the parse-the-path dance, and `--session <id>` / `-c` resume it on the next process. Always pass `-a`/`--approve` (or set `defaultProjectTrust:"always"`) or project config is dropped. Do not trust the exit code in JSON mode; determine success from the last `message_end`/`agent_end` `stopReason`.
- **RPC mode is the strongest surface for a long-lived driver:** one process, `prompt`/`steer`/`follow_up`/`abort`/`clear_queue`, plus first-class introspection (`get_state`, `get_session_stats`, `get_messages`, `get_tree`, `get_entries`, `get_last_assistant_text`) and session control (`new_session`, `switch_session`, `fork`, `clone`, `set_session_name`) that no other mode exposes. Correlate with the optional `id` field on every command. Implement the framing by hand (LF split), never with `readline`.
- **Batch mode:** `pi -p "a" "b" "c"` gives sequential turns in one process with a trustworthy exit code (`1` on error/abort, `143`/`129` on signals) and only the final assistant text on stdout — the cheapest reliable non-interactive shape. Pair with `--no-session` for throwaway runs or `--session-dir` to keep transcripts under Bee's own tree.
- **Approvals and blocking are Bee's job.** Since pi has no permission model, a Bee driver that needs gating must ship a pi extension registered via `-e ./bee-gate.ts` that handles `tool_call` and returns `{block:true,reason}`. In RPC mode that extension can even prompt Bee through the `extension_ui_request`/`extension_ui_response` sub-protocol (`select`, `confirm`, `input`, `editor` block for a response; `notify`, `setStatus`, `setWidget`, `setTitle`, `set_editor_text` are fire-and-forget) — this is pi's only interactive-approval channel outside the TUI. Note `ctx.mode === "rpc"` has `hasUI === true` but `ctx.ui.custom()` returns `undefined`.
- **Isolation:** set `PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR` per Bee action to give each run its own `~/.pi/agent` equivalent (settings, sessions, trust.json, packages), plus `PI_OFFLINE=1` to stop startup network calls, and `--no-extensions`/`--no-skills`/`--no-prompt-templates`/`--no-context-files` to pin exactly what loads. There is no sandbox — containerize if the workload is untrusted.

**Sources.**
- https://github.com/badlogic/pi-mono (redirects to https://github.com/earendil-works/pi) — root README
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/badlogic/pi-mono/main/packages/coding-agent/README.md — full CLI reference, modes, env vars
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/badlogic/pi-mono/main/packages/coding-agent/docs/rpc.md — RPC protocol, commands, events, extension UI sub-protocol
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/badlogic/pi-mono/main/packages/coding-agent/docs/extensions.md — 36 events, blocking, custom tool API
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/badlogic/pi-mono/main/packages/coding-agent/docs/session-format.md and .../docs/json.md — JSONL entry types, event stream shape
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/badlogic/pi-mono/main/packages/coding-agent/src/modes/print-mode.ts and .../src/main.ts — exit codes, mode resolution

## Comparison matrix

| Harness | Headless flag | Stream JSON | Multi-turn headless | Hooks (count, type) | Block mechanism | MCP client | MCP server | ACP | Sandbox | Session isolation env var | Exit code trustworthy |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Claude Code | `-p/--print` | `--output-format stream-json` (needs `--verbose`) | yes, one process (`--input-format stream-json`) or `-r`/`-c` per process | 33, `command`/`http`/`mcp_tool`/`prompt`/`agent` | exit 2 + stderr; JSON `permissionDecision:"deny"` | yes (stdio, HTTP, SSE, ws; OAuth) | yes (`claude mcp serve`) | no | yes (Seatbelt / bubblewrap+socat; not Win/WSL1) | `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` | partly — in-run errors print as the result; check `is_error`; SIGTERM=143 |
| Codex CLI | `codex exec` | `--json` (JSONL) | per process (`codex exec resume/fork`); app-server for one process | 12, `command`/`mcp_tool` | exit 2 + stderr; `{"decision":"block"}` | yes (stdio, streamable HTTP; OAuth) | yes, deprecated (`codex mcp-server`) | no | yes (Seatbelt / Landlock+seccomp / Windows) | `CODEX_HOME` (+ `CODEX_SQLITE_HOME`) | yes as pass/fail; no per-code table |
| Gemini CLI | `-p/--prompt` | `-o stream-json` (flag only) | per process (`--resume`/`--session-id`/`--session-file`); ACP for one process | 11, `command` only | exit 2 + stderr; `decision:"deny"` | yes (stdio, SSE `url`, HTTP `httpUrl`; no OAuth) | no | yes (`--acp`) | yes (docker/podman/sandbox-exec/runsc/lxc/Seatbelt) | `GEMINI_CLI_HOME` | yes — `0/1/42/53` documented |
| Cursor CLI | `-p` + `--force` | `--output-format stream-json` | Per-process `--resume <chatId>` | 21, command + prompt (LLM) | exit 2 or `permission:"deny"`; fail-open unless `failClosed` | yes (stdio/SSE/HTTP) | no | yes (`agent acp`, hidden) | yes (`--sandbox`, `agent sandbox`) | `CURSOR_CONFIG_DIR` | partly — only 0 documented |
| GitHub Copilot CLI | `-p` / `--prompt=` | `--output-format=json` (JSONL, schema unpublished) | Per-process `--resume=<id>` / `--continue` | 14, command + http + prompt | `permissionDecision:"deny"` / `behavior:"deny"`; exit 2 denies preToolUse; timeout always fail-open | yes (stdio + remote HTTP) | no | yes (`--acp`, stdio or TCP) | yes (MXC: Seatbelt/Bubblewrap/ProcessContainer) | `COPILOT_HOME` (+`COPILOT_CACHE_HOME`) | no table documented |
| Amp | `-x` / `--execute` | `--stream-json` (Claude Code-compatible) | **In-process** `--stream-json-input`, or `threads continue` | 5, in-process TS/Bun plugin only | `{action:'reject-and-continue'}` / `cancel()` | yes (stdio/HTTP/SSE) | no | no | none (use `--executor orb`) | `AMP_SETTINGS_FILE` | no — use `result.is_error` |
| OpenCode | `run` | `--format json` (NDJSON) | Per-process `-c`/`-s`, or HTTP server | 21 plugin hooks, in-process JS/TS | `throw` in `tool.execute.before`; `permission.ask` → `deny` | yes (stdio/remote HTTP) | no | yes (`opencode acp`) | none | `OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR` | yes (0 / 1) |
| Goose | `run` (headless default) | `--output-format stream-json` | Per-process `-n <name> -r` | 11, command only | exit 2 or `{"decision":"block"}`; blocking on PreToolUse/Stop | yes (stdio/streamable_http) | no (`goose mcp <name>` proxies a configured one) | yes (`goose acp`, `goose serve`) | Docker via `--container` | `GOOSE_PATH_ROOT` | **no** — agent errors exit 0 |
| Cline CLI | none (detected) / `--json` | `--json` (NDJSON) | **broken** (`--id`+`--json` fails) | 9 file hooks (+SDK plugin stages), command | `cancel: true` on stdout; fail-open, no fail_closed | yes (stdio/sse/streamableHttp) | no | yes (`--acp`) | state isolation only (`--data-dir`), no OS sandbox | `CLINE_DIR` / `CLINE_DATA_DIR` | partly — aborted runs exit 0 |
| Grok CLI | `-p` / `--single` | `streaming-json`, `streaming-messages-json` | Per-process `--resume <UUID>` | 15, command + http (https-only) | exit 2 or `permissionDecision:"deny"`; else fail-open | yes (stdio/HTTP) | no | yes (`grok agent stdio`, serve, relay) | yes (Landlock/Seatbelt, 5 profiles) | `GROK_HOME` | yes (0/1/130/143) |
| Antigravity `agy` | `-p` / `--print` | `--output-format stream-json` | **In-process** `--input-format stream-json`, or `-c`/`--conversation` | 5, command only | `decision:"deny"` (PreToolUse) | yes (stdio/HTTP/SSE/ws) | no | **no** (FR #31) | yes (namespaces/sandbox-exec/AppContainer) | none documented (`HOME` implied) | **no** — soft-denied runs exit 0 |
