| pi | `-p` / `--print` (also auto when stdin or stdout is not a TTY); `--mode json`; `--mode rpc` | `--mode json` (JSONL, delta-only `message_update`); RPC events on stdout | Yes — repeated positional prompts in one process (`pi -p "a" "b"`), `-c` / `--session <id>` / `--session-id` resume, or a persistent `--mode rpc` process | 36 in-process TypeScript extension events (no shell hooks, no timeouts) | `pi.on("tool_call")` returns `{block:true,reason,terminate}`; mutable `event.input`; handler throw also blocks | No (deliberate — third-party extensions only, e.g. `pi-mcp-extension`) | No | No | No (docs/security.md: use container/VM; Gondolin, Docker, OpenShell) | `PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR` (plus `PI_CODING_AGENT_SESSION_DIR`) | Partly — `0`/`1`/`129`/`143` in `-p` text mode only; `--mode json` returns `0` even on `stopReason:"error"` |

## What this means for a Bee driver

### Three surfaces, and which to build on

Every harness offers some of three integration surfaces. The inventory shows
where each is stable enough to build on.

| Surface | Stable enough today | Fragile or missing |
|---|---|---|
| One headless process per turn, JSONL out, resume by id | Claude Code, Codex (`exec --json`, `exec resume`), Gemini, Cursor, Copilot, Amp, Goose, Grok, Agy, Kiro | Cline (resume with `--json` is broken) |
| A protocol server the driver speaks to over stdio or a socket | ACP: Gemini, Cursor, Copilot, OpenCode, Goose, Cline, Grok, Kiro. JSON-RPC: Codex app-server. HTTP+SSE: OpenCode `serve`. Stdin stream: Claude `--input-format stream-json`, Amp `--stream-json-input`, Agy `--input-format stream-json` | Agy has no ACP; Copilot's ACP reserves stdout |
| An in-process plugin or SDK | Claude Agent SDK (TS, Python), OpenCode plugins and JS SDK, Amp Bun plugins, Cline SDK stages | none |

Recommendation: session mode stays what the legacy carriers proved, one
process per turn resumed by id, because it works for eleven of thirteen and
never parks an actor on a stdin stream. ACP is the right second implementation
for the eight that speak it, one carrier for all of them. Window mode is a PTY
for everyone, with the readiness rules below.

### Where the final answer is

| Harness | Answer location in the stream |
|---|---|
| Claude Code | `result` message, `result.result`; check `is_error` |
| Codex | last `item.completed` with `item.type == "agent_message"`; progress goes to stderr |
| Gemini | `json` mode: `response`; `stream-json`: accumulate assistant `message` deltas, the `result` event carries none |
| Cursor, Amp | Claude-shaped `result` message |
| Copilot | unpublished; read `~/.copilot/session-state/<id>/events.jsonl` |
| OpenCode | `text` events until the session goes `idle`; no terminal envelope |
| Goose | `complete` event with usage |
| Cline | `run_result.text` |
| Grok | concatenate `text` events until `end` |
| Agy | `result.response` |
| Kiro | unpublished; capture a run or use ACP |

### Hooks come in three families

- **JSON on stdin, exit 2 blocks**: Claude Code (33 events, five handler types), Codex (12, gated by content hash unless `--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust`), Gemini (11, timeouts in milliseconds, stdout must be pure JSON), Cursor (21, fail-open unless `failClosed`), Copilot (14, timeout always fail-open), Goose (11), Grok (15, HTTP hooks https-only), Kiro (11, two trigger vocabularies).
- **In-process plugin**: Amp (five lifecycle events returning `allow`, `reject-and-continue` or `modify`), OpenCode (21 plugin functions, no timeouts, no matchers), Cline (file-name hooks with no matchers, `--yolo` disables them).
- **Nearly none**: Agy (five command hooks, camelCase, a required `decision` field with five values).

Payload casing is `snake_case` for Claude, Codex, Gemini, Copilot and Kiro,
`camelCase` for Agy, and both at once for Grok. The legacy `kit.hook_kind`
normalizer already folds these; it needs Agy's `decision` values and Kiro's
second trigger vocabulary added.

Two rules follow. The gateway keeps both handler shapes, an HTTP endpoint for
the three that can post directly and a `curl` command handler for the rest.
And hooks are observation, never enforcement: most fail open on error or
timeout, so what an agent may do is decided by Bee's MCP tool policy and the
sandbox, not by a hook.

### MCP is universal

All twelve consume MCP over stdio, and all twelve accept an HTTP
server, which is what the gateway exposes per action. Only Claude Code can act
as an MCP server itself; Codex's is deprecated. Tool filtering differs enough
that the kit needs one selector per harness: `--allowedTools mcp__bee__*`
(Claude), `--allow "MCPTool(bee__*)"` (Grok), `available_tools` (Goose), glob
filters (OpenCode), `default_tools_approval_mode` (Codex).

Unreachable servers are a launch-time hazard: Agy blocks its prompt loop for
five minutes, Cline silently drops a server that takes over 1.5 s to
initialize, Codex bounds it with `startup_timeout_sec`, Kiro can be told to
fail with `--require-mcp-startup`. The carrier must have the gateway listening
before the child starts, every time.

### Isolation, trust and permissions, per harness

| Harness | Config isolation | Trust prompt pre-answer | Headless auto-approve |
|---|---|---|---|
| Claude Code | `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` | `projects["<root>"].hasTrustDialogAccepted` in `~/.claude.json` | `--permission-mode acceptEdits\|dontAsk\|bypassPermissions`, `--allowedTools`, `--permission-prompts none` |
| Codex | `CODEX_HOME` | `projects."<path>".trust_level="trusted"` in `config.toml` | `--sandbox workspace-write --ask-for-approval never`; default sandbox is read-only |
| Gemini | `GEMINI_CLI_HOME` | `--skip-trust`, `GEMINI_CLI_TRUST_WORKSPACE=true`, `trustedFolders.json` | `--approval-mode yolo`; `ask_user` removed headless |
| Cursor | `CURSOR_CONFIG_DIR` | `--trust` (headless only) | `-p --force --trust --approve-mcps`; without `--force` edits are only proposed |
| Copilot | `COPILOT_HOME` | `permissions-config.json` keyed by git root | `--allow-tool`, `--allow-all-tools`, `--no-ask-user` |
| Amp | `AMP_SETTINGS_FILE` | none | `--dangerously-allow-all`, `amp.permissions`, `amp mcp approve` |
| OpenCode | `OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR` | none | `run --auto`; otherwise every prompt is auto-rejected |
| Goose | `GOOSE_PATH_ROOT` | none | `GOOSE_MODE=auto` is the default |
| Cline | `CLINE_DIR`, `--data-dir` (not MCP config) | none, auto-denies on non-TTY | `--yolo` |
| Grok | `GROK_HOME` | `--trust`, `GROK_FOLDER_TRUST=0`, `trusted_folders.toml` | `--always-approve`, `--permission-mode auto` |
| Agy | `HOME` only | undocumented; interactive sign-in once per profile | `--dangerously-skip-permissions` |
| Kiro | `KIRO_HOME` | `permissions.yaml` allow rule | `--trust-all-tools` |

Sandboxes worth using from a driver: Claude (Seatbelt, bubblewrap), Codex
(Seatbelt, Landlock, Windows), Gemini (containers or Seatbelt), Copilot (MXC),
Grok (five profiles), Cursor (`agent sandbox`). Amp, OpenCode, Cline and
Kiro have none locally; Bee's Docker executor is the sandbox there, as the
legacy `exec.docker` entries already do.

### Process hygiene and exit codes

- Kill process groups. Cline treats SIGTERM as an abort request and keeps
  running; Kiro's TUI is three processes deep; Agy can answer and never exit;
  Grok and Claude leave tool grandchildren orphaned. The native executor needs
  process-group signalling before any of this is reliable.
- Pin versions. Cursor, Copilot and Amp self-update on launch.
- Trust exit codes only from Gemini (0, 1, 42, 53), Grok (0, 1, 130, 143),
  Kiro (0, 1, 3) and OpenCode (0, 1). Goose and Agy exit 0 after agent
  failures; read the result payload instead.
- Amp switches to execute mode whenever stdout is not a TTY. Claude implies
  `-p` the same way. A window carrier must hand the child a real PTY.

### Native agents are a harness kind too

A native Wippy agent needs no process, no PTY, no MCP hop and no hook file. The
driver contract still fits: `prepare` names the agent entry and its model
(including a local model behind a vLLM-style driver on a GPU bee), `command`
becomes a runner call for one turn, and `parse` reads runner events instead of
stdout lines. Hooks are runtime events on the thread. A dataflow swarm is the
batch mode of this kind. Because it runs inside Bee, it is the only harness
that can be published, installed from the Hub and moved between bees as an
app, which is what makes agents-that-make-agents cheap.

### Contract additions the inventory argues for

The legacy binding metadata (`kind`, `title`, `modes`, `default_mode`,
`hooks`) is not enough to drive thirteen harnesses from one carrier. Add
capability flags to the binding: `protocol` (stream-json, acp, app-server,
sdk, native), `hooks` (none, command, http, plugin), `answer_path`,
`resume` (per-process, in-process, none), `isolation_env`, `trust_preanswer`,
`exit_codes_trustworthy`, `input_ready` (how the window carrier knows the TUI
accepts keys), and `interrupt`. The carriers branch on these; the drivers
stay three functions.

## Expected event set on the thread

Every harness event, hook or transcript line is normalized into one of these
observation kinds before it reaches the thread. The source column names where
each kind comes from; a driver's `parse` and the gateway's hook handler both
emit only these.

| Kind | Meaning | Stream sources | Hook sources | Harnesses without it |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `launch` | child started, session id known | `system/init` (Claude, Cursor, Amp), `thread.started` (Codex), `init` (Gemini, Agy), first `step_start` (OpenCode) | `SessionStart`, `sessionStart`, `TaskStart`, `session.start` | `turn.request` | a prompt was submitted, the turn's request id | typed prompt or resumed turn | `UserPromptSubmit`, `BeforeAgent`, `beforeSubmitPrompt`, `userPromptSubmitted`, `PromptSubmit`, `PreInvocation`, `agent.start` | `text` | assistant text, `delta` for chunks | `assistant` content (Claude-shaped), `agent_message` (Codex), `message` deltas (Gemini, Goose), `text` (Grok, OpenCode), `step_update.text_delta` (Agy), `agent_event` (Cline) | `afterAgentResponse` (Cursor) | `tool.call` | a tool is about to run, with input | `tool_use` (Claude-shaped), `item.started` (Codex), `tool_use` (OpenCode), `tool_call` (Grok), `step_update` tool steps (Agy) | `PreToolUse`, `BeforeTool`, `preToolUse`, `tool.call`, `tool.execute.before` | `tool.result` | the tool returned, with status | `user` tool_result (Claude-shaped), `item.completed` (Codex), `tool_call_update` (Grok), `notification` (Goose) | `PostToolUse`, `AfterTool`, `postToolUse`, `tool.result`, `tool.execute.after` | `tool.failure` | the tool errored | error items | `PostToolUseFailure`, `postToolUseFailure` | Codex, Gemini, Kiro, Agy, Cline |
| `permission.request` | the harness asks whether a tool may run | `permission` events (OpenCode), ACP permission requests | `PermissionRequest` (Claude, Codex), `permissionRequest` (Copilot), `permission.ask` (OpenCode) | `model.request` / `model.response` | the full prompt sent to the model and its reply | none | `BeforeModel`, `AfterModel` (Gemini); `afterAgentThought` (Cursor, partial); `chat.params` (OpenCode, request only) | everyone else |
| `file.edit` | a file changed | edit tool results | `afterFileEdit` (Cursor), `AfterFileEdit` (Goose), `FileSave` (Kiro), `FileChanged` (Claude) | inferred from `tool.result` elsewhere |
| `shell.exec` | a shell command ran | `command_execution` (Codex) | `beforeShellExecution` `afterShellExecution` (Cursor, Goose); `Bash` matcher on tool hooks (Claude, Grok) | inferred from `tool.call` elsewhere |
| `subagent.start` / `subagent.stop` | a child agent inside the harness | none | `SubagentStart` `SubagentStop` (Claude, Codex, Cursor, Copilot, Grok), `AgentSpawn` (Kiro), `TaskCreated` `TaskCompleted` (Claude) | Gemini, Goose, Agy, Cline, Amp, OpenCode |
| `compaction` | context was compacted; a gap in the record | none | `PreCompact` `PostCompact` (Claude, Codex, Grok), `PreCompress` (Gemini), `preCompact` (Cursor, Copilot) | Goose, Kiro, Agy, Cline, Amp, OpenCode |
| `ask` | the agent needs a human decision; opens an inbox request | `bee:ask` tool call over MCP | `Elicitation` `ElicitationResult` (Claude) | handled by Bee, not the harness |
| `notification` | harness-level notice, e.g. idle, auth, error | `error` events | `Notification` (Claude, Gemini, Grok), `notification` `errorOccurred` (Copilot) | others |
| `turn.end` | the turn finished, with usage and outcome | `result` (Claude-shaped), `turn.completed` / `turn.failed` (Codex), `complete` (Goose), `end` (Grok), `result` (Agy), `run_result` (Cline), `session.status` idle (OpenCode) | `Stop`, `AfterAgent`, `stop`, `agentStop`, `AgentStop`, `PostInvocation`, `agent.end`, `TaskComplete` | `error` | the harness reported a failure outside a tool | `error`, `turn.failed`, `is_error` on `result` | `StopFailure` (Claude, Grok), `TaskError` (Cline), `errorOccurred` (Copilot) | exit code alone is not enough for Goose, Agy |
| `receipt` | child exited; exit code and whether it is trustworthy | process exit | `SessionEnd`, `sessionEnd`, `SessionShutdown` | none |

Bee adds its own kinds on the same thread, never from the harness: `message`
(request, progress, reply, notification, with `in_reply_to`), `delivered` and
`answered` marks, `approval` state changes (pending, approved, denied, expired,
cancelled), `publish` receipts, and `recap` checkpoints. A timeline is this
table in time order, one row per record, with the source tag showing whether it
came from the stream, a hook or a transcript.

## Common denominator of the main providers

Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI, Copilot CLI and Cursor all ship the same
core, under different names. A driver can rely on every row below without a
per-harness special case; only the spelling column changes.

| Feature present in all five | Claude Code | Codex | Gemini | Copilot | Cursor |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Headless one-shot run | `-p` | `exec` | `-p` | `-p` | `-p --force` |
| Streaming JSON events | `--output-format stream-json --verbose` | `--json` | `-o stream-json` | `--output-format=json` | `--output-format stream-json` |
| Session id issued at start | `system/init.session_id` | `thread.started.thread_id` | `init.session_id` | in JSON / `events.jsonl` | `session_id` |
| Resume across processes | `-r <id>`, `-c` | `exec resume <id>` | `--resume <id>` | `--resume=<id>`, `--continue` | `--resume <chatId>` |
| Fork a session | `--fork-session` | `codex fork` | worktree | `--fork` | `agent create-chat` |
| Instruction file | `CLAUDE.md` | `AGENTS.md` | `GEMINI.md` | `copilot-instructions.md`, `AGENTS.md` | `.cursor/rules`, `AGENTS.md` |
| Hooks: JSON on stdin, exit 2 blocks | yes | yes | yes | yes | yes |
| Hook config is a settings file with `matcher` | `settings.json` | `hooks.json` / `config.toml` | `settings.json` | `hooks/*.json` | `hooks.json` |
| MCP client over stdio and HTTP | yes | yes | yes | yes | yes |
| MCP server config by file and by flag | `--mcp-config`, `claude mcp add` | `-c mcp_servers.*`, `codex mcp add` | `gemini mcp add` | `copilot mcp add` | `agent mcp add` |
| Auto-approve for unattended runs | `--permission-mode`, `--allowedTools` | `--ask-for-approval never` | `--approval-mode yolo` | `--allow-all-tools` | `--force --trust` |
| Workspace trust pre-answer | `~/.claude.json` | `config.toml` | `--skip-trust` | `permissions-config.json` | `--trust` |
| Config isolation env var | `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` | `CODEX_HOME` | `GEMINI_CLI_HOME` | `COPILOT_HOME` | `CURSOR_CONFIG_DIR` |
| Model flag | `--model` | `-m` | `-m` | `--model` | `--model` |
| OS sandbox available | yes | yes | yes | yes | yes |

Hook events present in all five, the set the thread can count on:

| Bee kind | Claude Code | Codex | Gemini | Copilot | Cursor |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `launch` | `SessionStart` | `SessionStart` | `SessionStart` | `sessionStart` | `sessionStart` |
| `turn.request` | `UserPromptSubmit` | `UserPromptSubmit` | `BeforeAgent` | `userPromptSubmitted` | `beforeSubmitPrompt` |
| `tool.call` (blockable) | `PreToolUse` | `PreToolUse` | `BeforeTool` | `preToolUse` | `preToolUse` |
| `tool.result` | `PostToolUse` | `PostToolUse` | `AfterTool` | `postToolUse` | `postToolUse` |
| `turn.end` | `Stop` | `Stop` | `AfterAgent` | `agentStop` | `stop` |
| `compaction` | `PreCompact` | `PreCompact` | `PreCompress` | `preCompact` | `preCompact` |
| `receipt` | `SessionEnd` | `SessionEnd` | `SessionEnd` | `sessionEnd` | `sessionEnd` |

Present in four of five and worth using with a fallback: `PostToolUseFailure`
(all but Gemini), `SubagentStart` and `SubagentStop` (all but Gemini),
`PermissionRequest` (Claude, Codex, Copilot), `Notification` (Claude, Gemini,
Copilot). Context injection at `turn.request` exists in all five but through
different fields, so the kit needs one shim per harness for it.

## Gaps per harness

What Bee needs from a harness and where each one falls short. "Persist" is a
session id plus resume across processes, which is what lets a thread action
outlive one child; without it a session carrier cannot exist.

| Harness | Persist | Observe | Control | Workaround |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Cline | ids exist but `--id` with `--json` is broken | file hooks fail open, no matchers | MCP calls capped at 5 s, initialize at 1.5 s; `--data-dir` does not isolate MCP config | window mode plus hooks, or `cline --acp`; no session carrier until resume is fixed |
| Amp | threads live on ampcode.com only; nothing local to back up or move | no hook file; plugins are in-process TS | MCP requests time out at 5 s; no local sandbox | steer over `--stream-json-input`; persistence depends on their cloud, so treat as non-portable |
| Agy | conversation ids work, but isolation is `HOME` only and auth is interactive per profile | five hooks, camelCase, two entry shapes | no ACP; unreachable MCP blocks the prompt loop five minutes; exit 0 on soft denials; may never exit | pre-sign-in per action home, gateway up before launch, kill process group, read `result.status` |
| Kiro | fine (`--resume-id`) | `stream-json` schema unpublished; two hook vocabularies | no local sandbox | prefer `kiro-cli acp`; capture a run to pin the schema; `--require-mcp-startup` |
| Copilot | fine, but bare `--resume` needs a TTY | JSON schema unpublished | preToolUse always fails open on timeout | read `session-state/<id>/events.jsonl`; never rely on the hook to block |
| Cursor | fine | transcript format undocumented; no usage fields | `--trust` headless only; without `--force` edits are proposals | use hook `transcript_path`; always pass `-p --force --trust --approve-mcps` |
| Gemini | fine | `stream-json` `result` has no answer; accumulate deltas | no MCP server; alias with `_` breaks policy parsing | name the server `bee`, not `bee_gw`; treat hook stdout strictly as JSON |
| Codex | fine, best transcripts | hooks silently inert until content-hash review | default sandbox is read-only | `--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust` or pre-review; `--sandbox workspace-write` |
| Claude Code | fine; transcript format unstable | 33 events, best coverage | MCP calls over ~120 s are backgrounded | `mcp_tool` hooks for observation; 60 s wait slices |
| Goose | fine (`-n -r`) | no subagent events | exits 0 on agent errors; no trust prompt | read `complete`; `GOOSE_MODE` explicit |
| OpenCode | fine (`-s`) | no terminal envelope; watch for `idle` | `run` auto-rejects prompts unless `--auto` | use `serve` plus the plugin `event` hook |
| Grok | fine | https-only hooks | pending MCP call blocks SIGTERM; leaks worktrees | command hooks that curl the gateway; gateway fails waits on drain |

| pi | fine (JSONL session trees, `fork`, `switch_session`) | 36 in-process TS events, no shell hooks | no permission model, no sandbox, no MCP, no ACP; JSON mode exits 0 on error | `--mode rpc` for everything; a pi extension is the gate; gateway tools reach it only through an extension bridge |

Bee-side gaps the inventory makes unavoidable: the native executor needs
process-group signalling and an `inherit_env` option; the gateway needs both
HTTP and command hook handlers plus `mcp_tool` receivers; `thread_wait` must
slice at 60 s; window carriers need a per-harness `input_ready` signal; and
per-action home isolation needs a fallback to a private `HOME` for the two
harnesses that isolate nothing else.
